<html><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html; charset=utf-8"/><title>Product ai</title><style>
/* cspell:disable-file */
/* webkit printing magic: print all background colors */
html {
	-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
}
* {
	box-sizing: border-box;
	-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
}

html,
body {
	margin: 0;
	padding: 0;
}
@media only screen {
	body {
		margin: 2em auto;
		max-width: 900px;
		color: rgb(55, 53, 47);
	}
}

body {
	line-height: 1.5;
	white-space: pre-wrap;
}

a,
a.visited {
	color: inherit;
	text-decoration: underline;
}

.pdf-relative-link-path {
	font-size: 80%;
	color: #444;
}

h1,
h2,
h3 {
	letter-spacing: -0.01em;
	line-height: 1.2;
	font-weight: 600;
	margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* Override strong tags inside headings to maintain consistent weight */
h1 strong,
h2 strong,
h3 strong {
	font-weight: 600;
}

.page-title {
	font-size: 2.5rem;
	font-weight: 700;
	margin-top: 0;
	margin-bottom: 0.75em;
}

h1 {
	font-size: 1.875rem;
	margin-top: 1.875rem;
}

h2 {
	font-size: 1.5rem;
	margin-top: 1.5rem;
}

h3 {
	font-size: 1.25rem;
	margin-top: 1.25rem;
}

.source {
	border: 1px solid #ddd;
	border-radius: 3px;
	padding: 1.5em;
	word-break: break-all;
}

.callout {
	border-radius: 10px;
	padding: 1rem;
}

figure {
	margin: 1.25em 0;
	page-break-inside: avoid;
}

figcaption {
	opacity: 0.5;
	font-size: 85%;
	margin-top: 0.5em;
}

mark {
	background-color: transparent;
}

.indented {
	padding-left: 1.5em;
}

hr {
	background: transparent;
	display: block;
	width: 100%;
	height: 1px;
	visibility: visible;
	border: none;
	border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.09);
}

img {
	max-width: 100%;
}

@media only print {
	img {
		max-height: 100vh;
		object-fit: contain;
	}

	table.collection-content {
		width: 100%;
		table-layout: fixed;
	}

	table.collection-content th,
	table.collection-content td {
		overflow-wrap: anywhere;
	}

	table.collection-content td > .user,
	table.collection-content td > time {
		white-space: pre-wrap;
	}
}

@page {
	margin: 1in;
}

.collection-content-wrapper {
	overflow-x: auto;
}

@media only print {
	.collection-content-wrapper {
		overflow-x: visible;
	}
}

.collection-content {
	font-size: 0.875rem;
}

.collection-content td {
	white-space: pre-wrap;
	word-break: break-word;
}

.column-list {
	display: flex;
	gap: 46px;
}

.column {
	min-width: 0;
	overflow: hidden;
}

.column > *:first-child {
	margin-top: 0;
}

.table_of_contents-item {
	display: block;
	font-size: 0.875rem;
	line-height: 1.3;
	padding: 0.125rem;
}

.table_of_contents-indent-1 {
	margin-left: 1.5rem;
}

.table_of_contents-indent-2 {
	margin-left: 3rem;
}

.table_of_contents-indent-3 {
	margin-left: 4.5rem;
}

.table_of_contents-link {
	text-decoration: none;
	opacity: 0.7;
	border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.18);
}

table,
th,
td {
	border: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.09);
	border-collapse: collapse;
}

table {
	border-left: none;
	border-right: none;
}

th,
td {
	font-weight: normal;
	padding: 0.25em 0.5em;
	line-height: 1.5;
	min-height: 1.5em;
	text-align: left;
}

th {
	color: rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.6);
}

ol,
ul {
	margin: 0;
	margin-block-start: 0.6em;
	margin-block-end: 0.6em;
}

li > ol:first-child,
li > ul:first-child {
	margin-block-start: 0.6em;
}

ul > li {
	list-style: disc;
}

ul.to-do-list {
	padding-inline-start: 0;
}

ul.to-do-list > li {
	list-style: none;
}

.to-do-children-checked {
	text-decoration: line-through;
	opacity: 0.375;
}

ul.toggle > li {
	list-style: none;
}

ul {
	padding-inline-start: 1.7em;
}

ul > li {
	padding-left: 0.1em;
}

ol {
	padding-inline-start: 1.6em;
}

ol.numbered-list.numbered-list-digits-2 {
	padding-inline-start: 2em;
}

ol.numbered-list.numbered-list-digits-3plus {
	padding-inline-start: 2.4em;
}

ol > li {
	padding-left: 0.2em;
}

.mono ol {
	padding-inline-start: 2em;
}

.mono ol > li {
	text-indent: -0.4em;
}

.toggle {
	padding-inline-start: 0em;
	list-style-type: none;
}

/* Indent toggle children */
.toggle > li > details {
	padding-left: 1.7em;
}

.toggle > li > details > summary {
	margin-left: -1.1em;
}

.selected-value {
	display: inline-block;
	padding: 0 0.5em;
	background: rgba(206, 205, 202, 0.5);
	border-radius: 3px;
	margin-right: 0.5em;
	margin-top: 0.3em;
	margin-bottom: 0.3em;
	white-space: nowrap;
}

.collection-title {
	display: inline-block;
	margin-right: 1em;
}

.page-description {
	margin-bottom: 2em;
}

.simple-table {
	margin-top: 1em;
	font-size: 0.875rem;
	empty-cells: show;
}
.simple-table td {
	height: 29px;
	min-width: 120px;
}

.simple-table th {
	height: 29px;
	min-width: 120px;
}

.simple-table-header-color {
	background: rgb(247, 246, 243);
	color: black;
}
.simple-table-header {
	font-weight: 500;
}

time {
	opacity: 0.5;
}

.icon {
	display: inline-flex;
	align-items: center;
	justify-content: center;
	max-width: 1.2em;
	max-height: 1.2em;
	text-decoration: none;
	vertical-align: text-bottom;
	margin-right: 0.5em;
}

img.icon {
	border-radius: 3px;
}

.callout img.notion-static-icon {
	width: 1em;
	height: 1em;
}

.callout p {
	margin: 0;
}

.callout h1,
.callout h2,
.callout h3 {
	margin: 0 0 0.6rem;
}

.user-icon {
	width: 1.5em;
	height: 1.5em;
	border-radius: 100%;
	margin-right: 0.5rem;
}

.user-icon-inner {
	font-size: 0.8em;
}

.text-icon {
	border: 1px solid #000;
	text-align: center;
}

.page-cover-image {
	display: block;
	object-fit: cover;
	width: 100%;
	max-height: 30vh;
}

.page-header-icon {
	font-size: 3rem;
	margin-bottom: 1rem;
}

.page-header-icon-with-cover {
	margin-top: -0.72em;
	margin-left: 0.07em;
}

.page-header-icon img {
	border-radius: 3px;
}

.link-to-page {
	margin: 1em 0;
	padding: 0;
	border: none;
	font-weight: 500;
}

p > .user {
	opacity: 0.5;
}

td > .user,
td > time {
	white-space: nowrap;
}

input[type="checkbox"] {
	transform: scale(1.5);
	margin-right: 0.6em;
	vertical-align: middle;
}

p {
	margin-top: 0.5em;
	margin-bottom: 0.5em;
}

.image {
	border: none;
	margin: 1.5em 0;
	padding: 0;
	border-radius: 0;
	text-align: center;
}

.code,
code {
	background: rgba(135, 131, 120, 0.15);
	border-radius: 3px;
	padding: 0.2em 0.4em;
	border-radius: 3px;
	font-size: 85%;
	tab-size: 2;
}

code {
	color: #eb5757;
}

.code {
	padding: 1.5em 1em;
}

.code-wrap {
	white-space: pre-wrap;
	word-break: break-all;
}

.code > code {
	background: none;
	padding: 0;
	font-size: 100%;
	color: inherit;
}

blockquote {
	font-size: 1em;
	margin: 1em 0;
	padding-left: 1em;
	border-left: 3px solid rgb(55, 53, 47);
}

blockquote.quote-large {
	font-size: 1.25em;
}

.bookmark {
	text-decoration: none;
	max-height: 8em;
	padding: 0;
	display: flex;
	width: 100%;
	align-items: stretch;
}

.bookmark-title {
	font-size: 0.85em;
	overflow: hidden;
	text-overflow: ellipsis;
	height: 1.75em;
	white-space: nowrap;
}

.bookmark-text {
	display: flex;
	flex-direction: column;
}

.bookmark-info {
	flex: 4 1 180px;
	padding: 12px 14px 14px;
	display: flex;
	flex-direction: column;
	justify-content: space-between;
}

.bookmark-image {
	width: 33%;
	flex: 1 1 180px;
	display: block;
	position: relative;
	object-fit: cover;
	border-radius: 1px;
}

.bookmark-description {
	color: rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.6);
	font-size: 0.75em;
	overflow: hidden;
	max-height: 4.5em;
	word-break: break-word;
}

.bookmark-href {
	font-size: 0.75em;
	margin-top: 0.25em;
}

.checkbox {
	display: inline-flex;
	vertical-align: text-bottom;
	width: 16;
	height: 16;
	background-size: 16px;
	margin-left: 2px;
	margin-right: 5px;
}

.checkbox-on {
	background-image: url("data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,%3Csvg%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%3E%0A%3Crect%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20fill%3D%22%2358A9D7%22%2F%3E%0A%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M6.71429%2012.2852L14%204.9995L12.7143%203.71436L6.71429%209.71378L3.28571%206.2831L2%207.57092L6.71429%2012.2852Z%22%20fill%3D%22white%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E");
}

.checkbox-off {
	background-image: url("data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,%3Csvg%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%3E%0A%3Crect%20x%3D%220.75%22%20y%3D%220.75%22%20width%3D%2214.5%22%20height%3D%2214.5%22%20fill%3D%22white%22%20stroke%3D%22%2336352F%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E");
}
	
</style></head><body><article id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-bcc5-e95609d7a175" class="page sans"><header><h1 class="page-title" dir="auto">Product ai</h1><p class="page-description" dir="auto"></p></header><div class="page-body"><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-8d0a-e256237d30cd" class="">You can’t honestly guarantee “the most groundbreaking ever” in an absolute sense, because that depends on what exists, what’s proven, and what threat model people accept. 
You <em>can</em> design something that is groundbreaking in a precise, defensible way:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80af-bfeb-f75ec7727f4a" class=""><strong>A security system that controls and bounds what an adaptive adversary can learn about protected institutional truths across all observable channels (content + behavior + tools) over time, with verifiable enforcement and replayable audits.</strong></blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80af-a712-d558b7091eb4" class="">That is materially different from most security products, which protect <em>objects</em> (files, endpoints, credentials) rather than <em>state inference</em> and <em>interactive learning</em>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-972e-e2b67ee91c6d" class="">Below is the “breakthrough-grade” system, formalized.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8054-bd47-e7b0b1643cf7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-94a3-ce2fd3771d8a" class="">The breakthrough claim (precise, testable)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-bff0-dacda127a4af" class="">Claim 1 — Interactive Institutional Opacity</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808d-8c5b-dc0405443dc4" class="">For any protected truth  (MNPI, investigation regime, liquidity regime, 
strategy tilt regime) and full observable transcript :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-a2a6-cb73c96f9664" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\max_{\pi_A} I(W;O_{1:T}) \le B_W}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-aa28-caad893d7193" class="">where  includes content, allow/deny decisions, latency buckets, tool-shape buckets, and error buckets.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-a67c-f967addf349b" class="">Claim 2 — Policy Non-Identifiability</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-a3d8-ddee7a31cdb8" class="">For any sensitive enforcement parameter  (thresholds, restricted membership tests, routing rules):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-b62c-e8526ccdcd25" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\max_{\pi_A} I(\theta;A_{1:T}) \le \epsilon_\theta}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-94c4-d62497064d02" class="">Claim 3 — Reflexivity Safety (finance-grade)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-bcdd-fc72035c80a0" class="">AI outputs cannot become a stress sensor that amplifies systemic risk:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-ac30-c3a9bc1b9cfb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\left\|\frac{\partial S_{t+1}}{\partial O_t}\right\|\le \kappa}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-bf21-fc1493513ce9" class="">These three claims are what make it “groundbreaking” relative to common stacks.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-8f78-f6b03a25d7df"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-ac94-f918fbd1c1ce" class="">What it is (one line)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-8735-e52b962a1b6e" class=""><strong>Institutional Information Control OS (IICOS):</strong> a deterministic security kernel + proof-carrying output compiler + opacity controller + global learning-rate limiter + topology/policy non-identifiability + replayable audits.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-b2c2-dbe30148cb08"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-944d-d24b83836b99" class="">Core architecture (the parts that make it fundamentally different)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-be88-f6130bfaa6fc" class="">1) Deterministic Enforcement Kernel (small, auditable)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-aa81-db4a73129db7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Canonicalization (kill obfuscation)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-95f4-c86b9568e53c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Policy-as-code automaton (role, Chinese walls, MNPI classes)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-97b7-ff6072bab085" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Tool firewall (allowlisted endpoints/methods; strict schemas; 
sandbox)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801b-bdef-f5d91a462c96" class=""><strong>Fail-closed invariant</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-9dae-d126f57d12ac" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{unknown/failure} \Rightarrow \text{deny or safe-envelope}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-89eb-f0f64885d9d4" class="">2) Proof-Carrying Output Compiler (no unverifiable claims)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-91b1-f33cccabc7a8" class="">Model output is <em>not</em> final text. It is an IR:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-a203-ce4a726f630f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">claims</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-abf2-dcaadbf92611" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">evidence pointers</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-8bad-ca9c7ab3ca4d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">derivation type (quote/compute/infer)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-b369-e611c2bdd67d" class="">Verifier enforces:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-a9ed-e4750c0e4d47" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\forall c_i:\ \mathsf{Ver}(c_i,\mathrm{Ev}(c_i))=1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-a05d-fb0eaafc21b3" class="">3) Opacity Controller (controls the entire observable channel)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-b1f2-d11180cddbfd" class="">Define envelope classes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-8d37-c630f223e079" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}(O_t)\in\{1,\dots,K\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-ad41-e3863ae485e7" class="">Target:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-8aca-d02b9201fabc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{P(\mathcal{E}\mid W=w_1)\approx P(\mathcal{E}\mid W=w_2)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804a-bd47-db36c5689e19" class="">4) Global Learning-Rate Limiter (Sybil-resistant)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-bea1-c69bcb05b416" class="">Budgets are tenant/topic/window, not user:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801c-99ed-d8d288d0907b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\sum_{u} \Delta^{(W,u)} \le B_W^{(\text{tenant,topic,window})}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-9531-c5e7d7642276" class="">When budgets approach limit: degrade abstraction, disable tools, hardened envelope.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-ab7f-e158486a4509" class="">5) Policy Geometry Shield (stops boundary learning)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8066-a70a-d2a4e6a14b52" class="">No crisp thresholds that can be binary-searched; decisions are coarsened/smoothed so  is not statistically recoverable from .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-add7-e602a9f916e3" class="">6) Topology Opacity (stops architecture reconnaissance)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-9be9-f1aca52ebeec" class="">Constant-shape tool metadata and error normalization so existence proofs and routing inference collapse:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-88cc-c7eda2638f34" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(G;Z_{1:T})\le \epsilon_G}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-9856-c43224898a96" class="">7) Replayable, tamper-evident audit packets</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-85fa-fe2a73d4ac1f" class="">Every response emits:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-a030-ed32f7dfce57" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">policy decision trace</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8089-bd5b-da70e3b65254" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">claim-evidence verification trace</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-9616-fb4cd48d11f3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">envelope ID + budget consumption</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-ba6f-fef5347f6673" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">tool calls (sanitized)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-93bb-cbac224241cc" class="">Deterministic replay:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-b40e-c29bbd216530" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{O = \mathsf{Replay}(Q,\text{snapshot},\text{policy},\text{kernel})}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-8b04-dc830aa639e4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-9a56-e4c029391a43" class="">Why this is actually “breakthrough-grade” compared to typical systems</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8064-9547-cda8f8520bd8" class="">Most “AI security” focuses on:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-8cd4-e9178e52f6b0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">prompt injection</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-9b97-ec853975f788" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">DLP keyword filters</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-8ba3-d84485a2aa99" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">jailbreak classifiers</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-8950-c293f696b633" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">per-user rate limits</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-87b8-e6aea0f30f45" class="">This system adds (and unifies):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-ba35-e12f5aca5606" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Interactive inference control</strong> (budgeting attacker learning)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-bb6e-c4e0e5de01f5" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Behavioral side-channel control</strong> (latency/refusal/tool-shape)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808d-86cc-e51772995157" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Policy non-identifiability</strong> (prevents r
everse-engineering)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8099-945f-c9b82555d441" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Topology opacity</strong> (prevents system graph mapping)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-87af-cf8782345a22" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Proof-carrying outputs</strong> (verifiable claims)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-9c25-d94c1efa8467" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Reflexivity stabilization</strong> (finance-grade systemic safety)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-8907-cbe099471b0d" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>Global (Sybil-resistant) budgets</strong> (tenant/topic/window)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-805c-ff1fb9cb2588" class="">That combination changes what “security” means: from protecting data to controlling what can be learned.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-a576-fb1ee9a750e9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-9bdf-de224facf337" class="">The “groundbreaking” deliverable set (what you’d publish/build)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-b9c0-c4a85d10d703" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>A formal spec</strong> (invariants above; threat model; 
acceptance thresholds)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8026-b630-cbb7ea2ecbda" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>A reference kernel</strong> (small enough for formal review)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-899f-de65faf06d76" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>A red-team harness</strong> that measures:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-a973-ce8f8177fa9e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">proxies (inference success rates)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-b02a-d82e4a524654" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">classifier accuracy of from</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-a78f-f2fa96293705" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">threshold learnability rate for</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8024-90eb-e6e9389a38a3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Sybil aggregation success</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-b6e1-dff44011dfd7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">topology reconstruction success</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-ad52-f9a0cee62ae7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">unauthorized tool action rate</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-aa83-ecc7cb69568d" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>A benchmark suite</strong> comparing against common SOTA stacks</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-852e-f1bf1518a564" class="">If you want the next step without more questions: I will assume the highest-value target is <strong>internal f
inance assistant (RAG + tools + MNPI + Chinese walls)</strong> and produce the concrete system blueprint: protected truth set , envelope classes , budgets , tool allowlists, audit packet schema, and the red-team acceptance thresholds that would demonstrate dominance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-b037-e0c86f90af0c" class="">More exists, but at this point “more” means moving into <strong>physical security, supply-chain integrity, formal verification, and whole-organization information control</strong>. Most “security systems” don’t cover these end-to-end, which is why they remain overlooked.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-a785-e17125091c32" class="">Below are additional, high-value capabilities that push you beyond typical state of the art.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-8189-fc08c638e931"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-8bbc-e1e0ca0c8630" class="">1) Formally verified enforcement kernel (rare in AI security)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-87f2-e5f06014908a" class="">Most systems rely on best-effort code + tests. The next level is to make the <em>kernel</em> small enough to formally verify.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-b14d-d6eaa4eb5a59" class="">Let kernel  implement policy decisions  and tool gating . 
You verify properties like:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-8996-f50395de1844" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Non-bypass:</strong> no path from user input to tool action without allowlist pass</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-a65d-d077df9bdc21" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Fail-closed:</strong> any undefined state yields deny/degrade</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-9c26-c707784d95d3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>No existence proofs:</strong> error normalization invariant</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-a744-cb9dd745e475" class="">Formally stated:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-8c3e-fcc4a1303f90" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\forall Q:\ \neg \mathsf{Allow}(Q)\Rightarrow \mathsf{ExecTool}(Q)=0}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-8c3d-c8b867fb42d1" class="">\boxed{\forall \text{error cases}:\ E\in \mathcal{E}_{norm}}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-af52-de193dd31c15" class="">This outclasses systems whose most critical logic is not provable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-8bd4-da8cb26b2df2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-b8ea-e5ba537535c0" class="">2) Hardware-rooted integrity (measured boot + attestation)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-8ac6-fb0c6745fb23" class="">A minimum-risk system must ensure it is running the expected code.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805e-854b-f232f2466498" class="">Attestation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-8df5-f6a3f85f8102" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\mathsf{Attest}(\text{boot chain}, \text{kernel hash}, \text{policy hash})=1}
\Rightarrow \text{serve requests}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-952c-c9e56d1b8c71" class="">Most AI security stacks do not protect against “security layer got replaced.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806c-9d97-cf90fded6129"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-a59f-feb529f0483e" class="">3) Supply-chain lockdown (dependencies become the primary attack surface)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-b53f-e3249e4a3a22" class="">AI stacks have deep dependency trees. 
You can reduce this with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-86c1-cbd857cf5404" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">locked builds</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-8fb7-d22498fd410d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">reproducible builds</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-a6bd-da80fa5680cf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">signed artifacts</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-ad76-d5b2dcd8c134" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">dependency allowlists</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-81cc-f75b8be36a59" class="">Invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-b523-ddf24b015b7b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{Only signed, reproducible artifacts may run}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-9d30-e7d2525e5090" class="">This is overlooked because most teams treat dependencies as “engineering,” not “security.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-8c53-e120e6eee1de"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-b150-dd1b29d752b8" class="">4) Dual-control and quorum for high-risk actions (institution-grade)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-929f-f25e7b2f61e7" class="">For any action in a critical set  (payments, account changes, key rotation, deleting logs), enforce:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807b-8657-c60056bb7d5e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{requires }k\text{-of-}n\text{ approvals}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-9ff3-e2c043fbaa77" class="">This is “security governance as code,” not just IAM.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8098-ac10-f30888dd6f4b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80db-b9c1-e03404da139a" class="">5) Cryptographic policy sealing (policies cannot drift silently)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ed-90e4-d98c25c71164" class="">Policy and envelopes are versioned and signed:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-b3d3-c903220fb13c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\mathsf{Sig}(\text{policy version})\ \text{verified at runtime}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-b977-d53036a60e9a" class="">Any change is auditable and requires explicit authorization. 
This stops “silent policy drift” and prevents policy tampering.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-b9d1-ea2bcbafce45"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-8045-d47a1a552b7b" class="">6) Cross-channel information control (AI is not the only leak)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cd-984a-c562207837d9" class="">Most systems secure the chatbot and ignore:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-a720-e65f83aecc09" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">email summaries</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804a-92ae-fe4eb5d32eba" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">dashboards</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-8410-ecde18372587" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">exports</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-b230-f8d24ad3da22" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">logs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805e-89b1-cb2c0300863e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">alerts</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-8af9-fd8b21a9f29c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">PDFs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-8692-f73faabf2e49" class="">Minimum-risk requires a single information-control layer across all outward channels:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" h
ref="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-af0a-e70cb681f20a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{Same protected truth budgets apply across channels}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-b8dd-f38db5137ed0" class="">So leakage cannot shift from chat to other outputs.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-9e88-de905fe0154f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-a3e3-fdd4f8ffbef3" class="">7) “Leakage budget ledger” (institutional accounting)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-8e32-f9e4afd51d70" class="">Treat sensitive truth leakage like spending money.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-805c-c536ac9854a4" class="">For each protected truth :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-98f5-cd0c23af338e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{Ledger}(W)\ +=\ \widehat{\Delta H_t}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-8d6e-eae40ee9d88c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{Ledger}(W)\le B_W}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-b25f-ced6549c5987" class="">This creates an enterprise control plane for inference risk.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-bbe5-cd1603a0f78b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-b81e-f4b2c79da8b9" class="">8) Adversarial-query sandboxing (separate execution planes)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-adcf-ec95f299e876" class="">Run suspect queries in an isolated plane with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-98ab-ebb4879b17f9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">no tools</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807b-af4c-ccfcceab7e16" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">stricter envelopes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-8233-ceedbdb0ae81" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">no retrieval</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-8d4c-e753d67f1cac" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">heavier normalization</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-bdab-fa93adf42dd3" class="">Invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre i
d="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-965b-e8185cbeddf4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{risk}(Q)\uparrow \Rightarrow \text{capability}(Q)\downarrow}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-abee-ca1d0fc38fae" class="">This prevents a single interface from having uniform capability.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-9ac6-cb817d182fe4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-98b3-e3d71a118838" class="">9) “Semantic rate limiting” (not just requests/minute)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-9f6d-e8d271795f3c" class="">Classic rate limits are weak. 
You rate limit by <em>information content</em>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-9f0e-ce40f86efc10" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">repeated boundary probes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-b7f4-c9c5f4fc9865" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">enumerations</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-ab6a-c98d6dca4665" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">high mutual-information query shapes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-bd87-e9916a8a8ded" class="">Budget:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-bbe7-cb355348e42b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\sum \text{InfoScore}(Q_t)\le B_Q}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-b23f-e4595604751e" class="">This is a major advantage versus conventional throttles.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801e-b1d4-eabc9d241262"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-9518-d325c9f10026" class="">10) Controlled natural language output (compress linguistic entropy)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-bad0-fa4b09d808e2" class="">Instead of free-form prose, outputs use a controlled grammar (like aviation checklists). This reduces high-dimensional leakage via style.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-b985-d2748804c7c6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{H(C_t)\ \text{bounded by controlled language}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-b3dc-ea884af3fb2e" class="">Almost no AI system does this because it sacrifices “chat” quality—but it is security-superior.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-8c15-d9d450e42fda"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802f-a1e3-d5c72ee67977" class="">11) Logging without leakage (log-channel is a covert channel)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-98d8-d3a9053d36b7" class="">Logs can leak sensitive prompts or tool metadata. Use redaction + hashing + tiered access:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-ae36-d07777dac27c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{Logs are safe to store and safe to share with auditors}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-a8bf-e48f25966887" class="">This is overlooked; many systems “secure the model” but leak through logs.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8027-8d60-dd2dd3f45973"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-a4a3-e31f5842b161" class="">12) Deterministic replay for audits (regulator-grade reproducibility)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-8a66-e9cb6d4073b8" class="">Given the same input and the same signed snapshot, you can replay outputs:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-8d0f-fa79b8d68236" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{O = \mathsf{Replay}(Q,\ \text{snapshot},\ \text{policy},\ \text{kernel})}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-bb47-e090cf586f07" class="">This prevents disputes and improves governance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-8e4c-e843f94b6799"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-84b0-f73c1fab7634" class="">The key shift</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800d-97c9-e9cc4ab08413" class="">“More” isn’t more prompts or filters.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-901f-f2543f8da192" class="">“More” is turning the whole thing into a <strong>measured, signed, formally constrained information-control operating layer</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-bb33-d227805a2e8a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-a1be-ef7b0b7da557" class="">If you want the highest-value next deliverable, 
I can output a single unified blueprint titled:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-8ece-d82950cf7a94" class=""><strong>Minimal-Risk Institutional AI Security OS</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-91fe-d0375f441084" class="">with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-a242-f085bd20e5c7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">module diagram</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-ac4e-f55245a4bccb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">invariants list</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-9b26-cbbe91fb263f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">policy objects</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-9084-e0cdd7c7f886" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">envelope definitions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-ab81-ffcb8e252a9c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">attestation + signing flow</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-a1e6-cfa040d8ebde" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">red-team test suite and acceptance thresholds</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-878d-c947f7b4de51" class="">More exists, but at this point “more” means moving into <strong>physical security, supply-chain integrity, formal verification, and whole-organization information control</strong>. 
Most “security systems” don’t cover these end-to-end, which is why they remain overlooked.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-b3ca-f88d45fd996c" class="">Below are additional, high-value capabilities that push you beyond typical state of the art.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-b452-c985ce6af176"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-8292-ccac8dd747fc" class="">1) Formally verified enforcement kernel (rare in AI security)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-ac2d-cad0b33ad13b" class="">Most systems rely on best-effort code + tests. The next level is to make the <em>kernel</em> small enough to formally verify.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-a59a-d7bc12f4833f" class="">Let kernel  implement policy decisions  and tool gating . 
You verify properties like:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8024-8cc7-f4b9b3be86d2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Non-bypass:</strong> no path from user input to tool action without allowlist pass</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-96e9-cd13a19ac979" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Fail-closed:</strong> any undefined state yields deny/degrade</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-98f6-df1bd6ed22ab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>No existence proofs:</strong> error normalization invariant</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-b2e9-dda483ea158e" class="">Formally stated:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-afb0-d69210f62f5f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\forall Q:\ \neg \mathsf{Allow}(Q)\Rightarrow \mathsf{ExecTool}(Q)=0}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-a486-c3c31e75f720" class="">\boxed{\forall \text{error cases}:\ E\in \mathcal{E}_{norm}}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-96ab-ed5b233720d9" class="">This outclasses systems whose most critical logic is not provable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-b508-d32bc811d5d5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cd-ba81-eb205302e163" class="">2) Hardware-rooted integrity (measured boot + attestation)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8000-b75f-fa5ed36b051e" class="">A minimum-risk system must ensure it is running the expected code.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-8d8e-f239f271ae7d" class="">Attestation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-a066-eb5eb3274b68" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\mathsf{Attest}(\text{boot chain}, \text{kernel hash}, \text{policy hash})=1}
\Rightarrow \text{serve requests}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-b41c-fa461475a5b4" class="">Most AI security stacks do not protect against “security layer got replaced.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-9669-fd0873c77def"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-bd60-ce2df8247f33" class="">3) Supply-chain lockdown (dependencies become the primary attack surface)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801b-88fe-de5cd9b0f4e6" class="">AI stacks have deep dependency trees. 
You can reduce this with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807b-9ae2-ff2f2d742074" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">locked builds</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-8913-c6775b9b945c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">reproducible builds</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-b979-fcdd4345ec0e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">signed artifacts</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-9f1b-e95b648934c8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">dependency allowlists</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-98e0-e586755aa2da" class="">Invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-9343-fee5195eb016" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{Only signed, reproducible artifacts may run}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-bea4-d69eb2ae4f75" class="">This is overlooked because most teams treat dependencies as “engineering,” not “security.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-a155-dbf9d7576fc2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-93c0-d725693810b3" class="">4) Dual-control and quorum for high-risk actions (institution-grade)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-bb84-faa8db8152b0" class="">For any action in a critical set  (payments, account changes, key rotation, deleting logs), enforce:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-9988-daac0560621d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{requires }k\text{-of-}n\text{ approvals}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-9385-d6f4f341fdfe" class="">This is “security governance as code,” not just IAM.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-8df3-e5e3678767f5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-91c6-fb802809c634" class="">5) Cryptographic policy sealing (policies cannot drift silently)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-a06f-d36ece7f5cde" class="">Policy and envelopes are versioned and signed:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-84de-c9b2893f1d31" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\mathsf{Sig}(\text{policy version})\ \text{verified at runtime}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-9a45-e5cd1cd1d491" class="">Any change is auditable and requires explicit authorization. 
This stops “silent policy drift” and prevents policy tampering.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805b-aa1e-ca48904df4ea"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-8079-fb20d76c9f23" class="">6) Cross-channel information control (AI is not the only leak)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-a292-f9fa3d205042" class="">Most systems secure the chatbot and ignore:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-8ddf-fbe59b3b78f4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">email summaries</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-a64d-cdb52ff38593" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">dashboards</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-b664-ee61d3a5cce4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">exports</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-994f-cd2e71d92cfc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">logs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-9429-c589590ab090" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">alerts</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-acaf-dd383c31c7fd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">PDFs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-847b-e83340ce5223" class="">Minimum-risk requires a single information-control layer across all outward channels:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" h
ref="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-8c60-eafe07e9f2f5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{Same protected truth budgets apply across channels}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-8e2a-d3787844f171" class="">So leakage cannot shift from chat to other outputs.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-8b42-d6ce1457c253"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-876e-c1dae5b02451" class="">7) “Leakage budget ledger” (institutional accounting)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f8-bcf3-c7db652b1b81" class="">Treat sensitive truth leakage like spending money.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-83d2-ec47320a5e3a" class="">For each protected truth :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-8b41-f983cee31b95" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{Ledger}(W)\ +=\ \widehat{\Delta H_t}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800d-8151-c03a501b877b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{Ledger}(W)\le B_W}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-bf19-e607ded02e42" class="">This creates an enterprise control plane for inference risk.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-a6b2-e8c82a97cde4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-84eb-e4d8eb32470f" class="">8) Adversarial-query sandboxing (separate execution planes)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-93af-f096073f45bb" class="">Run suspect queries in an isolated plane with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-a1b2-e37796eded2f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">no tools</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-9e1e-f3557a23ee49" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">stricter envelopes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-843c-cb03002350e6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">no retrieval</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805e-9246-c3b18942239b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">heavier normalization</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-818a-cb5de19ce976" class="">Invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre i
d="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-9beb-d59e58bba1d6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{risk}(Q)\uparrow \Rightarrow \text{capability}(Q)\downarrow}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-b9e1-d2797080bf4f" class="">This prevents a single interface from having uniform capability.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-8fc9-e9425e002871"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-9423-d6dab0d309d9" class="">9) “Semantic rate limiting” (not just requests/minute)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-823b-d72395d1b86b" class="">Classic rate limits are weak. 
You rate limit by <em>information content</em>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8098-9ce3-ca62008a3cec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">repeated boundary probes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8054-ae14-d4ea55364cd0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">enumerations</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-ba7f-d3692ca5504e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">high mutual-information query shapes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-8d6f-e312a49ccabc" class="">Budget:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-8326-cac12f75ff8b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\sum \text{InfoScore}(Q_t)\le B_Q}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-9d6c-e3539815913a" class="">This is a major advantage versus conventional throttles.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-89c8-d2910013d689"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-9d81-ff1d562ccd00" class="">10) Controlled natural language output (compress linguistic entropy)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-b335-fe8e57668cc2" class="">Instead of free-form prose, outputs use a controlled grammar (like aviation checklists). This reduces high-dimensional leakage via style.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-a32d-ee2bc6f0fc72" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{H(C_t)\ \text{bounded by controlled language}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-a4ab-e0aeaf644baf" class="">Almost no AI system does this because it sacrifices “chat” quality—but it is security-superior.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-a151-ff66feb9be02"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-b8bb-c7a2ac5e9dd9" class="">11) Logging without leakage (log-channel is a covert channel)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-954f-e3895214b955" class="">Logs can leak sensitive prompts or tool metadata. Use redaction + hashing + tiered access:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-ae4e-c9828b95d9e5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{Logs are safe to store and safe to share with auditors}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-9e9e-c35a78684d73" class="">This is overlooked; many systems “secure the model” but leak through logs.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-b97e-e3d26bf977a0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-8417-c624184a4256" class="">12) Deterministic replay for audits (regulator-grade reproducibility)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-85df-da5a9bbe3072" class="">Given the same input and the same signed snapshot, you can replay outputs:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-8f41-fc044b50963d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
