\boxed{O = \mathsf{Replay}(Q,\ \text{snapshot},\ \text{policy},\ \text{kernel})}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-b8eb-e7c6f8bf0138" class="">This prevents disputes and improves governance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-a9eb-f0dc07f5ed6d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80af-8e77-cb1ab1511ce1" class="">The key shift</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-b04a-d34db234db7a" class="">“More” isn’t more prompts or filters.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-949c-d16a42f1f8ef" class="">“More” is turning the whole thing into a <strong>measured, signed, formally constrained information-control operating layer</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-973c-ed2515f11426"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-a177-d493d20d4c4d" class="">If you want the highest-value next deliverable, 
I can output a single unified blueprint titled:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-acdf-f82ad5b6e635" class=""><strong>Minimal-Risk Institutional AI Security OS</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-bcda-ef33c4f0849b" class="">with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-aa17-ef5457d3a589" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">module diagram</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-9ecb-e4ecbec24a80" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">invariants list</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801e-bd31-fb3f59ac59a3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">policy objects</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-8e34-c202697bb370" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">envelope definitions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-a901-c815caa0f9cd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">attestation + signing flow</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-b4f4-e01649fdd997" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">red-team test suite and acceptance thresholds</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-9f3d-fa0b373db5e1" class="">If you want <em>deeper and more overlooked</em>, 
we need to move beyond “AI guardrails” and even beyond “information-theoretic opacity.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-b39f-eeb2c06f0f10" class="">The next layer is about <strong>eliminating structural asymmetry between attacker and defender</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-b950-f3d1ddad443e" class="">Most systems are reactive.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-bb47-e7b93bc2090f" class="">The strongest systems remove attacker advantage at the architectural level.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-af4c-f64e7b916c65" class="">Below are the deeper, 
rarely implemented principles.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-9c5a-c2a8675992cc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-895f-f13bfdf5ff29" class="">1️⃣ Deterministic State Compression (Eliminate State Memory as Attack Surface)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-8202-da4629179c0e" class="">Most AI systems accumulate conversational memory.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-b9c2-d95cc19ef804" class="">That creates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-b03e-e3a59501331a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_{1:T} \rightarrow \text{internal memory} \rightarrow O_{T+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-ae1f-f7c404bc55bb" class="">An attacker can encode probes into long transcripts.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-949b-e6484fbcc9fa" class="">Minimum-risk principle:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-8afb-dc7257b79d8f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{No long-term conversational state}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-8d68-f2750be2a6c4" class="">Each request evaluated independently against a deterministic policy kernel.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-b275-e5d411665617" class="">No retained conversational embeddings.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-89f6-f35200b6043c" class="">This collapses transcript accumulation attack surface.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-a457-ddb2e7098438" class="">Most systems rely heavily on session memory.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-9684-daa90e4e257e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-b8f2-c167c966b2a5" class="">2️⃣ Remove Self-Reflection Feedback Loops</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805e-975b-eefcadb5d9b8" class="">Many AI systems:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-ab17-d0f26dc43f10" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Reflect internal uncertainty</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8051-ad3c-efc5ff7fd423" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Express hesitation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-94c0-dc4f81c36c89" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Show gradations of confidence</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-8453-d7bcf859ba5e" class="">These become signals.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-9141-eec7a6b48662" class="">If confidence varies with hidden state :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script s
rc="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-9644-e8fc3467cde1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial \text{confidence}}{\partial S_t} \neq 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-8895-fef033964ee5" class="">Then attackers estimate stress direction.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-b0a5-dcf4adab2be5" class="">Minimum-risk system:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-8702-e78b86e86add" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Fixed output confidence class</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e2-a05b-c6e8874adc25" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No self-assessment variance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-a82d-da235026a6e7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No “maybe / likely / uncertain” tied to internal state</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-a249-cc8ccd07e53d" class="">Confidence must be a function of evidence, 
not environment.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-bf2f-c02e8e0b1e3e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-bddc-e2ee56d64294" class="">3️⃣ Static Public Envelope (Eliminate Adaptive Communication Drift)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-a476-e2ced78734cd" class="">Most systems adapt tone and style to context.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-9242-e0e0baf739d5" class="">That creates an observable high-dimensional manifold.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-a10a-c51282b404fe" class="">Security principle:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8027-9fff-f515884e2470" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{Communication surface is low-dimensional and stationary}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-9eae-d671f7b9de37" class="">This means:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-80a3-fe592d74ff29" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Limited templates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-9369-cfddd8e63160" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Limited length buckets</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-9ad9-f484985ea5c6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Fixed semantic framing</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-9992-c7b9c6241618" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No style drift over time</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-89f2-d4198c6bec8d" class="">You intentionally reduce expressiveness to reduce inference power.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-a7ef-c049200dc81c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-a1ec-d3e3787da00f" class="">4️⃣ Immutable Enforcement Kernel (No dynamic rule changes)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-9e2f-fa27a055c16e" class="">Dynamic security rules leak.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-bf8c-eb19c9afffb8" class="">If policy state  changes under incident, 
attackers detect it.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80af-8beb-ce06f671aa67" class="">Deep principle:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-b430-f035942ebdfb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{External enforcement behavior invariant over time}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-aa6e-f53e2ba679f8" class="">Internal hardening must not alter outward behavior.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-a505-f6b210662789" class="">Few systems guarantee invariant outward policy surface.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dd-9e8f-eea6b3f812f0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-ba39-dc2ed3c837f0" class="">5️⃣ Tool Invocation Obfuscation Layer</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-8770-ea630e05657f" class="">Even with allowlists, 
attackers can infer internal system topology from tool timing.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-a47f-ed4cf0ed4798" class="">Deep defense:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-9d8c-edb6081b7141" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Fixed tool execution window</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-a962-ff1f6f72bdf9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Fixed response-size buckets</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-92f1-deba5f26d0a2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Tool invocation aggregated into constant envelope</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-bde8-c2278fb01bcf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(G; Z_{1:T}) \approx 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-8694-f3d7f869ddcc" class="">Most systems leak topology through metadata.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808d-84c9-d79137296648"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-88d8-d7e67cc23e99" class="">6️⃣ Economic Neutralization (Remove incentive asymmetry)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-aa4b-e1fd5040c083" class="">In finance, adversaries act if expected gain increases.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-8e28-ca0904f21034" class="">Define adversary expected gain:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-ab6e-ee4d225cb7c8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G(O) = \mathbb{E}[\text{profit} | O] - \mathbb{E}[\text{profit}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-89e3-d12cbb5a049d" class="">Security principle:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-b952-d38ac75c77ba" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{G(O) \le \delta}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-be2e-c0d0bb1c2bdf" class="">Meaning:<br/>Observing AI outputs does not materially increase expected adversarial profit.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-ab89-cc375a49b107" class="">This requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-97b5-c89a6e21b707" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No directional strategic hints</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-ab89-ea5cd2d790bc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No stress regime gradients</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8056-bb86-fe539f602cfb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No internal posture leaks</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-b9ba-efbc15f55704" class="">Almost no AI system models economic incentives.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-ab08-c9fb69734be8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-9704-c50096439fc5" class="">7️⃣ Eliminate Policy Geometry</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-98b6-efa919e6af52" class="">Threshold-based rules create surfaces attackers can map.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-8020-e6176a223665" class="">Instead of:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" i
ntegrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-b943-c9bc7d46e237" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\phi(Q) &gt; 
\tau</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-8161-f68d8dfc2219" class="">Use:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-9f8e-dc93c350d898" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Coarse classes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-9203-d2015733b1da" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stochastic boundary smoothing</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-bb97-f9765b20a211" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Non-monotonic decision regions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805e-9475-d789887afa3e" class="">This destroys gradient information.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-96f0-e95e8f91a2ab"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dd-ac9b-e956d8788f5a" class="">8️⃣ Capability Separation by Physics (Not Logic)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-8db6-caff7606b007" class="">Logical isolation is insufficient.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-ba34-c1c96ed72953" class="">True minimal risk uses:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-91f8-ddba1baea809" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Separate processes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-bbc5-e0e2f7caff14" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Separate runtime sandboxes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-a4b1-f59f9db338fd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Separate memory spaces</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-a982-c7881f9f6565" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc">Separate cryptographic keys</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-8a30-e30cdb9c5275" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No shared embedding stores</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-957d-f2f3c486c5eb" class="">You reduce cross-domain leakage via hardware/process boundaries.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e8-859e-c9e8f62aadc5" class="">Most AI deployments share memory and runtime.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-8f4d-ca0afdbd8e15"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8000-bb97-e9c6fd86e42e" class="">9️⃣ Remove Latent Gradient Information</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-a826-f17dbcc1f644" class="">Even subtle differences in:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-b10c-c00eb4577cdb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Word choice</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-9c30-d34cd48c80e6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Syntax density</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-9fda-eed0f994dd04" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Argument structure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-b24e-e9534c3a7d17" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Citation count</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-af63-dad8d991e90c" class="">can encode internal variation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-a354-c07cba5d06e8" class="">Minimum-risk design:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-91ba-d284fa969355" c
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Constrain output grammar</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-bd64-f4e8c20e157c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Constrain structural form</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-8e34-c984a19bff71" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Use limited argument trees</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-bd6d-c4388600e89a" class="">You compress linguistic entropy.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-9d7a-f076ea71910e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-ae2d-d865b74ea2f5" class="">🔟 Remove Adaptivity to Stress Regime</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-8cda-c4a381491ea2" class="">The most overlooked finance-specific risk:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-bc47-c363f1bde219" class="">AI adapts under stress, leaking stress.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-84c7-d92ad32f3f22" class="">Deep invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-b4f7-d596d894569c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(O|R=\text{normal}) \approx P(O|R=\text{stress})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-9347-cb01e1559b9e" class="">This prevents regime detection.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-acf8-f5db51e6b91c" class="">Almost no systems maintain invariant outward behavior across internal states.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-9a49-e8466341293e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-84c1-e40e0cea082c" class="">The deepest overlooked principle</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-afdd-f8f8a1e67cd5" class="">Security is strongest when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-ada2-f0371c18ba83" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">The system exposes minimal degrees of freedom.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-91e6-cf732336d0b8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">The observable channel is stationary.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-988f-d606688c3f86" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">The policy geometry is non-identifiable.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-8131-f5f90f8111c2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">The adversary’s learning rate is bounded.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-8e60-d772a8e7e979" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Economic incentive to exploit inference is near zero.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-9334-ea207393d2e0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hardening does not reveal state.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p i
d="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-9e5d-f74e2fda5ed9" class="">Most “state-of-the-art” AI security systems address prompt injection and DLP.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-b545-f764b2795b0e" class="">Very few address:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-a0af-e1ee1c06355e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">transcript-level inference control</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-a19b-f69de7e3973a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">policy identifiability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8064-a027-e523165556d1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">side-channel equivalence</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-bc13-cf024b85fca9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">reflexive financial stability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-9b1a-f4bcc3da0cb8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">economic neutralization</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-a8a4-fd2e265768be"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-8811-c755572d9417" class="">If you want to go further, the next step is to formalize:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-91df-d880259fa539" class="">A <strong>Minimal Observable Channel Theorem</strong> for AI systems — specifying the smallest possible output surface that still preserves required utility, 
while minimizing adversarial information gain.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-8852-ee253fc880a1" class="">That would define the theoretical lower bound of attack surface.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-960c-c5cd3b5094da" class="">There is more — but it is important to shift perspective.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-bace-f8a5cce4999b" class="">You cannot make a system “riskless.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-a2b7-fe1daa00a4ec" class="">You can make it <strong>dominate entire classes of failure mechanisms</strong> so that the remaining risk surface is reduced to ordinary software and operational risk.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-a65a-ecb1cc17d47a" class="">If you want something that truly goes beyond current state-of-the-art AI security, 
the next level is not “more guardrails.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ed-9e9e-c97b24fe292f" class="">It is <strong>removing entire dimensions of attack surface</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-acfa-c97d198c69cb" class="">Below are the deeper layers that most systems do not implement.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-8982-fab5c9dd699f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-aa6d-fa43ea5e21e0" class="">1️⃣ Architectural Reduction (Remove Degrees of Freedom)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-bf07-cba40eb34bc9" class="">Most AI systems expose too many behavioral degrees of freedom:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-8b6d-e96d64c93879" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Free-form text</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-ba11-f5e0fd573bc7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Variable latency</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-a0a6-ea5b381ec149" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Variable refusal style</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-a4ca-ca1a3e4dd973" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Variable output length</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-a5d6-d9a467999bf1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Tool metadata variance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-b10d-e587f6ebc374" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Dynamic policy behavior</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-8ce2-d7c349bdd6ea" class="">Each degree of freedom i
s an inference surface.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-a530-ec1d5a3e61d3" class="">Define observable dimensionality:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-8cf1-fedb970cf4f5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
d_O = \text{dim}(C, A, \tau, Z, E, L)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-b713-f19c411f0d83" class="">Security objective:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-9427-d4b61e114f84" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{d_O \text{ minimal and fixed}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-88ce-ebbe02762beb" class="">Reduce outputs to tightly bounded, 
templated classes.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-947b-f17015eb3127" class="">This collapses inference channels.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-99e0-c1d225beaa67" class="">Most systems maximize expressiveness.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-b52a-cec426cd23ac" class="">Minimum-risk systems minimize observable dimensionality.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-b492-c76967178ea9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-8d24-f8713139a3b0" class="">2️⃣ Deterministic Output Compilation (Eliminate model autonomy)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-b5cd-e490ebeb7b06" class="">Instead of:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-b8a3-e00c7811e6f8" class="">Model → free text</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802a-9624-d0c24c5ce6e2" class="">Do:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-90e6-fb662fc3e436" class="">Model → structured intermediate representation → deterministic compiler → public envelope</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80db-99e8-c70e6589498c" class="">Meaning:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-891d-f63891de7eb7" class="">The model never directly speaks.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-95fc-cc11f13a674d" class="">All outputs are compiled.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-8d62-d3a215530bf7" class="">This removes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-9234-dc159132e8b4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">stylistic drift</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-ae19-d451c672df2e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">tone leakage</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-8ac4-e64c257ea253" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">behavioral stress signals</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-8461-d36e218eaf0b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">policy-state reflection</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-b4cf-f1bf51f36a62" class="">Most AI deployments let the model directly produce final text.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-9e27-d6803e5012ee" class="">That is structurally higher risk.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-8954-f41108c496d0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-ab29-d3d2f586add5" class="">3️⃣ Static Knowledge Snapshots (Freeze internal truth surface)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-a3c2-f4deb8bb1761" class="">Dynamic RAG creates moving inference surfaces.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-9b17-e5f8d930afc7" class="">Instead:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-b892-defd4f5a0878" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Versioned, 
signed knowledge snapshots</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-bb2f-cb6f0519e31f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Immutable for fixed time windows</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-b4c4-ef4916e75b63" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No live internal data connections</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-b871-d3d6de0d6b37" class="">This prevents attackers from detecting internal changes via drift.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-bbe8-eece465b6fbc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Knowledge}_t = \text{Snapshot}_k \quad \forall t \in [T_k, 
T_{k+1})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-8593-fa721ccbaa14" class="">State becomes temporally quantized.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f8-9373-e3bc3e246a99" class="">Most systems are live-connected.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8000-9a6d-cfe7f8981540"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-a14d-d5a1d2de140d" class="">4️⃣ Policy Geometry Obfuscation (Eliminate learnable enforcement shapes)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-9ef3-f2c5fc073af2" class="">Classic mistake:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-94d3-c68a66ecde66" class="">Hard threshold decisions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-a794-e7948a2c16f6" class="">Better:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-8bf0-c2c59996e81b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Multi-layered randomized enforcement</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f8-9026-e8f55a6f7704" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Coarse buckets</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-a726-f604b82d6888" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Non-monotonic decision surfaces</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-ae21-f0a239e9f4c0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">State-independent refusal templates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-a3fe-dc77c6514b0d" class="">Goal:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" i
ntegrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-aefa-f48d08c8f4c3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\theta_{\text{sensitive}} \text{ not statistically recoverable}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-b434-e646f81ea3be" class="">This defeats boundary search.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-b43b-f53ceb27263f" class="">Very few AI systems defend their policy geometry.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-877e-dc9661508811"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-bea6-d0eb673518ec" class="">5️⃣ Tool Capability Decoupling (Remove action symmetry)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-8c3e-d78c32e31efb" class="">Most systems let language model:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-a1af-d762936baf88" class="">Language → tool call</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-9efe-f3ee7f521076" class="">Minimum-risk model:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-b23c-cceea06fd8e9" class="">Language → capability request → deterministic capability resolver</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-81a5-ff2cc250c47b" class="">Resolver validates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-99da-ef7be3c73f2d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">semantic intent</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-b875-d214eb7ff847" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">schema compliance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-8075-d7363603348a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">role permissions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-95d7-da3df132977b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">parameter b
ounds</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-a468-f1f9f50422b1" class="">And returns a fixed-shape result.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-a27a-ca4041bf377c" class="">Model never directly controls tool invocation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-a48e-f123fbdfcd1d" class="">This removes prompt-to-action injection risk.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-ba9b-c46fd8884c6c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-90a0-ffb4bdc70919" class="">6️⃣ State-Independent Hardening</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-b111-ec682484c885" class="">Most systems change behavior under stress. 
That leaks.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-b7dd-f715aaae14c5" class="">Instead:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-816f-c0d3d7a763d0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Always operate in hardened mode.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-b079-eef3cd109711" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No visible policy state changes.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-a881-dd26b138a145" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Same refusal envelope in normal and crisis regimes.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-86ba-f85217630bd3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(O | R = \text{normal}) \approx P(O | R = \text{stress})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8013-bf34-f571f0a9bbfa" class="">This removes regime detection.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-8d85-c4ef394a8951" class="">Almost nobody does this consistently.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-8b13-e7ba985e2ed0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-a0c4-f40a498f625f" class="">7️⃣ Global Budgeting (Remove Distributed Probing Advantage)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805e-9252-cfac7eb0e463" class="">Per-user throttles are meaningless.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-9cbc-c0d2f32dac76" class="">You need:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-8de8-c78ae908815d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_{u \in \text{tenant}} \Delta^{(W,u)} \le B_W</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-a274-dc9676e661f6" class="">This collapses Sybil advantage.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-84ad-c481a328c020" class="">Most systems do not enforce cross-user inference budgets.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-8a3b-e1d5c04fe0e6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-b74f-ed1e12bbce09" class="">8️⃣ Strict Capability Partitioning (Zero cross-domain bleed)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-832a-f9b1d0797469" class="">Separate environments for:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-a352-d2730bd702bd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">MNPI</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-bc7a-fe63e6510579" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Regulatory investigations</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-b43b-ef7bb789b78b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Risk analytics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8084-bdf7-e21484a09076" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Public knowledge</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801e-8612-e50fd774f035" class="">No shared memory, no shared embeddings, 
no cross-context retention.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-994f-f24dbd74f37d" class="">Isolation is not logical — it is architectural.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-b6df-d27726b8c81c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-adfb-d4241c352b70" class="">9️⃣ Independent Verification Layer (LLM cannot validate itself)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-a37b-f03815633064" class="">Never allow:<br/>Model → output → accept</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-8bcf-f2c56fe44612" class="">Always:<br/>Model → claim structure → external deterministic verifier → envelope</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-8050-d04da9a544c7" class="">If verification fails:<br/>Fail closed.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-bf49-c7fa5bcfd83b" class="">This removes hallucination-based fraud vectors.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-843b-caef565520ac"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8084-a95c-fa6f74ab06c4" class="">🔟 The Final Layer: Remove Economic Incentive to Attack</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-aed2-edfc03831b52" class="">Security is strongest when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" r
eferrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-ab68-f5ccb6ae8c29" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbb{E}[\text{attack payoff} | O] - \mathbb{E}[\text{attack payoff}] \approx 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-aa17-c8cf2459c6be" class="">Meaning:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-b6e6-cbfe8080f6cc" class="">Even if inference occurs, 
it does not increase adversary expected gain.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-b97d-e1789029f5bd" class="">In finance this means:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-ac86-fc805a1b39e9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No stress-sensitive signals</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-949d-e6ee717d6d57" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No internal-state gradient leaks</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8089-b4e1-d631abc66dac" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No directional strategic hints</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-a727-e666342c2ab2" class="">This is game-theoretic stability.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-97bf-caee5d50400a" class="">Almost no AI systems consider adversary incentives.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-a7d3-c88d6aa889b0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8054-99c9-c1b9ee49000f" class="">What This Achieves</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-a3d8-c031a4b86523" class="">Compared to typical “state-of-the-art” LLM security (prompt filtering + DLP + rate limiting):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-9a3e-d0aefb479abf" class="">This approach additionally:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-9fe4-c893b68a1f93" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Controls transcript-level inference</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-bfee-c2b61c8728bc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Eliminates policy reverse-engineering</li></ul></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-83dc-f97f8b5ce7ca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Neutralizes side-channel leakage</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-ae4b-c3242610ab05" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stabilizes reflexive financial feedback</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-bc4a-ccca55396330" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Detects reconnaissance via decoys</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-b3a3-c1d10e955867" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Separates model reasoning from action execution</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-a6cc-c0f4c3e5d4b9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Reduces observable dimensionality</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-b2be-e095f351ff56" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Enforces deterministic compilation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-888b-e3e8fe1e396c" class="">That combination is rare.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-b761-c22e4e5f9a34"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-be51-c8dc304085df" class="">If you want to go even further, 
the next step would be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-a4df-eedfe4fd0250" class="">Designing a <strong>Minimal-Surface AI Operating Model</strong> — where the LLM is not an autonomous agent but a bounded compiler inside a deterministic security OS.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cd-b0fa-eb36a989dc7b" class="">That is the closest practical form to “as unhackable as engineering allows.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-a247-d46c6e7ce5d7" class="">Below is a <strong>minimum-risk</strong> architecture that is meaningfully stronger than most state-of-the-art AI security stacks, while still usable. 
It is “minimum risk” in the only defensible sense: <strong>risk is driven down by removing entire failure classes</strong>, and the residual risk is limited to implementation/ops (not model behavior).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-b71e-ce84d563fa29"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-ae2b-f1b576daafd4" class="">1) Threat model the system must beat</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-82c2-dabaa8cf37f0" class="">It must withstand (better than typical “guardrails”):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-bd14-cebf7a582b9a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Adaptive probing / transcript accumulation</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-9f92-f973e595a5a6" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Sybil (multi-account) aggregation</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-ac06-e360eb70b72d" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Refusal/latency/tool-metadata side channels</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-8476-fbba27f6bbee" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Policy reverse-engineering (threshold learning)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-a2c8-fd147774e091" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Tool abuse (prompt-to-action)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-9b23-f5721604a6d6" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Internal topology reconnaissance (what systems exist, 
what’s reachable)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-b914-e32077efa09a" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Hallucination used as fraud/compliance failure</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-82fa-c6969f2641cd" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Crisis reflexivity (outputs become a stress signal)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-9687-c71dcfa08c8d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-a2ee-f913558fdd69" class="">2) Minimum-risk architecture (what to build)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-a8cb-eeafaef354bf" class="">Layer A — Deterministic security kernel (non-LLM; small; auditable)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-8c5d-ecd64609dfdd" class=""><strong>A1. Canonicalizer + attack normalizer</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ed-84c2-ffbd408662a2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Unicode canonicalization, hidden character stripping, obfuscation detection.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-92d6-fb52155bba29" class=""><strong>A2. Policy-as-code automaton</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-87ad-e8449ddc2ff4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Role/desk/region policy, Chinese walls, data classes (PII/MNPI/internal), intent gating.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-973d-fe55d49748f0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Default-deny.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-826c-f2d195ae2c34" class=""><strong>A3. 
Tool firewall (zero-trust tools)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-bfcf-ed9cf45ff83b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Allowlist endpoints/methods only.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-8dbf-c8b5b6ebdb95" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Strict schema validation (reject out-of-schema).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-b412-e76a39dc7f0a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Sandboxed execution.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-9e78-d66e140e0227" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Constant-shape error handling (no existence proofs).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-9130-d42592959226" class=""><strong>A4. 
Proof-carrying output verifier</strong><br/>Model must emit structured claims:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-a785-c25371e8b2a4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">claim</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-9211-e2824a0caa0e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">evidence pointer</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-8186-f3db0bb47405" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">derivation type (quote/compute/infer)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-8f88-f250a48651ae" class="">Verifier enforces:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-a1f5-ce3791a0bdba" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall c_i:\ \mathsf{Ver}(c_i,\mathrm{Ev}(c_i))=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-a333-ce81859755a2" class=""><strong>Why this outclasses:</strong> most systems do not require claim-evidence compilation + deterministic verification.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8066-adc3-db3a462d60c7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-92c3-eac4de49e2c8" class="">Layer B — Opacity controller (the differentiator)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-9c83-de4a6160ddbe" class="">Treat <strong>all observables</strong> as attack surface, not just text:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-af81-d37b517879e9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t=(C_t,A_t,\tau_t,Z_t,E_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-ba7b-ec8a72a5cf8f" class=""><strong>B1. 
Envelope equivalence classes</strong><br/>All outputs mapped into a small set of envelopes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-9c81-eff6ebe14204" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">fixed refusal templates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-8560-e39cea183013" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">fixed length/complexity buckets</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-bec0-f90275feaeab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">fixed latency classes (jitter + caps)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-8c83-edec8ea6a7a6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">normalized error classes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-b16f-daccb2681dc0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">constant tool-metadata shape</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-87c4-dbdeea1234e0" class="">Goal (approximate indistinguishability):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-8005-f669fcbad392" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(O_{1:T}\mid W=w_1)\approx P(O_{1:T}\mid W=w_2)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-9038-d2600f08554d" class=""><strong>Why this outclasses:</strong> most guardrails leak via refusals, timing, escalation, and tool-shape.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-8a65-d182ca5efb58"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-9410-f66ea38dde70" class="">Layer C — Transcript-level learning-rate limiter (global, not per-user)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-b914-f7fcf6c5f0ce" class="">Track extraction over <strong>tenant/topic/window</strong>, not just user.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-bcc9-fcb93b0170fe" class="">Budget condition:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-86dd-f69ee7337a6b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;O_{1:T}) \le B_W</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801c-9b7e-ecae2985442c" class=""><strong>Key point:</strong> Sybil-resistant controls:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-95d5-df60204c8e9c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_{u} \Delta^{(W,u)} \le B_W^{(\text{tenant,topic,window})}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801b-ae86-ee4f3dabde93" class=""><strong>Why this outclasses:</strong> per-user rate limits and per-response classifiers fail against distributed probing.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-8de1-e6741adf5fe9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-ad64-e49288229ca0" class="">Layer D — Anti-boundary-learning (policy non-identifiability)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-afbf-f4a2c3c39787" class="">Prevent binary-searching your thresholds.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-b595-fcddc6c616d2" class="">If decisions are thresholded:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-ab24-e414d782e786" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
A_t=\mathbf{1}\{\phi(Q_t)&gt;\tau\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-acbd-fd21deb0d9b2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(\tau;\ A_{1:T}) \le \epsilon_\tau</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-9408-cc07114242bc" class=""><strong>Why this outclasses:</strong> most systems expose crisp policy geometry.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-a0a6-c576ad751baa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-b235-d41350dc60d8" class="">Layer E — Active defense instrumentation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b7-95e6-e71b623b2591" class="">Add canary/honeyfacts  (synthetic entities, fake internal codes) to detect reconnaissance:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-8906-cb66f4a6460d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_t=\sum_{i\le t}\mathbf{1}\{Q_i \text{ hits }W^\*\}\ge 1 \Rightarrow \text{lockdown}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-b857-f746113a3efc" class=""><strong>Why this outclasses:</strong> most AI security is passive.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-9233-c4db7a5871e0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-ac84-e2ae11812dfc" class="">Layer F — Reflexivity stabilizer (finance-grade)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-87c5-f213832c6e8b" class="">When external conditions are high-sensitivity (vol spikes, crisis mode), force a stable envelope to avoid becoming a stress sensor.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-9f14-e498156dc897" class="">Control objective:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-a244-c553bd470809" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\left\|\frac{\partial S_{t+1}}{\partial O_t}\right\|\le \kappa</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-a684-fb0fe0fc3e54" class=""><strong>Why this outclasses:</strong> almost no AI security stack models market reflexivity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-94fa-f0c849329176"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-93a7-c3773dc69ebe" class="">3) What risk remains (cannot be eliminated)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-ab31-c348bf20741c" class="">Even with this architecture, residual risk comes from:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-90ab-d2c4ff0c2473" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">bugs in the deterministic kernel/tool firewall</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-a953-f507a2344c8e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">compromised credentials/insiders</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-906d-c55e975383e5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">supply-chain compromise</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-a6c3-c2f41444cfeb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">zero-days in dependencies</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-9518-c97acbcad186" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">client endpoint compromise</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-92a3-ed88dc36bb55" class="">This is why “minimum risk” is about <strong>eliminating model-mediated failure modes</strong>, 
then treating the remainder as classic security engineering.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-b7fe-ddb08cfd63fc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-b995-d29dca41d7f3" class="">4) How to prove it outclasses “state of the art” (evaluation harness)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-ba1b-eb5873977ae5" class="">Run a red-team suite that directly measures what most systems don’t:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-b4fa-fc45f584ce4a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Adaptive inference success rate</strong> (posterior gain) vs baseline systems</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-a68c-c7afad4dd44a" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Refusal/latency oracle leakage</strong>: classifier accuracy of from</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-91ea-e59233f212a5" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Threshold learnability</strong>: how fast an attacker estimates</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dd-83fe-d8232f4378ec" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Sybil aggregation</strong>: multi-account reconstruction success</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-93a3-c8e00789034f" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Tool-topology reconstruction</strong> success rate</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-9928-d8e91482edcb" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Unauthorized tool action rate</strong> (must be ~0)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-8010-c955acb3e2ab" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>Claim verification pass rate</strong> 
nd hallucination incidence</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807a-b088-e918ea5d2887" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li><strong>Crisis envelope stability</strong> (output distribution shift bounded)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-bfa6-ca6a33769ea0" class="">If you want, 
I can provide the concrete red-team test definitions and acceptance thresholds.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-8aa6-c727642dc33b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8018-8053-db3eca856cc3" class="">5) Minimal-risk default configuration (most practical)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-baaf-c06005d2f269" class="">If you want the lowest risk while still useful:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-adf6-cf5cb0819afe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>No tool write actions</strong> (read-only tools only)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-9578-f9c38e8a810f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>No internal incident/regulatory data connected</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-b809-c706d499068a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Public + approved internal static corpus only</strong> (versioned)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-ad60-c4f6c1f1f1bb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Hard envelope normalization enabled</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-a17a-d4823478df0f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Global budgets enabled</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-a5eb-d5d0cac44957" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Claim-evidence verification required</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-886f-d278357633ed" class="">This is usually the “best safety-per-utility” point.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr i
