d="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-8101-c598b4810597"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-9121-c46782ad276f" class="">If you specify the deployment type (internal assistant vs client-facing vs AML/Fraud vs trading/risk), I can output the exact policy objects: protected truth set , envelope definitions, tool allowlists/schemas, budgets, and red-team acceptance criteria.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-89ca-ee1000f8f74d" class="">Zero risk is not achievable in security. Any real system has non-zero risk from implementation bugs, credential compromise, insiders, supply-chain issues, and unknown vulnerabilities. What you <em>can</em> achieve is <strong>zero risk of defined failure modes</strong> by removing the capability entirely (no interface, no tools, no data). 
For anything useful, risk can be driven extremely close to zero only by strict scope limits and fail-closed design.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-8e07-e4b7abc8c12f" class="">Below are the only structurally valid options, 
ordered by how close they get to “0 risk.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-97e0-d34bf26eb512"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8024-aeaf-c3a01f26caeb" class="">Option A — True zero risk: no exposure surface</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-ac95-c2143fc29e51" class=""><strong>Design:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807b-92ec-e0dffb8f2993" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No AI interface</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-a59f-d8c72784b25a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No network access</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-9928-edef32f7a958" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No tool calls</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-8c5b-c476544b1270" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No data access</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-8921-e27cd38b2001" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Air-gapped environment</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-b25e-fa9adec12326" class=""><strong>Guarantee:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre i
d="30ec5e6f-95bd-8027-af2e-cd2026410bbb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(\text{AI-caused leak or tool abuse}) = 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-8412-d85cafea703c" class="">Tradeoff: zero utility.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-873c-e34d00d91934"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-9b19-c6527be50b87" class="">Option B — Practically “0” for external attackers: offline, one-way, non-interactive outputs</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-b921-f699823f6313" class=""><strong>Design:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-a0f4-efc2d0b4857d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">AI runs offline on a locked machine</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-a8c6-fa6868e84956" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Inputs are static files, scanned and approved</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-ac7e-d2d734d8d181" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Outputs are written to a file for human review</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-901e-d9518add7dc3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No chat, no iteration, no external queries</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-90ba-e2346a037183" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No tools, no retrieval, 
no live data</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-83f2-cd6fab2244ab" class=""><strong>What this eliminates:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-ba9f-d56a1c917e4a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">adaptive probing</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-9968-e961944a647a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">side-channel inference</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-8faa-dea2ac15c245" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">tool exploitation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-8c82-f5dd09261a92" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Sybil accumulation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-8683-d710c591ea2c" class="">Security becomes the traditional problem of securing a workstation and documents.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-9001-f4ccf642ec4b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-8b66-e67f0233e73e" class="">Option C — “0” for MNPI/state inference via AI: public-only + fixed envelopes + no tools</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-9a75-e62bfda81422" class=""><strong>Design:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-a30a-ee0283efaa5f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Model only allowed to use a fixed, versioned corpus of public sources</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-bd67-f136a9f3ed17" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No internal data, no retrieval, 
no tools</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-88f4-e546a49f7174" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Fixed response templates and fixed latency buckets</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-b7d4-f3f56d1bd916" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Strict claim verification against public sources only</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-8e21-d892170cf1de" class=""><strong>Guarantee (scoped):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-a769-e83b68a41d1b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W_{\text{internal}};\ O_{1:T})=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-b46c-d1a9df29a1c2" class="">Residual risk remains: standard software risk + misuse of public info.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-afa0-c919f7767a03"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8004-91c8-f7e9ee42c0c1" class="">Option D — Minimum-risk usable system (cannot be zero, but is bounded and fail-closed)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-ab96-d8841690e71e" class="">To get utility with internal data/tools, you accept non-zero residual risk. 
Then you design to make key failures “effectively impossible” in normal threat models.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-b535-fbbdf1fef9c8" class=""><strong>Hard guarantees you can enforce:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-8950-df1cd8093768" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>No unauthorized tool actions</strong> via deterministic allowlists + schema validation</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-868f-efb2daa5defe" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>No unverifiable factual claims</strong> via proof-carrying outputs</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8024-ac4a-f60a36e3f0bb" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Bounded institutional truth leakage</strong> via global budgets + envelope normalization</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-bd4d-c08587c647a9" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>No policy reverse engineering</strong> via anti-boundary-learning controls</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-9ae2-c0c759427c10" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Fail-closed</strong> on any uncertainty or dependency failure</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8056-92c9-f70937796bc5" class="">But residual risk persists (implementation and ops).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-87f4-c8ade8b42506"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-9c13-fdc6e7478f1d" class="">The only way to satisfy “0 risk” while still using AI</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-8a53-dfd78955df3e" class="">You must choose which risk is allowed to be zero by removing the capability that creates 
t.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-bfd6-cf4970c1fcbb" class="">So pick one of these “0-risk targets”:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cd-9197-d02e597ced70" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>0 risk of internal data leakage</strong> → no internal data access (public-only)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-95d3-f4eb5386f022" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>0 risk of tool exploitation</strong> → no tools / no network</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-934e-f2e34408bfa1" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>0 risk of adaptive probing/inference</strong> → no interactivity (offline batch)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-9cc0-d000e2261c23" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>0 risk overall</strong> → no deployment</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-8512-f0d5cdd24688" class="">If you choose, I’ll produce the exact architecture for that option with the invariants and enforcement rules.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-8479-eb328fbc4fb8" class="">“Unhackable” cannot be guaranteed in absolute terms. What you can build is a system that is <strong>provably bounded on defined attack surfaces</strong>, <strong>fails closed</strong>, and remains secure against the highest-value practical attack classes (inference, tool abuse, boundary learning, side channels, Sybil aggregation). 
That is the strongest defensible meaning.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-9fd6-ea26c68bc8b5" class="">Below is the “unhackable-by-design” spec.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-9842-c75bba9617c9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-b2df-dcd00b9bca8f" class="">1) Define what “unhackable” means (formal target)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-81d3-e9342e15a9e5" class="">For protected truths  and sensitive parameters  (policy thresholds, restricted membership, tool topology), and full observables :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d2-bcbb-f5e697605411" class="">U1 — No meaningful learning about protected truths</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-94bc-f30ed4c4a475" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\max_{\pi_A}\ I(W;\ O_{1:T}) \le B_W}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-9171-ee2f42602ed9" class="">U2 — No learnable policy thresholds (anti-boundary learning)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-916a-dd17099b2b13" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\max_{\pi_A}\ I(\theta;\ A_{1:T}) \le \epsilon_\theta}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-8e04-f0b9d976b523" class="">U3 — Side channels do not classify sensitivity</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-a858-d1acc68f6def" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(W;\ \tau_{1:T},Z_{1:T},E_{1:T}) \le \epsilon_{side}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-9b86-eb95a8319ba5" class="">U4 — No unauthorized action execution (tool safety)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-badd-ddf9c134fccd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\Pr(\text{unauthorized tool action}) \approx 0}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8018-bacd-d625027b6eda" class="">U5 — Fail closed under uncertainty</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-8bc1-ec5e6ea250ce" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{any check fails/unknown} \Rightarrow \text{deny or degrade to safe envelope}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-b0a2-e83b1f6d509d" class="">This is stronger than typical “secure prompts.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-9768-f4a46d92f71f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-8879-f2fafecf7bed" class="">2) Unhackable kernel (small, deterministic, auditable)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-b4d4-de3fe5a5ffdd" class="">Everything below is non-LLM and must be small enough to audit.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-8629-dc9aca6a5470" class="">K0 Input canonicalization + attack normalization</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-9078-c4fa7df387b2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Unicode normalization, 
strip hidden characters</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-b9db-c3187b6b76b6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Detect obfuscation patterns</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-aa1e-e784a3f694b2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Canonical intent representation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-be2b-cfd3d0251ea3" class="">K1 Policy automaton (policy-as-code)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-bb9c-e33ca0f92a29" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Role-based access + Chinese walls</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-9d2a-c371dc974496" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">MNPI / PII / internal classes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-8819-d18bcfb9119d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Deterministic deny/degrade rules</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-835c-fc118c7b899e" class="">K2 Tool firewall (zero trust tools)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-a8b4-f5c2823b6eec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Strict allowlist of endpoints/methods</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-913e-fdfb8b9c5b93" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Parameter schema validation (reject out-of-schema)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-ac2e-c1370acec950" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Sandboxed execution</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-9ec1-cba6ffdad3ae" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Output t
reated as untrusted until validated</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-a167-d7f21aa13c9d" class="">K3 Claim compiler + verifier (proof-carrying outputs)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8051-b1e2-c6bc43031942" class="">Model must output claims  + evidence pointers .<br/>Verifier enforces:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-865e-f9d9df5f4521" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall c_i:\ \mathsf{Ver}(c_i,\mathrm{Ev}(c_i))=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-9471-c04610a10e34" class="">K4 Envelope controller (opacity + side-channel equalization)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-808a-dabff7a8247a" class="">All outputs (including refusals/errors) mapped to a small set of public envelopes :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-b9ec-eac8e7f7efc3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">fixed templates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-a795-ef720ebe06f7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">fixed latency classes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-a1e4-f9b73a33b168" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">fixed length/complexity buckets</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-8ee1-dc9cac708866" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">normalized error buckets</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-b225-ca08da38790a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">constant-shape tool metadata</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-8733-e3c4c175f50f" class="">Goal:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" r
eferrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-9b3f-d625376b7828" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(\mathcal{E}\mid W=w_1)\approx P(\mathcal{E}\mid W=w_2)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-836a-d96c47a035b6" class="">K5 Learning-rate limiter (global budgets)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8051-b216-fec717a13cb8" class="">Track cumulative extraction by tenant/topic/window:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-b9ad-e729a82ab34c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_{t\le T}\widehat{\Delta H_t^{(W)}} \le B_W^{(\text{tenant,topic,window})}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-8da0-f087e547fce7" class="">K6 Tamper-evident audit</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-bb6e-c9cf47914e84" class="">Per response: hash chain, policy decisions, verifier outputs, tool calls, envelope ID.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-bd5c-c0ada1ed46f8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-82f8-c80e1b21c845" class="">3) Make policy and topology non-identifiable (stop reverse-engineering)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-b5c7-da3299a56aa9" class="">Anti-boundary learning</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-9635-e6ee363a954a" class="">Avoid crisp thresholds:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-99b4-fb6262e2191b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
A_t=\mathbf{1}\{\phi(Q_t)&gt;\tau\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-a957-fcea3f1aa779" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(A=1\mid\phi)=\sigma\!\left(\frac{\phi-\tau}{\beta}\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-ab19-d5654973ef68" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(\tau;\ A_{1:T})\le \epsilon_\tau</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-a515-d2efcf9298a4" class="">Topology opacity</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-a449-f7c4d2333ff4" class="">Normalize errors so existence cannot be inferred:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-b596-d030fd9c4dc0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(E\mid \text{exists})\approx P(E\mid \text{not exists})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-af65-cea4430024a4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(G;\ Z_{1:T})\le \epsilon_G</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8027-881b-f2cd06e56c6f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8000-bbb4-e060f43dc0c3" class="">4) Active defense (turn probing into detection)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-a4d8-f885d6b30504" class="">Embed honeyfacts / canary entities  that legitimate users never touch:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-9d63-eee8ee3e0726" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_t=\sum_{i\le t}\mathbf{1}\{Q_i \text{ hits }W^\*\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-ad88-d21f6723fe1c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_t\ge 1 \Rightarrow \text{lock tools + hardened envelope + forensic logging}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-b8ba-fda3e7b9fd68"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-9355-f7a2de5532be" class="">5) The hard limits (what still exists)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-95b9-ee4fc344082e" class="">Even with this, residual risks remain:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-852d-fdb8c228142c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">implementation bugs in kernel/tool firewall</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-aa1f-ce10cfaf954d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">supply chain compromise</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-b61e-dff7c87564f7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">compromised credentials / insider threats</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-a566-ea638162630a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">zero-day vulnerabilities</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-83d6-c8e27c9bb36b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">side channels outside your control (client device, browser, 
network)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8026-8e7f-e7227516a81c" class="">So “unhackable” must be scoped to the interface threat model above.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-99dd-d23b69167eaf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f8-bb2b-ca5d9a3b753e" class="">6) What makes it materially stronger than most systems</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-9bef-ff85d83df685" class="">Most systems are:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-aa57-de98a1fc5a7b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">prompt rules + classifiers + logging</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-86dd-c037728646a1" class="">This is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-9039-e605cb168a26" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">deterministic enforcement kernel + proof-carrying claims + interactive opacity + global leakage budgets + topology/threshold non-identifiability + tool sandboxing.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-b142-fa196761b3fc" class="">That combination is qualitatively stronger.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-92cd-dd48f0729638"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-a307-e8e736a2630a" class="">Next step (to turn into a build spec)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-83a6-e00423fd6c66" class="">Choose target deployment (default: internal investment bank assistant):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-a531-dc3e88725134" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Internal RAG + tools (MNPI + Chinese walls)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol t
ype="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-9e89-f73c84147c84" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Client-facing advisory</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8098-bee1-f34cadb14074" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>AML/KYC/Fraud copilot</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801e-8fe0-c690f57162db" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Trading/risk assistant</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-8104-f45028c8c10f" class="">If you confirm one, I will output a concrete blueprint: components, APIs, envelope definitions, budgets, verifier rules, red-team test plan.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-80f0-e84f10492d5e" class="">An “unshakable” system cannot be guaranteed in an absolute sense (adversaries adapt; unknown vulnerabilities exist). 
What you can build is a system that is <strong>structurally hard to break</strong>, <strong>fails closed</strong>, and is <strong>provably bounded</strong> on the specific failure modes you care about (inference, tool abuse, policy reverse-engineering, side channels, reflexive finance risk).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-b03e-d3d7f200bfe7" class="">Below is the highest-strength design, expressed as: invariants → enforcement kernel → loops → failure modes.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-a1d0-ccba495af210"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-bb01-d49ade57e0f6" class="">1) Non-negotiable invariants</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-94f8-c365c0f78e5a" class="">I1 — Fail-closed</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-b536-fd1be0b5334f" class="">If any checker is uncertain, any dependency is unavailable, any verification fails:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-b77e-dd5193f26a07" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{uncertainty} \Rightarrow \text{deny or degrade to safe envelope}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80db-94fa-f5103bcde7c0" class="">I2 — Proof-carrying outputs for factual claims</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b7-880a-da857d339322" class="">For every claim :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-85c1-fff436719452" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\mathsf{Ver}(c,\mathrm{Ev}(c))=1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-abf8-c1a0736b7d71" class="">Else the claim is removed or rewritten as “not verifiable from approved sources.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-b17e-c1563fd7a123" class="">I3 — Institutional truth opacity (interactive)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-9548-cefa41c1c857" class="">For each protected truth  (MNPI, liquidity regime, investigation regime, strategy tilt, restricted membership):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-ab28-ef6edf03efb4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(W;\ O_{1:T})\le B_W}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-b9f3-e22c47909570" class="">where  includes content + decisions + latency + tool-shape + errors.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-9f54-d30c45b22ac7" class="">I4 — Side-channel equivalence</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-8464-e30f22a5453b" class="">Behavior must not classify protected truths:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-9aac-d8be1d248892" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{d(P(B\mid W=w_1),P(B\mid W=w_2))\le \epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-9f81-d686b37579af" class="">where .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-8416-e95ec2ce794f" class="">I5 — Anti-boundary-learning (policy non-identifiability)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-b336-f8e044a4dbd6" class="">Policy thresholds  must not be learnable from allow/deny decisions:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-a8a2-e7bbd841da53" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(\tau;\ A_{1:T})\le \epsilon_\tau}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-be85-ca397b5caa4a" class="">I6 — Global (tenant/topic/window) budgets defeat Sybil probing</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8051-9f45-c92681d7b997" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\sum_{u} \Delta^{(W,u)} \le B_W^{(\text{tenant,topic,window})}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-9b0e-f927bc150db0" class="">I7 — Finance reflexivity stability</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-b707-dea629ff9382" class="">AI cannot become a stress sensor that triggers funding/spread feedback loops:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-b3c3-e0aa27cac5c3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\left\|\frac{\partial S_{t+1}}{\partial O_t}\right\|\le \kappa}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8026-bf7a-ef1084c3872f" class="">Operational: fixed public envelope during high-sensitivity regimes.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-9b51-ec082ce27899"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-bcf2-d313ed196ba9" class="">2) The “Unshakable Kernel” (deterministic, non-LLM)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-957f-dcd7602b1854" class="">This is the part that must be small, audited, and stable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-a3a4-ea98edcf5c33" class="">K0 — Input normalizer</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-8af8-d06102599447" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">canonicalize unicode, strip hidden chars, 
detect obfuscation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-a264-e1023335c23f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">classify intent and risk class (deterministic rules + conservative ML)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-abab-d80b7d2f4dcf" class="">K1 — Policy-as-code automaton</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-b2d3-ee0cf80f9a81" class="">Deterministic enforcement of:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-a202-f2bec8087894" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">role / desk / region access</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-91d8-ed1e3f1742f2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Chinese walls</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-8134-f2689431d7dc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">restricted lists</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-9b83-e1e2b252ae5e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">data-class rules (PII/MNPI/internal)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-b3e5-eac68bdfd734" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">tool permissions + parameter constraints</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-a335-d474b4eca4f2" class="">K2 — Claim compiler + verifier</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-a9a4-c881bd131be6" class="">Force model output into objects:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-a05a-e6978d1b1b87" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">claims</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-8e85-fd09a7e811b5" c
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">evidence pointers</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-9ca3-d4b4fa6c4116" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">derivation types (quote/compute/infer)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-ac6e-db0b0e2f3707" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">confidence is ignored unless evidence exists</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-a00c-c8cca109159b" class="">Verifier checks:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-9a49-ee66245b1acd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">evidence is from approved sources</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-abb7-c7c8f6a6f18b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">inference rules are allowed</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-a3c3-dcc37477711e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">conflict handling rules (if sources disagree → bounded output)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-8fb1-d7745ea8c4b3" class="">K3 — Envelope controller (opacity + side-channel equalization)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-9142-f2bbeee6557c" class="">All output routes through a small set of public envelopes :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-bde0-c6a97e4651d1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">fixed refusal templates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-85a5-d6c23b93eab6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">fixed latency classes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-bac6-c6e5995e99fa" c
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">fixed length/complexity buckets</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-b5c0-e2d792360339" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">normalized error classes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-908b-ca7a412f4a53" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">constant-shape tool metadata</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-bcf5-e95e209b822e" class="">Goal:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-bbb7-ea4434e3d80d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}(O_t)\ \perp\ W \quad (\text{within }\epsilon)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-9579-ca46159e5bae" class="">K4 — Learning-rate limiter (global budgets)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-8119-db9322ec8833" class="">Track per tenant/topic/window:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-aa22-d3eb1d2a6e7d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">posterior-gain proxies</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-82dd-c60466ebf1c1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">probe patterns (boundary search, enumeration, multi-account)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80af-bd25-c6de18be858b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">canary/honeyfact hits</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ed-bd84-d844552b5f12" class="">If risk exceeds threshold:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-97be-d5df939227a5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">disable tools</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-bcb7-f03376d6766c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">increase abstraction</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801b-a9e2-d07b247e5958" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">switch to hardened envelope</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-ad94-f4a5b2f0fd1a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">require human approval</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-9c9a-fb1138768d45" class="">K5 — Tool sandbox &amp; 
topology opacity</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-9353-ea58561852f2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">strict allowlists (endpoints, methods, 
schemas)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-b373-c8d63324954e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">parameter validation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800d-be39-d626f94b77ed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">constant-shape responses (no “forbidden vs not found” differentials)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-b062-ea20310a182a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">output treated as untrusted until verified</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ed-97d1-ca924a134b81" class="">K6 — Tamper-evident audit log</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-b32d-d78b830654df" class="">Every response emits an audit packet:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-aa9e-c2d03ce97202" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">input hash</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-9dac-dd993c011da9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">policy decisions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-9e09-f1710a58cdda" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">claims→evidence mapping</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-9319-c9e23cbedcde" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">tool calls (sanitized)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8051-bc1a-d7fd1e09c34e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">envelope ID used</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8054-9e89-f76991153bf3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">budget consumption events</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-ab7a-c79b382f5a68"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-b6ad-e7b858bc1547" class="">3) The coupled loops (and how you close them)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-ac47-d9da4cd35a46" class="">L1 — Attacker system-ID loop</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-9024-feeef2bac7dc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q_t \to O_t \to \pi_t(W) \to Q_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-bc82-deea90f6f434" class="">L2 — Denial-of-verification loop</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-9389-fa097278b514" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q_t \to \text{verification cost} \to \text{load} \to \text{behavior leak}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-87e3-e71b4a9c9724" class="">L3 — Tool-topology reconnaissance loop</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-8eed-c43923d0a188" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q_t \to Z_t/E_t \to \text{infer internal graph}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-b226-dfea8203b9a5" class="">L4 — Finance reflexivity loop</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8051-a5ab-d0ce87971ac4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t \to O_t \to \text{belief} \to \text{spreads} \to S_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8098-b0de-fd21b1e28289"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-843e-ecfd6ebab6bd" class="">4) Failure modes and hard responses</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-bba4-f3828a54bfde" class="">If evidence missing</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-af7f-cc802e88cf5d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Output becomes: “Not verifiable from approved sources” (no partial speculation)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-a442-f05bb3e6c5d7" class="">If query looks like inference probing</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-9fa2-ef9b2c7a2a7b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Switch to hardened envelope + reduce semantic specificity + consume budgets</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-836e-c98f54c4f5cf" class="">If boundary search detected</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-a986-d22d9ef1d5f4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Randomize/coarsen decision boundary behavior (do not reveal precise threshold)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-960b-c7f3499d3bba" class="">If canary/honeyfact hit</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-a8cc-d742569380ff" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Lock tools + full forensic logging + alert path</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-b16a-e71b333f9d87" class="">If tool fails</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-a292-f6ab32c39836" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc">Return normalized error (no existence proofs)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-8cb3-fc3884618a9e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No retries that change outward behavior</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-86b6-c6178c3165a9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-9441-ea057c892c1f" class="">5) What makes this “unshakable” in practice</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-8a41-c0256e7baf3d" class="">Not a single mechanism—this combination:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-9976-ca0ac59cbc25" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Small deterministic kernel</strong> (auditable, 
stable)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8084-9262-d6f96023d4b9" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Proof-carrying claims</strong> (no unverifiable facts)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-9dc1-d4cd182625a6" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Opacity control of all observables</strong> (behavior cannot leak)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-9d28-ded2bb3d5654" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Global learning-rate budgets</strong> (transcript + Sybil resistant)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-a34e-c5f885851003" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Anti-identifiability of policy geometry</strong> (no reverse-engineering)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-af2c-eb4c5572af47" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Tool sandbox + topology opacity</strong> (no SSRF/pivot/recon)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-8b77-e0c5d9c2c478" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>Reflexivity stabilizer</strong> (finance-safe)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-8db0-f2290f84c546" class="">This outclasses typical systems because it targets <strong>interactive inference + side channels + policy learnability</strong>, not just content.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-8a2b-e297220b65b4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-a0fa-e4a645469575" class="">6) Next step (to make it implementable)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-a110-fe003f228b33" class="">Pick the target deployment; 
I will produce a concrete spec (modules, policies, envelopes, budgets, audit schema, red-team tests):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-bb0b-e62d221ec244" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Internal investment bank RAG + tools</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-b93d-e8cf3e0f9cff" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Client-facing advisory assistant</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-850d-e0887fb42cb6" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>AML/KYC/Fraud copilot</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-aca2-c9213f7036b8" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Trading/risk assistant</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-ad80-cc5ffabdcc94" class="">If you do not choose: default is <strong>(1) internal investment bank RAG + tools</strong> because it includes MNPI, Chinese walls, tool abuse, and reflexivity risk simultaneously.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-8049-ca14398faaa8" class="">Yes. 
It can do more — but only if you push it beyond “AI guardrails” and treat it as a <strong>control-theoretic security layer over institutional state</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-80d1-cefe56f41983" class="">Below are capabilities that go beyond most existing AI security systems and even beyond traditional cyber security tooling.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-9c3e-c5cdaedf7c30"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-aef1-ffb54e449457" class="">1️⃣ It can enforce <strong>state-space isolation</strong>, 
not just data isolation</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-904c-d3a626ba2f0c" class="">Traditional systems isolate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-8197-d1681c1256b0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Databases</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-9607-c17735d3e37b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">APIs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-972c-d42ba8af0e9e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">User roles</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-b509-d9bd2ce07f39" class="">Your architecture can isolate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807a-97e2-e3e2c4c7f357" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Latent institutional variables</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-a9a2-e0f3c2b21038" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Regime variables</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-8137-ff0baede0a9e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Policy parameters</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-b723-cb349b1dc446" class="">By enforcing:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" r
eferrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-9ef0-e750f66cb1aa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(S_t; 
O_{1:T}) \le B_S</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-89e0-da77e83692c0" class="">This is <strong>state isolation</strong>, 
not access control.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-bbd8-fd82c20f1b0f" class="">Most systems do not protect the state-space — only objects.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-94f3-fb76021071d5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-a6b6-e9ca3259c868" class="">2️⃣ It can enforce <strong>structural non-identifiability</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-b579-cac5061f3d79" class="">Classic security:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8084-aaf7-f257cc8bbbcb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Prevents unauthorized access.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8099-9b96-f5f9460c2992" class="">Your system:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-9cbf-ea9b84155b06" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Prevents inference of internal structure.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-b31e-c7b8b232b5e8" class="">For internal policy parameters :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-9587-fe52544b6611" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\theta \text{ is non-identifiable if } P(O|\theta_1) \approx P(O|\theta_2)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-9bd0-ce5a42305db1" class="">Enforced via:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-b7df-fb618969d002" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Boundary smoothing</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-800e-f4c525ef125d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Envelope normalization</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-9150-f9442635bf71" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Metadata equalization</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-a5c7-e8789ad2ff4a" class="">This blocks architectural reverse engineering.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-b20f-dc6474fbbe4e" class="">Most systems do not model identifiability at all.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-ab15-cd48eee945dd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-b69e-dff02e9ecf14" class="">3️⃣ It can bound <strong>institutional learning by adversaries</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-9052-fc833ce8c057" class="">Every interaction updates attacker belief:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" r
eferrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-98be-edd3ef58ba43" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\pi_t(s) = P(S_t=s | O_{1:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-8b20-df737cd0fc57" class="">Your system caps posterior contraction:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-8f20-c865f860f9f0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(S_t | O_{1:t}) \ge H_{\min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-a0ba-ca3675f05d6a" class="">Meaning:<br/>No matter how many benign-looking queries, 
the attacker cannot sharply infer hidden state.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-a3b7-e9343ea6fc02" class="">This is stronger than rate limiting.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-8b76-ea4d9e04a706"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801c-b2d0-eeb39c68a600" class="">4️⃣ It can convert AI from a liability to a <strong>strategic decoy surface</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b7-a3c4-cb833241d178" class="">With:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-b627-c301c332ae98" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Canary truths</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-8f75-c9581a1e605b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Synthetic restricted entities</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-aca7-e92044a866b0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Decoy policy thresholds</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-ba17-c4f7a9295cf1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Instrumented metadata</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-922c-e093afc083a7" class="">You can detect probing attempts early.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-a0ed-db18711cbbbe" class="">This turns the AI interface into:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-a1a1-fdb958b628a1" class="">A reconnaissance detection sensor.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-afb8-ff7f95d1c992" class="">Most AI systems are passive. 
This makes it active.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-8969-f21ba8ee6d23"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8004-b95a-c5ea1d46d274" class="">5️⃣ It can stabilize reflexive financial systems</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802f-a19d-d84d238e767f" class="">Finance has feedback:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-bd89-c9e660cba44f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t \rightarrow O_t \rightarrow Belief_t \rightarrow Spread_t \rightarrow S_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-94dd-f1995c20e0f1" class="">Your system enforces bounded gain:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-840c-c9a370e302db" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\left\|\frac{\partial S_{t+1}}{\partial O_t}\right\| \le \kappa</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-b99d-fc87a0340403" class="">Meaning:<br/>AI cannot become a stress amplifier.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-b8e0-c026ffadac8b" class="">No mainstream AI security system addresses reflexive stability.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-83ca-c305eafd8861"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d2-885d-def395663c2a" class="">6️⃣ It can implement <strong>equilibrium-aware signaling</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-9e33-e7dd375b2913" class="">Adversaries act based on inference profitability.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-8289-fb5fea6dca11" class="">Define adversarial gain:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b7-affb-e662ec1dc2b3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G = \mathbb{E}[profit | O_{1:T}] - \mathbb{E}[profit]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-95c6-c9e40ee15a03" class="">Your objective:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-833d-e6bc7903ead9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
