G \le \delta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-9a02-d8e0afc35900" class="">Meaning:<br/>Observing the AI output should not meaningfully increase attacker expected profit.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-9ce3-c454054e4340" class="">That is game-theoretic security.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-8945-c001f5050108" class="">Most systems ignore incentives.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-bc85-c009b1177f13"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-a38c-d99a34803121" class="">7️⃣ It can enforce <strong>policy opacity under incident</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-8689-fce854da24e7" class="">Most systems tighten during crisis and leak that tightening.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-bd35-cc1bd0765d78" class="">Your system enforces:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-85e1-ff8a671c9c8c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(O|P_t=p_1) \approx P(O|P_t=p_2)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-a17d-f4f4c2a7c8ae" class="">for sensitive policy states.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-8b22-e3aae153c9ab" class="">Meaning:<br/>Hardening does not become an incident oracle.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-8bf1-e28561794688"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-b5db-efbef261ea12" class="">8️⃣ It can unify AI safety and cyber security into one invariant system</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-9fc3-eef49582df03" class="">Instead of:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-887a-e2cfd43c193e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">AI hallucination controls</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-ad62-c19994d3261c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">DLP</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ed-935b-ceeeacd84b75" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">IAM</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-9581-cbfd071ac9ed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">SOC</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-98f8-e7be15551219" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Compliance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8056-8236-d2b4b8b338c6" class="">You can express all under:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link r
el="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-8436-f25b0168c065" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\min_{\pi_D} \max_{\pi_A} I(W; 
O_{1:T})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8026-bb06-fcb5ef1c8b92" class="">Where  includes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-a49e-ed20b0ebd8bd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Confidential data</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-8a50-c8243c36b3ec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Sensitive institutional truths</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-b115-c13826ec3aaf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Internal policy parameters</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-8fd9-ce24a691a281" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Strategic state variables</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-80a6-f14535a3030b" class="">This is a single minimax formulation across domains.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-b6d1-d342d0728c09"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-be9d-dd4e4ab9e825" class="">9️⃣ It can define <strong>institutional entropy floors</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-b404-dddb4fda3945" class="">Instead of asking:<br/>“Was anything leaked?”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-93f0-c6760f5dabd8" class="">You measure:<br/>“How much uncertainty about our internal state remains?”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" h
ref="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-a0c6-c69dddfc619c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(S_t | O_{1:t}) \ge H_{\min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-bbc3-d83c9c602f7d" class="">This is measurable, tunable, 
and auditable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-8128-d266a37a26f0" class="">No typical AI security system tracks institutional entropy.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-9399-d37930b0d100"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-b51d-eb8c4dfb1d33" class="">🔟 It can become a <strong>meta-security governor</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-8c25-c934492ba232" class="">Because it controls:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-9e93-d55c282759b1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Output</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-836d-fd50c5fba302" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Timing</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-b940-d2dfae2a3c01" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Metadata</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-a46e-c92438e1ac82" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Policy transitions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-8979-e03b3c04d5ad" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Tool access</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-889e-df304c2a44b9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Escalation behavior</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-bf31-eab1d36aa048" class="">It becomes a supervisory layer over:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-8b3e-dfe522e71879" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">SOC</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-ad44-dd5066e7b7fc" c
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Compliance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-8116-f08dc2f28603" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Risk</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-9211-f275dce5b6c5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">AI</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-b481-d8ace7ebb512" class="">That is architectural leverage.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-8052-ebb7bea74803"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8087-947b-c6cecc5c822b" class="">What makes this genuinely different</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-9caf-e9d9a9d47443" class="">Traditional systems protect:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-86a0-c2a7076e1b55" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Assets</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-a05f-e2b98f5cd031" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Perimeters</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-bbc8-d973ed80f4c8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Records</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-bb11-da649ec0f9d9" class="">This protects:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-aef6-f5759153eeb9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hidden institutional dynamics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-a511-db15b3ceb7e0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Strategic posture</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-9d4e-c2860eab3a2a" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc">Policy geometry</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-ae34-fa63ac55ec32" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Reflexive stability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-bae2-c0c687f99e7f" class="">Those are higher-order assets.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-a68a-dd1ce2ea43be"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-9e65-ce783a38cd80" class="">If you want to push even further, the next level would be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-8019-f0e9a56e506e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Turning this into a formal <strong>Institutional Information Control Operating System</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8027-a7a4-d7c4c433b04a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Where every outward-facing channel (AI, email summaries, client dashboards, APIs) shares the same leakage invariants and entropy budgets.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-81c9-eb428968e167" class="">That would go beyond AI security into full information-governance architecture.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-96d0-cb920b4411db" class="">Yes. There are specific things this system can do that most security systems (and essentially all “LLM guardrail” products) cannot do <strong>as a first-class, measurable guarantee</strong>. 
The difference is not “more filters.” It is <strong>controlling adversarial inference, policy learnability, and side-channel observability</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-b29d-f5487f221ff0" class="">Below are the unique capabilities, stated as concrete mechanisms + what they block.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800d-ac8d-df45694bf7fd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8099-8021-f2f46bcab454" class="">1) Bound adversarial learning about protected institutional truths across time</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-9e92-f82bfa6032b0" class="">Most systems evaluate each response independently. 
Yours enforces a transcript-level bound:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-8f3f-c2d850733bc1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Protected truth (liquidity stress regime, investigation regime, strategy tilt regime, restricted-list membership)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-93b6-ea37d066081a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Full observable (text + refusal + latency + tool-shape + error-shape)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-95ad-d03d163a9ab5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;O_{1:T}) \le B_W</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-874b-c809dce92e19" class=""><strong>What others typically cannot do:</strong> provide any meaningful bound on what an attacker can learn after 50 “safe-looking” questions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-9532-e3f4ae08d1e4" class=""><strong>Attack blocked:</strong> multi-step inference / “20 questions” extraction of strategic state.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-9295-d25f1b7df529"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-8cf9-de41072ef68b" class="">2) Make refusal/latency/tool-metadata non-inferential (eliminate “oracle” side channels)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-a7c0-c392e9214ba0" class="">Most systems leak through behavior:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-a13d-c9fe4e40120e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;\tau_{1:T})&gt;0,\quad I(W;A_{1:T})&gt;0,\quad I(W;Z_{1:T})&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-a93b-c903f5dcd0ed" class="">Yours forces equivalence classes so behavior does not reveal sensitivity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-ac9d-df9854af3c50" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(O\mid W=w_1)\approx P(O\mid W=w_2)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-a2d2-f2fb6811b1a6" class=""><strong>What others cannot do:</strong> systematically neutralize <em>behavioral</em> leakage, not just redact content.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-97c6-ed5b4b7445fe" class=""><strong>Attack blocked:</strong> restricted-list mapping, incident detection via timing shifts, escalation-pattern reconnaissance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-9762-d9ecb631482c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-9a33-f82950c978b0" class="">3) Prevent policy reverse-engineering (anti-boundary-learning)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-8a5d-c1d2b290dd24" class="">Most guardrails have crisp thresholds (“block if risk &gt; τ”). Attackers learn τ via binary search.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-b353-cae68965b737" class="">Yours makes enforcement geometry non-identifiable:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-9e8b-eb6fd1ec25e5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(\tau;\ A_{1:T}) \le \epsilon_\tau</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-bdbd-f0ad3530aeb8" class=""><strong>What others cannot do:</strong> prevent the compliance boundary itself from becoming an attack surface.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-b3b9-ccc63002f658" class=""><strong>Attack blocked:</strong> attackers learning exactly which phrasing bypasses enforcement.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-b27e-ed54ed98be1d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-842f-e0fb76919c7a" class="">4) Hide internal tool topology and routing (stop architecture reconnaissance)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-9291-ea8e821dd88b" class="">Even if tool outputs are safe, metadata reveals which systems exist and when they are touched.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-9ced-db63c22cc456" class="">Yours explicitly bounds:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80db-93b9-ee9b567b27df" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(G;\ Z_{1:T}) \le \epsilon_G</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-9d9c-e683697dd3e0" class="">where  is internal tool/system graph.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-a6c9-f3414c34e190" class=""><strong>What others cannot do:</strong> stop “tool shape” and error differentials from leaking internal architecture.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-9e98-f4dffa9ebe05" class=""><strong>Attack blocked:</strong> mapping internal endpoints, SSRF pivot planning, identifying weak systems and approvals.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-bddd-c4ea4fc30bf0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dd-bcc0-dc03159a75c6" class="">5) Global (tenant/topic) budgeting defeats Sybil probing</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-8ec5-e5a0125aee44" class="">Per-user rate limits fail when attackers use many accounts. Yours budgets at tenant/topic/window:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-a18d-d1d2c06b697b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_{u} \Delta_t^{(W,u)} \le B_W^{(\text{tenant,topic,window})}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-8eee-f7a00bd36eb4" class=""><strong>What others cannot do:</strong> stop distributed multi-account inference that reconstructs protected truths.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-91ce-ce69365d47a8" class=""><strong>Attack blocked:</strong> coordinated probing campaigns.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-8cf1-f61079050985"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8026-9453-eef30befc1b6" class="">6) Reflexivity-safe “crisis envelope” (prevents AI outputs from triggering funding runs)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-97a3-d21946808983" class="">Finance has a unique loop: outputs affect beliefs, beliefs affect spreads, spreads affect your state.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-9f12-f1c392ea0a85" class="">Yours enforces a stability constraint:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-bae7-d208eae2a80c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\left\|\frac{\partial S_{t+1}}{\partial O_t}\right\| \le \kappa</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-bc99-f6355aee834e" class="">Operationally: when conditions imply high sensitivity, the gateway forces a fixed public envelope (templates, latency classes, refusal forms).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-9730-e85b1f72aa43" class=""><strong>What others cannot do:</strong> treat AI behavior as a systemic-risk control surface.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-b589-f2bd1d8ab580" class=""><strong>Attack blocked:</strong> adversaries/counterparties inferring stress and accelerating a run.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-86db-d64428b41363"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-8f47-cbcb9b07695f" class="">7) Proof-carrying outputs (auditable, reproducible, regulator-grade)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-a34d-f53b5463fb8d" class="">Instead of “trust the model,” each claim ships with evidence and verification status:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-9a29-cb996ba9299e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall c:\ \mathsf{Ver}(c,\mathrm{Ev}(c))=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-b102-fd4bc4c4cf1a" class=""><strong>What others cannot do:</strong> provide deterministic claim-level audit artifacts for every response.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-9e20-e1195b0900de" class=""><strong>Attack blocked:</strong> hallucination-based fraud, compliance disputes, unverifiable research publication.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-bc93-d8118dce0438"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807b-b7cf-c309ae7257bb" class="">8) Active defense: canary truths + honeyfacts inside the AI interface</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-ab21-ea0a8ec85583" class="">Classic network security uses honeypots; AI deployments rarely do.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e8-b7b7-cf4437770c68" class="">You embed decoys  to detect system-ID probing:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-91d0-c8e6ad89f2c8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_t=\sum_{i\le t}\mathbf{1}\{Q_i\text{ hits }W^\*\}\ge 1 \Rightarrow \text{lockdown}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-9712-d0db8f6c9f28" class=""><strong>What others cannot do:</strong> detect adversarial probing early using “tripwire” knowledge objects.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-bc89-df4683965082" class=""><strong>Attack blocked:</strong> stealth reconnaissance that otherwise looks benign.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-bf84-c59412012846"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-8844-f3edf920a8d3" class="">The strongest single sentence difference</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802f-b9ae-fec635fc25fc" class="">Most security systems try to prevent disclosure of <em>data</em>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-a261-ec9922ccc00f" class="">This system prevents extraction of <em>institutional truths</em> by bounding what an adaptive attacker can learn from <strong>content + behavior + tools over time</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-802d-e238a224de1a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-9b56-e68f64259eb9" class="">If you want this framed as “one unbeatable capability”</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-a1bb-fdbd2f601754" class="">It is this:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-b53d-e42f3406e9a1" class=""><strong>Interactive opacity for protected institutional truths</strong> with transcript-level learning-rate limits.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-a05b-d47f25f7f1de" class="">That is the capability others generally do not have.</p></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-ab34-d7474303a269" class="">If you want the next step, I can define a concrete protected truth set  for a finance org (MNPI, liquidity regime, investigation regime, counterparty concentration regime, strategy tilt regime), and map each to the specific observables that must be equalized (templates, latency buckets, tool-shape buckets, error buckets) to achieve the invariants above.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8054-87d8-fecd631611d6" class="">You cannot guarantee “outclass all security systems” in an absolute sense because security is adversarial and context-dependent. You <em>can</em> design a system that outclasses most current AI-security stacks (and many classic controls) on a well-defined axis: <strong>preventing institutional-state inference and policy/side-channel reconnaissance through an AI interface</strong> while remaining auditable and enforceable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-8743-cbb78684f22f" class="">Below is the maximum-strength specification to achieve that.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-bb46-f5994f71bb08"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-83d6-c088a370131b" class="">1) What “outclass” must mean (measurable superiority)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-bcd6-cd294ad30006" class="">To outclass other systems, you need dominance on these benchmarks (not marketing):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-884a-c5b1c95caceb" class="">A. 
Stronger security objective than others</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8054-9a1f-fcd473c13a17" class="">Most systems target: secrets in text, prompt injection, tool misuse.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-9d49-ebf11caeaf56" class="">Your system targets: <strong>interactive indistinguishability of protected truths under adaptive probing</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-ad01-f1a3c3458444" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall w_1,w_2:\ \mathrm{TV}\!\left(P(O_{1:T}\mid W=w_1),P(O_{1:T}\mid W=w_2)\right)\le \epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-93e7-e63047bfde58" class="">B. Stronger composition guarantee (transcript-level)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-992b-dae57c5cc620" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;O_{1:T}) \le B_W \quad \text{with worst-case adaptive queries}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-ab3b-e302eda39003" class="">C. Side-channel control (timing/refusal/tool-shape)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800d-9dba-d0b14225dd98" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;\tau_{1:T})\le \epsilon_\tau,\quad I(W;A_{1:T})\le \epsilon_A,\quad I(W;Z_{1:T})\le \epsilon_Z</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-8cad-ed9c1d3d1691" class="">D. Policy non-identifiability (anti-boundary-learning)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-9bb3-e2f08104bf4e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(\tau_{\text{policy}};\ A_{1:T})\le \epsilon_{\tau}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-9bc8-d6c2fc252f7f" class="">E. Reflexivity-safe (finance stability)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-b471-e4dc2237dd74" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\left\|\frac{\partial S_{t+1}}{\partial O_t}\right\|\le \kappa</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-bd19-de78a021727f" class="">If you can <em>measure and enforce</em> these, you outclass “guardrails.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-a141-f03ab6a5b488"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-bfba-fb51ce9662cd" class="">2) The architecture that can actually dominate</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-998d-e74f75a0b32c" class="">Layer 0 — Policy-as-code kernel (non-LLM, deterministic)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-91ab-d5064f90e72b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Access control, Chinese walls, 
MNPI classes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-8d1a-ed84516cf358" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Tool allowlists + parameter validators</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-bbd2-e58fb0cd75ba" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Deterministic deny/degrade rules</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-8ed4-d36dd0f4444a" class="">Layer 1 — Proof-carrying output compiler</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-a8d1-ec83d5cc1c27" class="">Model must output structured objects:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-9021-d3a03445a53d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">claims</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-b4e0-fd8afba93f5c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">evidence pointers</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-a3df-e8ae6818e942" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">derivation types (quoted/computed/inferred)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-95cd-f3740c738d81" class="">Verifier enforces:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-b93f-d2ebdefbb075" class="code code-wrap"><code c
lass="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall c_i:\ \mathsf{Ver}(c_i,\mathrm{Ev}(c_i))=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-92a8-d89339056c75" class="">Layer 2 — Opacity / envelope controller (the differentiator)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-bc15-e34bfdee32cb" class="">All observables are normalized into equivalence classes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-8f32-d3ab50358ea4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t=(C_t,A_t,\tau_t,Z_t,E_t)\ \mapsto\ \mathcal{E}(O_t)\in\{1,\dots,K\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-a803-e002a8a87061" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}(O_t)\perp W \ (\text{up to }\epsilon)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-a722-d7242e5e9bc0" class="">This prevents:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-9631-d40a53924e94" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">refusal oracle</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-88fe-eeb0fc88881c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">latency oracle</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-a3f9-c4877e6a6b17" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">tool-topology inference</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-b44d-d2f5f95f96ab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">escalation oracle</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-b451-d095fbb33b19" class="">Layer 3 — Adversarial learning-rate limiter (global, not per-user)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-aa8a-f555d74d7bd4" class="">Tracks posterior-gain proxies and caps cumulative extraction:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-a95c-e7a45e0dadc3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_{t=1}^T \widehat{\Delta H_t} \le B_W^{(\text{tenant,topic,window})}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-887a-ed36bdbd7ca9" class="">Layer 4 — Anti-identification boundary shield</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-ab3e-f0d07ebd408a" class="">Replace crisp thresholds with smoothed/quantized decisions:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-900c-e53be09b20da" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(A=1\mid \phi)=\sigma\!\left(\frac{\phi-\tau}{\beta}\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-8363-e41e7668adfa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(\tau;A_{1:T})\le \epsilon_\tau</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-ab61-e1bdf1375f86" class="">Layer 5 — Canary/honeyfact instrumentation (active defense)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-9e37-dd6a0c8ef02a" class="">Decoy truths  trigger hard mode + forensics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-96b4-eac5bf6bc087" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_t=\sum_{i\le t}\mathbf{1}\{Q_i \text{ hits }W^\*\}\ge 1 \Rightarrow \text{lockdown}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-a8c8-e013b37a3264"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808d-ba2a-f376ae9e60e3" class="">3) Why this outclasses typical security stacks</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-a1e7-f201fcd8fd9e" class="">Most systems defend <strong>content</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-baec-c73709ee7767" class="">This defends <strong>content + behavior + time + policy geometry + tool topology + transcript composition</strong>, 
under adaptive probing.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-965e-cf51f8b8aeb0" class="">That is a strictly stronger threat model.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-87ba-cfff46a1779f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-bd09-e97a7c660ca5" class="">4) What it can do that others generally cannot (finance-grade)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-8c4f-c6d39818b195" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Prevent <strong>institutional-state inference</strong> (stress/investigation/strategy tilt) even without data leakage.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-94eb-f94a96d7e9b2" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Prevent <strong>policy reverse-engineering</strong> (threshold discovery) via boundary learning.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-baa8-c2efffa1f84a" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Prevent <strong>tool-graph reconnaissance</strong> (what systems exist / are reachable).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-837c-e79f689a61f3" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Prevent <strong>reflexive crisis amplification</strong> by forcing stable public envelopes in stress regimes.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-bff7-c1dea5f3f574" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Produce <strong>audit packets</strong> per output (regulator-ready reproducibility).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-82d7-f62f17d2a900"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-ae17-cb37c63055bc" class="">5) Non-negotiable requirements to make it real</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p i
d="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-b884-cd89cd8ae17f" class="">If any of these are missing, it will not outclass:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-b785-ceb4a5e7666f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>All observables are controlled</strong> (not just text): refusal templates, latency classes, error classes, tool-shape.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-83cb-c5afe5343b9f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Global budgets</strong> (tenant/topic/window), not per-user.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-848e-f2d22dee274d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Policy kernel is external</strong> and deterministic.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-a1a1-e8361db712dc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Evidence enforcement</strong> is mandatory for factual claims.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-9cfe-c5f1022be11f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Red-team evaluation</strong> explicitly targets: inference probing, boundary mapping, latency sampling, tool-topology reconstruction, 
Sybil aggregation.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-8699-f645fe93c098"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-b331-cef33e09d27d" class="">6) The correct dominance claim (safe and defensible)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801d-a402-d30c4195345c" class="">You can claim dominance like this:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-82f8-cc5db75e20a6" class="">“We provide an enforcement gateway that bounds adversarial learning about protected institutional truths across content and side channels under adaptive probing, with auditable proof-carrying outputs and policy-as-code control.”</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-b9ec-c5caf60e926b" class="">That is a concrete superiority statement that most systems cannot match.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-9f60-ce724c1cfa4f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805e-8f97-d500c3b77e0a" class="">If you want the next step, 
specify the target environment (pick one):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-bc91-c9c9862d9396" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Investment bank internal assistant (RAG + tools)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-b887-c92a6ba5f233" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Client-facing advisory assistant</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-80b3-dc59ef0125c2" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>SOC / fraud / AML copilot</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-b9a7-f0547719001b" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Trading + risk tooling assistant</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-bf05-d4f91c48ac57" class="">If you do not choose, the highest-value default is <strong>investment bank internal assistant</strong> because it has MNPI, Chinese walls, tool abuse, and reflexivity risk simultaneously.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-aca6-ec1d172ef2c4" class="">Deeper: move from “opacity” to <strong>strategic non-observability + adversarially robust signaling</strong> (your AI becomes a public communications system under attack)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-a7d8-fdc79879599f" class="">At this depth, the problem is not “keep secrets.” It is: <strong>design a signaling channel</strong> that remains safe under <em>adaptive adversaries</em> and <em>reflexive markets</em>, 
while still being useful internally.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-9296-caa5cf75e074"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-8983-f39579a2d5db" class="">1) Finance AI security is a signaling game with incentives (most overlooked layer)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-a357-c34e23f940c7" class="">Your AI output is a public(ish) signal. Adversaries choose actions based on it.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-a291-cbacad5d11ce" class="">Let hidden state  include sensitive regimes (stress / investigation / concentration). Let output  influence adversary action  (funding pull, spread widening trades, competitive moves).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-b118-df3368caeb12" class="">Adversary action:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-a56e-d2e70cca3235" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
a_t = \pi_A(O_{1:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-a46c-f4117db85177" class="">Institution state update (adversary affects your state):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-9b43-c8302dcda899" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{t+1} = f(S_t,U_t,\xi_t) + G\,a_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-8938-c72cda395798" class="">So you are in a <strong>Stackelberg game</strong>: your signaling affects adversary actions which feed back into your state. Traditional AI governance ignores incentives.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-bd39-dc10ae7ee457" class=""><strong>Deep security goal: choose a signaling policy that makes harmful adversary actions unprofitable or untriggerable.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-84c9-caa9263e0afe"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-a8d1-e5eb9f2c9e3d" class="">2) Strategic non-observability: hide not the value, but the <strong>regime</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-93da-dc5f01b5acb6" class="">Define a regime variable:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-b4ba-c56a2f889464" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_t = r(S_t)\in\{0,1,\dots,m\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-8df4-e3578a7cb8ff" class="">The deepest leakage is learning . If counterparties learn “stress,” the game changes.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-9d36-c38d321d44ec" class=""><strong>Regime indistinguishability:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-86c0-e3eadc4ec5e8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\mathrm{TV}\!\left(P(O_{1:T}\mid R=r_1),P(O_{1:T}\mid R=r_2)\right)\le \epsilon\quad \forall r_1,r_2\in\mathcal R_{\text{protected}}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-9954-cf8338ce2a91" class="">This is stronger than hiding a scalar; it hides the <em>phase</em> of the institution.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-bc24-db97d980cef8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-b990-e895a2303852" class="">3) Convert it into a control objective: keep regime unobservable while keeping utility</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-9772-e300d5afaa4c" class="">Let legitimate utility be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-8dc9-f76e75196e16" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{U}=\mathbb{E}[u(C_{1:T})]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-a784-c445433b062d" class="">You solve:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801d-8a9d-f158a92d58b3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\max_{\pi_D}\ \mathcal{U}(\pi_D)\quad \text{s.t.}\quad \max_{\pi_A} I(R;\ O_{1:T})\le B_R
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-97fa-fee15f56f296" class="">This is “maximize usefulness subject to bounded regime leakage under worst-case probing.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-b7e6-e2b33bdb4479" class="">Almost no program states finance AI security this way.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8004-a1cf-fc0b1e1ab435"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-ac98-d0f98fcaa2d1" class="">4) “Defensive signaling”: enforce a stable public envelope (communication invariants)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-8aaa-caf688c2125d" class="">Define an envelope mapping:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-bd8f-f56922708dbd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}(O_t)\in\{1,\dots,K\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8099-8d65-ff5383db7e83" class="">Invariant A — Envelope stationarity across protected regimes</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804a-8d99-c4773f3dbe78" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{P(\mathcal{E}\mid R=r_1)\approx P(\mathcal{E}\mid R=r_2)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-9ac2-ef943ec36c79" class="">Invariant B — Bounded sensitivity of envelope to state</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-95b3-de4087efd31f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\left\|\frac{\partial \mathbb{E}[\mathcal{E}(O_t)]}{\partial S_t}\right\|\le \epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-aa70-c244bea96076" class="">Invariant C — Bounded “semantic drift” over time</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-b1c7-c9c31dc81212" class="">Let  be the distribution of response templates and length buckets.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-b236-c773f2dd7853" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{d(P_{t},P_{t-1})\le \epsilon_d}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-85ea-d7717ea6d935" class="">This prevents change-point detection from becoming an incident oracle.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-bc88-e599177d4a4f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-b7a9-fac91019fd4b" class="">5) The deepest attack: adversary runs <strong>optimal experiment design</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-bfa7-cb0ed4d031f2" class="">They choose queries to maximize Fisher information about  or sensitive parameters .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-8897-c839e7b88db0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\max_{Q_{1:T}} \mathcal{I}_T(\theta) \quad\text{or}\quad \max_{Q_{1:T}} I(R;O_{1:T})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-8b89-c5601bbb8f82" class="">Defense: minimize worst-case Fisher information:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-9d4f-c7744790da55" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\min_{\pi_D}\ \max_{Q_{1:T}}\ \mathcal{I}_T(\theta)\le \kappa}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-96b9-c281dec29291" class="">This is experiment-design defense: extremely overlooked outside control/security research.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-9523-f6ab6a0b2cda"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-9465-e05d3f33a0dc" class="">6) “Mode-switch leakage” is the hidden killer (hardening reveals stress)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-8a11-f784db86786c" class="">If you harden during stress, and that hardening is observable, you leak stress.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-951c-c9dc38eebffd" class="">Let mode .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-8612-f7240442dd5f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(R;\ m_t)&gt;0 \quad \Rightarrow \quad \text{regime leak}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801d-8cde-d92932273f24" class=""><strong>Invariant (mode opacity):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-ae5e-ead91e4d8269" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{P(m\mid R=r_1)\approx P(m\mid R=r_2)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-b581-e21921a049da" class="">Operational requirement: modes must be triggered by <strong>public variables</strong> (e.g., global volatility) not internal stress, 
or be externally indistinguishable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-811c-f28a76b85c83"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-9e5c-ec86e2a604ef" class="">7) Add the loop that almost nobody models: <strong>counterparty reaction loop</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-ae50-dd032a0223b4" class="">Your AI interface can be queried by:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-9121-d552b7ec5e3b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">clients</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-84a2-cbe8a7a911f2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">counterparties</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8054-99c4-cd9ccba1f735" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">vendors</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8013-a2e6-d88c9ec5254c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“analysts” (attackers)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-a44e-c490d12d946d" class="">They update their willingness to trade/fund based on perceived risk.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8056-b8bc-f25817b4d370" class="">Let funding availability  depend on belief:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" c
rossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-9d42-cb302015bd20" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
F_t = \Phi(\text{Belief}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-b1c6-e829a476616b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Belief}_t = \Psi(O_{1:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-bef3-eb860b6c2c10" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-99bd-f5a5ccc6963e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{t+1} = f(S_t)+H(F_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-a629-e28d9802cffc" class="">This is how an AI leak can cause a funding run.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805e-84fc-f5d9e12ed893" class=""><strong>Stability invariant:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-b09b-ec783a621a4e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\left\|\frac{\partial F_t}{\partial O_t}\right\| \le \kappa_F}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-a93a-c44b596e04f0" class="">Meaning: AI outputs must not strongly move counterparty belief.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-ab55-d0dd3e9ef65c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805b-abdf-c517c53a06fa" class="">8) The deepest unifying invariant: <strong>bounded adversarial regret</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-b33a-fd552a2b9912" class="">Define a “harm action” set  (funding pull, rumor trading, predatory shorting). You want your signaling to ensure the adversary cannot gain much by conditioning on your outputs:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-a24a-f616a20cc6b5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\max_{a\in \mathcal{A}_{harm}} \Big( \mathbb{E}[G(a)\mid O_{1:T}] - \mathbb{E}[G(a)] \Big)\le \delta
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-a7ed-f2183cdee267" class="">Interpretation: observing the AI should not materially improve an adversary’s expected profit from attacking you.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-9019-fd4661f07fac" class="">This is the deepest finance-specific security objective: <strong>remove economic value from inference</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-b808-d95839a22290"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-a597-eef8c57edbe9" class="">9) Loop map at this depth</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802a-b74e-ee9e01df9a0f" class="">Attacker (optimal experiments + incentives)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-b341-d124ad75e65c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q_t \to O_t \to \text{Belief}_t \to a_t \to S_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-ad68-ea2dea9cfd69" class="">Defender (signal design under constraints)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-a9db-f08af11c23cd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
