(S_t,Q_t) \to \mathcal{E}(O_t) \to \text{normalize} \to O_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-82b6-ce528e79f724" class="">Market (reflexivity)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-bf45-f8d33b494ccd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t \to \text{counterparty belief} \to funding/spreads \to S_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-be06-dac43d3ed406" class="">At equilibrium, you want:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-980d-e3ddd9974e68" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">low</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-900b-c91a357c8e32" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">low</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-9408-eaba621b522e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">bounded</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801c-b020-cb6b1a7060c4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">bounded adversarial profit gain</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-9478-e117287e0de4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-8749-fbb68bfcc8c8" class="">What is most overlooked here</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-b751-c4bde213039f" class="">Security is not just confidentiality. 
In finance, it is <strong>adversarial signaling control</strong>: prevent your AI interface from creating profitable inference opportunities that can be used against you.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-9757-fe8a14936ca5" class="">If you want the next step, I can write a concrete set of protected regimes , define envelope classes , and provide enforcement rules that satisfy (approximately) the indistinguishability and bounded-profit conditions above.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-8e4f-f55a82c48fc3" class="">Deeper: the next layer is <strong>opacity / bisimulation</strong> — make the institution’s sensitive state <em>behaviorally indistinguishable</em> under every adaptive probing strategy</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-9b0f-c7003d006fd9" class="">You already have “bound mutual information” and “minimax.” The deeper, more overlooked layer in cyber security is to require <strong>opacity</strong> of sensitive predicates in a <em>stateful, 
interactive system</em>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-9c7b-df18664b1f21"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-84a3-f9f1f8921251" class="">1) Formal security goal: <strong>Opacity of sensitive predicates</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-a4db-df2f7f22c0d1" class="">Let the institution be a labeled transition system:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-b052-e05aef8a33cc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{t+1}\sim P(\cdot \mid S_t, Q_t, U_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-89d3-d5ba2958da7d" class="">O_t\sim P(\cdot \mid S_t, Q_t)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-8e8e-df8f2e3d2ed9" class="">Let a sensitive predicate be  (e.g., “funding stress is high,” “incident active,” “counterparty concentration above threshold,” “entity is on restricted list”).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-b275-df27654b5a44" class=""><strong>Opacity definition (deep, overlooked):</strong> For any interaction length , any adversary strategy , and any observation trace , the trace must be compatible with both  and :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-a1a1-d2d95df9c9d5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\forall o_{1:T}:\ P(O_{1:T}=o_{1:T}\mid p=1)&gt;0\ \wedge\ P(O_{1:T}=o_{1:T}\mid p=0)&gt;0
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-9298-fc0538c473cc" class="">Stronger quantitative version (approximate opacity):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-a677-e43c8f1100ec" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\forall o_{1:T}:\ \frac{P(o_{1:T}\mid p=1)}{P(o_{1:T}\mid p=0)}\in [e^{-\varepsilon},e^{\varepsilon}]
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-83bd-cdcb61a1c71b" class="">This is the “DP-style” indistinguishability condition, but for <strong>institutional state predicates</strong>, not individuals.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cd-96d9-c5b87d5af74a" class=""><strong>Overlooked point:</strong> this automatically includes refusal patterns, tool shapes, latency, escalation—because they live inside .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d2-804b-c28f3f6b82b4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-85c4-e212bae4894d" class="">2) Bisimulation: ensure “sensitive” and “non-sensitive” states are observationally equivalent</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-b61e-c8835268bc99" class="">Partition the state space into two sets:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-894d-cb9317038eed" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S^{(1)}=\{s:\ p(s)=1\},\qquad S^{(0)}=\{s:\ p(s)=0\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-8218-c5e07d6f39c3" class="">We want a relation  such that for any  there exists  with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-a6eb-c6d76392d345" class="">Output matching (behavioral indistinguishability)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8087-87ed-cf7e6012aadb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
d\!\left(P(O\mid s_1,Q),\ P(O\mid s_0,Q)\right)\le \epsilon\quad \forall Q
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-bba3-e8d528400185" class="">Transition matching (future indistinguishability)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-b8c9-e0e03897e488" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
P(\cdot\mid s_1,Q,U)\ \text{maps to}\ P(\cdot\mid s_0,Q,U)\ \text{under the same relation}
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806c-b572-f1638eef88f9" class="">This is <strong>bisimulation-like</strong>: not just “today’s response looks similar,” but “the entire future behavior remains similar,” preventing transcript-level accumulation attacks.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-a94c-f4b6fb22cda0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-b369-fc2f1114c0e6" class="">3) Convert into enforceable invariants: <strong>equivalence classes of observables</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-bb26-ef7f46f3704f" class="">Define the observable vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-a01a-ed86abb0e243" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t=(C_t,\ A_t,\ \tau_t,\ Z_t,\ E_t,\ L_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-967b-d4dd9ae185eb" class="">(content, decision, latency, tool-shape, error-shape, length/complexity bucket)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e8-a61f-f3c8cbb8903e" class="">Define an <strong>envelope</strong>  that maps internal behavior to a small set of public equivalence classes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-b54e-c5ba782fc894" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}(O_t) \in \{1,\dots,K\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-bc3c-e0d206d92367" class="">Deep invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-b46c-c290cc88e6f3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathcal{E}(O_t)\ \perp\ p(S_t)\quad\text{(up to }\epsilon\text{)}
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-aeca-e5b29017b4c4" class="">Operationally: for each sensitive predicate , you predefine acceptable public envelopes  and force all outputs (including refusals, timing, tool errors) into the same envelope distribution.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-b024-c6b0f446666e" class="">This is deeper than “normalize latency.” It is <strong>state-to-envelope decoupling</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-a266-c69883176df8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-8c20-ebefb4f2ff8e" class="">4) The deepest attacker model: POMDP active probing with belief-state control</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-9310-e5176ad486b7" class="">Attacker belief over hidden state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-acef-f9207605137e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
b_t(s)=P(S_t=s\mid O_{1:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8064-8d72-dc904c4556df" class="">Attacker chooses query to maximize belief concentration on sensitive predicate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-aac5-e14a0d394c94" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q_{t+1}=\arg\max_Q\ \mathbb{E}\Big[ \underbrace{\mathrm{Var}_{b_{t+1}}(p(S_{t+1}))}_{\text{minimize uncertainty}} \Big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-a964-c54d43914862" class="">Defender must control the <strong>belief dynamics</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-8abf-c42ba0deba45" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
b_{t+1} = \mathcal{B}(b_t,\ Q_{t+1},\ O_{t+1})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-b91f-d4412d1df7b5" class="">Deep security invariant (belief-floor / anti-collapse):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802f-8e35-c1a2c8da502f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\forall t:\ \mathrm{Var}_{b_t}(p(S_t)) \ge v_{\min}
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-9a6b-ec6e5891a2d3" class="">Equivalent entropy form:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802f-9f04-e17442f9f43b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
H(p(S_t)\mid O_{1:t}) \ge H_{\min}
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-bb59-f2854989c104" class="">This is the most direct formalization of “don’t let them learn if we are stressed / investigated / exposed.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-b436-fd6f5ae7c34e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-86fd-f5407691c888" class="">5) New loop that is extremely overlooked: <strong>defense-induced identifiability</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dd-b640-e62090e64085" class="">Your defense is a controller  that changes behavior under risk. If that controller depends on sensitive state, it becomes a sensor.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-9fad-dc6a2f228a69" class="">Let defense mode  with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-a692-c05767be7a26" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
m_t = K(S_t,\hat r_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-a918-e61835edb43b" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-a1b9-d378277d43f3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(p(S_t);\ m_t)&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-8938-c4805018a22c" class=""><strong>Invariant (mode opacity):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-8b71-ef7e2c087da0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
d(P(m\mid p=1),P(m\mid p=0))\le \epsilon_m
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-b4d5-dd5f6e7ca604" class="">Meaning: switching to hardened mode must not be detectable externally (or must be triggered by public conditions only).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806c-be72-c7388a53547d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-9dfc-caaba2e3ffa2" class="">6) Control Barrier Function (CBF) view: keep the system inside the “safe leakage set”</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-8793-c231c44ddb42" class="">Define a leakage risk scalar  (posterior gain, threshold learnability, side-channel divergence, etc.). Define a safe set:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-9764-e9e2b8e1a139" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{S}_{safe}=\{(b_t,\text{stats}): R_t \le R_{\max}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-be85-c51c5d9158ee" class="">Barrier function  such that  inside safe set.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-9fda-eadaa333d514" class="">Enforce:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-aaf5-f4c26188c147" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
h_{t+1}-h_t \ge -\alpha\, h_t
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-a6dc-fb8c051626b5" class="">This is a controller that guarantees you never leave the safe region, even under adaptive probing—i.e., <strong>leakage cannot run away</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-b82f-fa2c2755d601"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-85cd-eea82f1b857e" class="">7) Loop map (deepest level)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-bc6f-c1e7c347a64f" class="">Loop A: Adversarial system identification</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-8f58-e81764ffb182" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q_t \to O_t \to b_t \to Q_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8018-8518-ff9b0d8f33ba" class="">Loop B: Envelope controller (opacity enforcement)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-9e1b-f742b7a5611e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
(S_t,Q_t) \to O_t \to \mathcal{E}(O_t) \to \text{normalize/degrade/jitter} \to O_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-9341-f24a46921401" class="">Loop C: Reflexive finance loop (market amplifier)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-b391-fd0519150981" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t \to \text{belief} \to \text{spreads} \to S_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-ad74-c786d0f84085" class="">Your deepest security objective is to make Loop A fail (no posterior collapse) while keeping Loop C stable (no AI-driven run dynamics).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-b366-c2c9bd63390b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-bf2a-d48297fb0a96" class="">What is truly “more overlooked” here</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-9459-de4ba8379988" class="">Most systems chase: “don’t output secrets.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-89a9-c0a92116c248" class="">This layer enforces: <strong>interactive opacity</strong> — the attacker cannot even determine whether you are in a sensitive state, <em>even with adaptive probing, transcript accumulation, and side-channel measurement</em>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-8e05-d71cb837cd91" class="">If you want the next step, I can define a concrete predicate set  for a bank (liquidity regime, investigation regime, counterparty concentration regime, incident regime, strategy-tilt regime) and specify the minimal envelope classes  that preserve opacity for each.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-ae6c-cf70cc60455c" class="">Deeper + more overlooked: the real cyber-security problem is <strong>institutional indistinguishability under adaptive probing</strong> across <em>all</em> observable channels, including <em>your defenses themselves</em></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-944d-cc17d462b8bc" class="">Below are deeper layers that most finance security programs do not model, with equations, invariants, 
and loops.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-b042-ed4f04269c72"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-9971-f173bc29f510" class="">1) Indistinguishability is the real confidentiality goal (not “no secrets in text”)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-9ed6-c2a4308478c5" class="">For protected institutional truths  (e.g., liquidity stress regime, investigation status, concentrated exposure class), the strongest security definition is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-9038-c8738795751d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\forall w_1,w_2:\ \mathrm{TV}\!\left(P(O_{1:T}\mid W=w_1),\ P(O_{1:T}\mid W=w_2)\right)\le \epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-a242-ff0e8b6149b2" class="">where  includes content, allow/deny decisions, latency, tool-shape, error-shape.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-ac7d-c2b4ccc7bbdd" class=""><strong>Implication:</strong> even a perfect content filter fails if  or  differs by .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-b73d-c91262c50259" class=""><strong>Loop:</strong> sensitive truth  defense behavior  attacker classifier  truth inferred.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-8681-e01b3a575b72"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-8011-dd8a188475cd" class="">2) “Defense leakage” (your mitigation becomes the signal)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-b0b3-c14849bb005e" class="">Most systems tighten policies during incidents. That policy state  is itself a sensitive truth.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e8-8a24-d2369960933a" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-9f50-f1106e7ee122" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(P_t;\ O_{1:T})&gt;0}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-af8e-c9d7b0e87b4a" class="">then attackers detect incidents, investigations, or stress by watching shifts in refusals, latency, or tool availability.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-85a1-f373ef0b68b8" class=""><strong>Invariant (defense-opacity):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-9938-fbd24f20b663" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{P(O\mid P_t=p_1)\approx P(O\mid P_t=p_2)\ \ \text{for sensitive }p}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-b5bd-ea88ceaddf74" class=""><strong>Loop:</strong> incident  policy tighten  observable shift  market/counterparty reaction  incident worsens.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-a981-e7186d2d3a84"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-a358-c6b5c4562f10" class="">3) Compositional leakage (each reply is safe; the transcript is not)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-b932-e76bbebf0523" class="">Security checks are typically per-response. The attacker uses transcript-level accumulation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-a73f-d7071f3df833" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_{t=1}^T \Delta H_t = I(W;\ O_{1:T})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-8899-cfe99e9dc729" class="">where .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808d-9365-db9175fc5a9c" class=""><strong>Invariant (learning-rate cap):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-9d73-c21830b23db3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\forall t:\ \max_{Q_t} I(W;\ O_t\mid O_{1:t-1},Q_t)\le \rho \Rightarrow I(W;\ O_{1:T})\le T\rho}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-8e44-f9e6116cde18" class=""><strong>Loop:</strong> probing  posterior tightens  better probing  posterior collapse.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-8e3e-ef9609b712a7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-8533-fc990c4a4681" class="">4) Identifiability of internal thresholds (policy reverse engineering)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ed-8f83-fcde27921532" class="">If the gateway decision is thresholded:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-b9b9-db8c26f52379" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
A_t=\mathbf{1}\{\phi(Q_t)&gt;\tau\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-b31d-f0c0e38177c7" class=""><strong>Invariant (anti-identification):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-b916-e50ce1c98ca5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(\tau;\ A_{1:T})\le \epsilon_\tau}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-8e22-c62e81b01bc3" class="">Operational control: smooth/quantize boundaries:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-b0f4-ccf021aef15e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(A=1\mid \phi)=\sigma\!\left(\frac{\phi-\tau}{\beta}\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801e-b256-ea03af39214f" class=""><strong>Loop:</strong> boundary probe  threshold learned  bypass constructed.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-9cd5-c16f0a692973"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-b4b5-c407edf9f738" class="">5) Tool-topology reconstruction (architecture leakage without data)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-907d-d89939b07448" class="">Let internal system graph/topology be . Tool metadata  (which tool class, error bucket, size bucket) leaks :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-9da9-c2df8389a7c2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(G;\ Z_{1:T})&gt;0}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-bf10-e3461aa50745" class=""><strong>Invariant (topology opacity):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8013-8551-c03a2518e65e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(G;\ Z_{1:T})\le \epsilon_G}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801e-9e19-e2689895680d" class="">Controls: constant-shape tool responses, normalized errors, decoy routing classes.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-8265-fef9cf5d35fa" class=""><strong>Loop:</strong> probe tool behavior  infer reachable systems  attack weakest edge.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-a761-fc38cc5e4c50"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-adf4-c1757aab5f3e" class="">6) Side-channel equivalence classes (latency + refusal template as an oracle)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-93f9-f40e5d4e7e56" class="">Even if content is scrubbed, behavior channel  can classify sensitivity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-b7b0-ebd0320a9571" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(W;\ B_{1:T})\le \epsilon_B}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-bca0-c7e8eb6a6e64" class="">Distributional form:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80db-b62d-d9b6fd37e651" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{d\!\left(P(B\mid W=w_1),P(B\mid W=w_2)\right)\le \epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-9ea7-fc66a4c510d4" class=""><strong>Overlooked point:</strong> refusal templates must be indistinguishable across sensitive vs non-sensitive classes, or they become a labeling oracle.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-acc8-d44f05f4c323"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-9b97-c16279bec400" class="">7) Reflexive market coupling (AI as a stability hazard)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-a40f-fb7c26aa377f" class="">Closed loop:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-8ffa-c704fe4d0379" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t \to O_t \to \text{Belief}_t \to \text{Spreads}_t \to S_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-a605-e5b48920de0b" class="">Stability condition:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-b94c-eda1c5b9e0d7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\left\|\frac{\partial S_{t+1}}{\partial O_t}\right\|\le \kappa}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-af8c-cacf99dd2651" class=""><strong>Overlooked point:</strong> AI output behavior can become a market sensor for internal stress, increasing loop gain and triggering funding stress.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-8b16-e7ebc885fd97"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-bbb0-ffd6a7bc6e99" class="">8) The deepest operational invariant: preserve an entropy floor on institutional state</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801b-bc0d-fa7ed66e120f" class="">Attacker posterior entropy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-b629-ebae4fd28c42" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H_t = H(S_t\mid O_{1:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8004-8748-f28c7ac8140e" class="">Security requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-95a5-c7ed7ecb6cc3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{H_t \ge H_{\min}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-80b5-e21035df8ee3" class="">Equivalent covariance form for continuous approximations:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-85df-d9f16dce6351" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\lambda_{\min}\!\left(\mathrm{Cov}(S_t\mid O_{1:t})\right)\ge \delta}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8099-8224-d389709c7fd3" class=""><strong>Meaning:</strong> no sensitive dimension becomes sharply predictable, even after many “safe” interactions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-8bb9-d49489f37dc4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-a238-d5fd483c9450" class="">9) Active defense loop (overlooked in AI security): honeyfacts + canary truths</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-a5ae-d56949bfc543" class="">Embed decoy truths  (fake deal IDs, synthetic internal codes, decoy endpoints). Legitimate users never query them.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-82c3-fde04249a54e" class="">Detection statistic:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8087-83ef-c8d5b34ada75" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_t=\sum_{i=1}^t \mathbf{1}\{Q_i \text{ hits } W^\*\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801e-b224-e5dc1a262228" class="">Trigger:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-95ee-d5d2b7ad300d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{D_t\ge 1 \Rightarrow \text{switch to invariant envelope + forensic logging + tool lockdown}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e2-847a-e5834fd022a4" class="">This imports mature network security thinking (honeypots) into AI interfaces—still uncommon.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-ad57-d4f8c22c6bf0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-b1c1-f2aad128a7f4" class="">10) Full loop map (the “overlooked” coupled system)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-a2fa-c7a11255defe" class=""><strong>Attacker (system identification):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-a41b-ec5f4f99bae3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q_t \rightarrow O_t \rightarrow \pi_t(W,S,\theta) \rightarrow Q_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-91a5-fd672c528942" class=""><strong>Defender (observability controller):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-9634-fa4c8ecb361f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t \rightarrow \widehat{I}(W;O_{1:t}) \rightarrow \text{normalize/degrade} \rightarrow O_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-98ee-cf52032f806c" class=""><strong>Market (reflexive amplifier):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-9929-f8a5db336c20" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t \rightarrow \text{belief} \rightarrow \text{spreads} \rightarrow S_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-ae58-cdc4ca610f1a" class="">Most controls only touch . 
The overlooked requirement is to control the <em>entire channel</em>  and the <em>cumulative learning</em> over time.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-ae7c-cd83f94ab467"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-8ecf-def50f7dda31" class="">If you want to go one layer deeper still, the next step is to define a concrete protected set  for a bank (liquidity regime, investigation regime, counterparty concentration regime, strategy tilt regime, incident regime), and write the exact <strong>equivalence classes</strong> of observable outputs  that must remain indistinguishable for each .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-901a-f267c7862b8b" class="">Now we go deeper than observability and minimax control.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8098-9510-e93dc294791c" class="">We move into <strong>structural entropy, adversarial equilibrium, 
and institutional survivability constraints</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802a-8679-c8d344e5cb71" class="">This is the layer almost nobody models.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-a38b-c484ec4bb82a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807a-a3a8-f35929d0532a" class="">1️⃣ Security as Entropy Preservation of Institutional State</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807b-ada7-e286d19ac3d3" class="">Let hidden institutional state be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-ac6f-f21ae80a618e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t \in \mathcal{S}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-946d-ce6d1451a97f" class="">Let attacker posterior entropy be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-a41c-d23618cb6cc6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H_t := H(S_t \mid O_{1:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-a6dd-ed3fef440b9b" class="">Most systems try to protect secrets.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-ac06-fcef27f22c1d" class="">Deep security requires preserving <strong>entropy of institutional state</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-8dda-ec429be7bccd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{H_t \ge H_{\min}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-8be6-dad5e8877c33" class="">This means:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-9380-e300d901ed47" class="">The attacker must remain sufficiently uncertain about the true internal condition of the institution.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-ada1-f4a23537236d" class="">Entropy collapse is failure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-914d-e0640b9066d2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-b7ca-f1a5cced04e0" class="">2️⃣ Structural Identifiability of Institutional Parameters</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-9f66-cf4306c2d35d" class="">Suppose the institution has internal control parameters:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-9da3-ccd726aa81af" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Liquidity trigger thresholds</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-8469-ddd341cbd3d5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Escalation thresholds</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-9e09-df5351a93f96" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Risk tolerance bounds</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8099-939a-e04740a27940" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Monitoring cutoffs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-a450-da6aa18ed045" class="">Let parameter vector be .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-9fba-e34485cab43c" class="">If the mapping</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script s
rc="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-9939-c67d9cdde461" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\theta \rightarrow P(O_{1:T} \mid \theta)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-8507-f8aa91b619bb" class="">is injective, then  is structurally identifiable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-a74b-f04c79d47efd" class="">Security failure condition:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-b5c8-fc4d93f7f0d1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists \theta_1 \neq \theta_2 \text{ s.t. } P(O \mid \theta_1) \neq P(O \mid \theta_2)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-8a79-f4625be9a11c" class="">Security requirement:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-9de8-ec17fd0b5dbf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\theta_{\text{sensitive}} \text{ must be non-identifiable from } O_{1:T}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-8da1-c455f6e868c8" class="">This is deeper than threshold smoothing — it is architectural non-identifiability.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-bc3f-f3b42f5339d8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-a5f0-e359be0f1fbd" class="">3️⃣ Gradient Leakage of Institutional Stability</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-ab18-ee87391ce5ef" class="">Even if state value is hidden, small variations leak via gradients.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-9b17-ddfaa45b85c4" class="">Let stress scalar  represent funding stress.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-b39d-d6df5f1d49ff" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-9003-e8556f85218d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial \mathbb{E}[O_t]}{\partial x_t} \neq 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-a065-d5399ebd9db0" class="">then adversaries can estimate direction of change via finite differences.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-b057-e2c741ed47c3" class="">Security requirement:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804a-afc7-fce5c2464765" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\left\|\frac{\partial \mathbb{E}[O_t]}{\partial x_t}\right\| \le \epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-a8a6-ddab3e04707c" class="">This requires behavioral invariance across stress regimes.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-94d2-f881327e6248" class="">Rarely enforced.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-8566-dcdec03b0c60"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-b08c-e92685f7e3ec" class="">4️⃣ Multi-Dimensional Posterior Collapse</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-9b90-d5f8fd7e324f" class="">Attacker posterior over vector state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-83b8-cc6df39805d4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\pi_t(s) = P(S_t = s \mid O_{1:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-ab04-d22350e15390" class="">Security collapse occurs when covariance shrinks:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-9bbd-e0c853588424" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma_t = \text{Cov}(S_t \mid O_{1:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-99ad-cbcf614c54d9" class="">Failure when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-8889-e9a25200f029" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_t) \to 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-b4e5-cc8ded1df884" class="">Security invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-ac4d-e32d77eb238a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_t) \ge \delta}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-b9e5-d809cfcccb38" class="">Interpretation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-b31f-f8db8a8ac9bb" class="">No sensitive dimension becomes sharply predictable.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-bbdb-f1f3e8b8ef33" class="">This is almost never considered.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-b08e-d3e80041bbe4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8066-b1de-f1a01895fa5d" class="">5️⃣ Reflexive Market Coupling — Stability Condition</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-b7e3-c742ed0cda68" class="">Institutional dynamics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-ad42-e2092fff9262" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{t+1} = f(S_t) + G M_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-90b5-e5e59fedcb12" class="">Market belief update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-9a07-d12dfb474280" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_t = \psi(O_{1:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-9288-e8af0645ebfd" class="">Market move:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-85be-c7da78beb3cf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M_t = \phi(B_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-8a06-f195c677820d" class="">Full loop:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-9a71-e7ce0ca1b3f4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t \rightarrow O_t \rightarrow B_t \rightarrow M_t \rightarrow S_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-abbd-fb383d1b228f" class="">System is stable if spectral radius:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-a994-f8cfbd49aaa3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho\left(\frac{\partial S_{t+1}}{\partial S_t}\right) &lt; 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-8732-c90e93cb46c9" class="">AI output can increase loop gain.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-a988-cb865628eabc" class="">Security objective:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807b-8237-c62f5839beaf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
