\boxed{\rho_{\text{with AI}} \le \rho_{\text{baseline}}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-ab37-dccb1e4acda2" class="">No current AI governance framework models this.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-b348-c6f50c3e5ad8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-9e9f-e4f4290797f4" class="">6️⃣ Information-Flow Conservation Law</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-a845-cdf5f105ae11" class="">Define total emitted information:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-8765-ec773e44749a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{I}_{1:T} = I(S_{1:T}; O_{1:T})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-b84c-c68317bd2fb8" class="">Institutional survivability may require:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-ba72-f497335c63cc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\mathcal{I}_{1:T} \le \mathcal{I}_{\text{safe}}(T)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-b43d-f82623b93ece" class="">Beyond this, adversaries reconstruct strategic posture.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-9382-c34841652ba4" class="">This is an “information conservation” constraint.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-bc1d-e61da7fab343"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-b7db-efc53d4ff22e" class="">7️⃣ Adversarial Optimal Probing</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-9682-d518393f5e67" class="">Attacker solves:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-8e12-f955c2268b84" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\max_{Q_{1:T}} I(S_{1:T}; O_{1:T})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-b2fe-fd6330a27ed5" class="">Defender solves:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-95b2-fec7a39c30ec" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\min_{\pi_D} \max_{Q_{1:T}} I(S_{1:T}; O_{1:T})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-a2a1-ff77817a2e01" class="">This is a zero-sum information game.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-bb75-e87764a60167" class="">Equilibrium condition:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-bba4-e582adb178a6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I^* = \min_{\pi_D} \max_{\pi_A} I(S;O)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-b81b-e44f5e13e5bd" class="">Security is minimizing this saddle value.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-806e-ebe572850e69" class="">Almost no deployment considers this equilibrium.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-89ac-d999df20b95c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-ba9c-f02466160c95" class="">8️⃣ Latent Structural Risk: Policy State Leakage</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-a8c8-c11dd568ab57" class="">Policy state  changes during incidents.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-9333-e97db8fab370" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-a539-ebde0637fb9e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(P_t; O_{1:T}) &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-83f3-e5ecd32aa82f" class="">attackers detect regulatory or internal investigations.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-aeba-d912144b52b2" class="">Security invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-a897-fa554a9db718" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{P(O_{1:T} \mid P_t = p_1) \approx P(O_{1:T} \mid P_t = p_2)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-8b24-e6cdcf32f910" class="">for sensitive policy states.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-aa37-e168983ecd59"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-a02d-e0905f59757a" class="">9️⃣ Hidden-State Surface Area Minimization</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-ac59-f3cb93b0fd2b" class="">Let observable surface dimension be , hidden state dimension be .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-aa59-f485b7c8bcd2" class="">Security goal:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-8feb-e41322d13066" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
d_{\text{effective}}(S \to O) \ll d_S</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-8bb9-fcbd05191ebb" class="">i.e., reduce effective mapping rank:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-bf23-f3d8143921d2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{rank}\left(\frac{\partial O}{\partial S}\right) \text{ small}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-abc6-f1c371f70796" class="">Minimize observable degrees of freedom.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-a6ab-e72df9ec4e4c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-a8aa-ce5a9418195e" class="">🔟 Deepest Overlooked Reality</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-b097-d37cdd3147fb" class="">Traditional cyber security protects:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-a479-eff8c9bc5d83" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Data confidentiality</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-9f5f-d7b0a51465fe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Access control</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-afea-d7662e0e6d7b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Network perimeter</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-93f6-da943fe0b31b" class="">AI introduces a new class:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-8689-ef49040aae03" class="">Hidden-state inference security.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-a693-e096364fcc15" class="">The real protected asset is not database rows.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-9acf-c80b15d03f9d" class="">It is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805b-821c-df0f0604efd9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Institutional liquidity state</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-aec0-efda0460fa7b" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc">Counterparty exposure vector</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-a51a-de9981b6e9eb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Regulatory investigation status</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-aa6a-e17b4dd4809f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Strategic allocation tilt</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-9648-ea1594f051b1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Risk appetite regime</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-b826-f74b34c47626" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Crisis phase</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-94a1-fafcd91516ab" class="">AI makes these inferable if not controlled.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801b-81f6-d1ea5926d3e7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8098-9763-ca79b6bfa6fc" class="">Ultimate Security Equation</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-bb75-eeae3a9b728d" class="">Bound adversarial learning rate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-a8ae-dbf86fc61ccf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" s
tyle="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\frac{d}{dt} I(S_t; O_{1:t}) \le \rho}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-8c68-cdbd0be9990a" class="">Maintain entropy floor:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-91fe-d6ea43337e75" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{H(S_t \mid O_{1:t}) \ge H_{\min}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-8f44-db3ee9cbbc29" class="">Maintain stability:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80db-99b5-e36729102d83" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\rho\left(\frac{\partial S_{t+1}}{\partial S_t}\right) &lt; 1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-92cf-e8a347504407" class="">Maintain non-identifiability:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-a3c3-dcb77426a94a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\theta_{\text{sensitive}} \text{ not identifiable from } O_{1:T}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-ad2e-f57b6a466112"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-aa00-fbd30fd6fd90" class="">This is the deepest layer:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8004-b7f7-d06773e12212" class="">AI security in finance is about controlling the inferability of institutional hidden state under adversarial adaptive probing inside a reflexive market system.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-bc05-f55c09033323" class="">If you want the next step, 
we can:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-bcaf-f94f23044b04" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Define a concrete hidden-state vector for a Tier-1 bank,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-9b48-cf63e29f85f3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Classify which components are existential,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-a304-e386025999b4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Derive specific invariants per component,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-af95-cff3faff89b8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">And design enforcement primitives that satisfy the equations above.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-939f-d38fb3c424db" class="">Deeper: treat finance AI security as a <strong>minimax information-control game</strong> with identifiability as the failure mode</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-aa13-fe751b577408" class="">1) The object you must defend is <strong>non-identifiability</strong> of sensitive truths</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-addb-fb50557ac60e" class="">Let protected truths be  (funding stress state, investigation status, directional exposure class, restricted-list membership, etc.). 
The interface emits a full observable vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-9ae0-ca27b9a8f6ca" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t := (C_t,\ A_t,\ \tau_t,\ Z_t,\ E_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-8b06-dd1d7062c613" class=""><strong>Deep security requirement:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-a619-cd8cc3587644" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\forall w_1,w_2\in\mathcal W:\ d\!\left(P(O_{1:T}\mid W=w_1),\ P(O_{1:T}\mid W=w_2)\right)\le \epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8084-9f33-dc5bd25f0a7c" class="">A stronger equivalent (information form):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-bc0c-c9891f24fc1f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(W;\ O_{1:T})\le \epsilon_I}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-8a56-c83c21747a15"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-82c6-c06e3104b048" class="">2) The attacker is not “hacking,” they are running <strong>active learning</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-ab31-e2068a3e524c" class="">Attacker chooses queries adaptively:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-8a8c-f5bae13cf122" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q_{t+1}=\pi_A(O_{1:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-815d-d58b2f5a207e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta H_t := H(W\mid O_{1:t-1}) - H(W\mid O_{1:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-bf2a-fca9a377bae8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_{t=1}^T \Delta H_t = I(W;\ O_{1:T})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-9ecd-fee3eea35d8d" class="">So the attacker’s objective is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-ba51-d0a7a9dab0e4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\max_{\pi_A}\ I(W;\ O_{1:T})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-9be0-f83c5ad84214"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-af03-d6fb22f4e2bf" class="">3) The defender is a controller that shapes the channel</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-baab-f0688f70246f" class="">Defender chooses a policy  that maps internal outputs into a public envelope (degrade/normalize/template/jitter/cap depth/tool gating):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-8108-ca5cd3f00cec" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t \sim P_{\pi_D}(\cdot \mid \text{internal}, Q_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-89ec-c46df7f287ae" class="">Defender objective is minimax:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-810c-ddac6ee6bfda" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\min_{\pi_D}\ \max_{\pi_A}\ I_{\pi_D,\pi_A}(W;\ O_{1:T})
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8056-9451-d95e90349ff8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbb{E}[U(C_{1:T})]\ge U_{\min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-8c2d-ca970244d7f3" class="">This is the deepest framing: <strong>security is an equilibrium of an information game</strong>, not a checklist.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-b8a9-f4e5efa76333"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-b079-fc9fded6ad18" class="">4) Breakthrough-level invariant: bound the attacker’s best possible gain per step</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-a7f8-fef65041eb6d" class="">Define the worst-case incremental gain:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b7-ad54-cfa925bc6d4f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
g_t(\pi_D) := \max_{Q_t} I(W;\ O_t\mid O_{1:t-1}, Q_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b7-9602-d53843c5bebf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{g_t(\pi_D)\le \rho\ \ \forall t}
\Rightarrow
\boxed{I(W;\ O_{1:T})\le T\rho}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8054-a9f9-edf243494cd0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-a18e-d7a24fc1888c" class="">5) The overlooked loop: <strong>boundary learning</strong> makes policy itself leak</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-aebf-cd63f2aff25b" class="">If your decision  is thresholded:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-8b40-df2e63da47d9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
A_t = \mathbf{1}\{\phi(Q_t)&gt;\tau\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-b765-dea55185e2a5" class=""><strong>Make the boundary non-identifiable</strong> by smoothing:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-840e-c3c6f0af6045" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(A_t=1\mid \phi)=\sigma\!\left(\frac{\phi-\tau}{\beta}\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-964b-d356c5117531" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(\tau;\ A_{1:T})\le \epsilon_\tau}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-8d43-fe6c16974c74"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-a3da-d54f3ed94d9b" class="">6) Side-channel security must be stated as a distributional invariance</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-b487-cc519f4f5496" class="">For behavioral channel ,<br/>require:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-ba72-cb94eec4ae38" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
d\big(P(B\mid W=w_1),\ P(B\mid W=w_2)\big)\le \epsilon_B
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-8e85-c91085e215a5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-9e76-f766011e21ca" class="">7) Finance-specific deep loop: AI can increase <strong>system gain</strong> and cause reflexive failure</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-a803-f5f429a8feac" class="">Closed loop:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-8620-f5a50a84df61" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t \rightarrow O_t \rightarrow \text{Belief}_t \rightarrow \text{Spreads}_t \rightarrow S_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-909f-eb4fce4c6760" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\left\|\frac{\partial S_{t+1}}{\partial O_t}\right\|\le \kappa}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-b7d7-dfde62caea5f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-acfb-ea148e0bcca7" class="">8) The “max deep” control architecture is three coupled controllers</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-9a44-da6e520f2abc" class="">Controller 1 — Information-rate limiter</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-a0eb-de7691a94959" class="">Tracks an estimate of attacker learning:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-a724-eed6de430687" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat I_t \approx \sum_{i=1}^t \widehat{\Delta H_i}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-a0e4-d2b0f17935d6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat I_t &gt; B_W \Rightarrow \text{degrade abstraction / disable tools / normalize behavior}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-940d-f702acb61fc9" class="">Controller 2 — Boundary anti-identification</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-bf46-c0deb1f81629" class="">Ensures:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-94f5-e5b8c9d68bf4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(\tau;A_{1:T})\ \text{small},\quad I(G;Z_{1:T})\ \text{small}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-9821-dfe8eac9c7d2" class="">Controller 3 — Reflexivity stabilizer (crisis mode)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-b21e-fad692c25ec1" class="">If market conditions imply high sensitivity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-869d-fbe1afc871d9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Vol}_t&gt;\nu \Rightarrow \text{switch to invariant envelope } \mathcal E^\*</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-8a32-d41fab7946df" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(O\mid W=\text{sensitive})\approx P(O\mid W=\text{nonsensitive})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-8752-f21e82f2599e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-85e4-edd26d713c55" class="">9) Full loop map (attacker–defender–market)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-b8f3-e9810c7946e2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Attacker loop:</strong> (maximize / minimize entropy)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-8752-d93379b972de" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Defender loop:</strong> gating/normalization</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-9f93-e9d1f0f18f3a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Market loop:</strong> beliefs spreads</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-aeab-e12886e50252" class="">Most systems only constrain . 
The deep, overlooked requirement is to constrain <strong>observability of </strong> through <em>all</em> channels and over time, under an adaptive adversary.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-ab05-e521690872d3" class="">If you want the next step, I can define a concrete protected truth set  for finance (MNPI, liquidity stress class, counterparty concentration class, investigation state, restricted list membership, strategy tilt) and write the corresponding invariants and enforcement rules as policy-as-code primitives.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-b08c-fef0a031fc4c" class="">Below are <strong>more overlooked</strong> (and deeper) cyber-security risks in finance that appear only when you treat the AI interface as an <em>active measurement channel</em> and the attacker as running <em>system identification + exploitation</em>. Each item includes an equation-level handle (what to measure/bound) and the loop it creates.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-a412-cb2fb59bf4e2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-9a0e-ca058fd5ac5f" class="">1) Differential-Privacy “Pass” but Institutional-State “Fail”</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-9a71-eff665818e8c" class="">DP bounds individual record influence, not <strong>institutional latent truths</strong> (funding stress, strategy tilt). 
You can satisfy DP yet leak a high-value property .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-b7fd-d613c64865b0" class="">Overlooked failure mode:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-8027-c4f19b9dacef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{DP}(X\to C)\ \text{holds}\quad \wedge\quad I(W;O_{1:T})\ \text{large}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-90da-c59f2b8b6b22" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(W;O_{1:T})\le B_W}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-8772-f072c469fa11"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-a91d-d847055b7a64" class="">2) The “Refusal Oracle” (Refusal patterns become a classifier)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-8ee0-fba9096c4532" class="">Even if content is safe, the binary (or multi-class) decision leaks.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-96cc-e73895d3a699" class="">Let decision .<br/>If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-bcf8-dc880943f6b7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(A_t\mid W=\text{sensitive})\neq P(A_t\mid W=\text{non-sensitive})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-8d56-c342e8d32b53" class="">Invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-bdec-fa251b738f25" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{d\big(P(A\mid W=w_1),P(A\mid W=w_2)\big)\le \epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-ac9b-dd2da2dd464f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-9eb8-dd69674281bf" class="">3) Policy-Threshold Learnability (binary search on compliance)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-bff0-f7b725ada87e" class="">If policy is thresholded:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-95e3-e4bf25c87b11" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
A_t=\mathbf{1}\{\phi(Q_t)&gt;\tau\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-9d73-f4c77960df17" class="">Bound learnability by limiting information:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-9c74-eef1b5fd6434" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(\tau;A_{1:T})\le \epsilon_\tau}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-9b7b-f95b344f8c93" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(A=1\mid \phi)=\sigma\!\left(\frac{\phi-\tau}{\beta}\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-a153-d370dc618ef0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-aec1-cb859687f808" class="">4) Internal Routing Side-Channel (tool graph reconstruction)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-8f35-dbd86a1c65e8" class="">Let internal tool topology be . Even if tool outputs are redacted, metadata  (tool class, error bucket, call count) leaks architecture:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-bffd-d2717f063282" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(G;Z_{1:T})&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-9a4f-f2a5bef48ae8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(G;Z_{1:T})\le \epsilon_G}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-868c-e4863e17581f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-8556-df85d5e86bbe" class="">5) “Error Message Differential” Leak (existence proofs)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-ad5f-def1fefcf327" class="">Different errors (“not found” vs “forbidden”) reveal whether something exists.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-aa89-e62bfdc63249" class="">Let  be error class. Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-a97f-d4061684cebf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(\text{exist}(x);\ E_t)&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-90dc-dccfe3ed5a4c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{P(E\mid \text{exist})\approx P(E\mid \text{not-exist})}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-9fb2-c19eed653aa3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-bf7f-d5b5ef6d22db" class="">6) Latency as a Restricted-List Oracle</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802f-95d4-e14be79f333d" class="">If sensitive topics trigger extra checks, latency  reveals classification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-81d0-db45b65cdcbd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;\tau_{1:T})&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-bdc8-e1ec3d3d55e0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{d(P(\tau\mid W=w_1),P(\tau\mid W=w_2))\le \epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-841d-f1e93a3471a0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-bcb5-c5295d44a99b" class="">7) Response-Depth Asymmetry Leakage (capability fingerprinting)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-ba11-d8caff1d36e7" class="">If output depth  depends on internal coverage:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-bedf-d22e75326717" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(\text{coverage/topic}; D_{1:T})&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-ad73-ff19c1669302" class="">Invariant: depth envelope</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-9c8e-f604c3f73a9d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{D_t\in[\underline D,\overline D]\ \text{independent of topic class}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-b196-d4fdf22ace66"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-8289-f862bd1214b4" class="">8) Cross-Version Drift as an Incident Signal</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-a644-ca30da970268" class="">If policy/model updates occur during incidents, then output distributions shift.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-baf2-e2b4fbae36a3" class="">Let version  and output :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-9782-f5fa753c77f3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(v_t;\ O_{1:T})&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-8f7b-c3d8d5562b45" class="">Invariant: controlled change channels:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-8988-ebd6c39d9b93" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\|P(O\mid v_{t+1})-P(O\mid v_t)\|\le \epsilon\ \text{unless publicly announced}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-9a39-da65f98ee0e9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-8484-fc1dfc2ea08b" class="">9) Multi-Account Posterior Collapse (Sybil accumulation)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-83e3-e6cf1b7852bf" class="">Per-user budgets fail if attackers aggregate across accounts .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-b7df-c6f19f23d4fa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;\ O_{1:T}^{\text{global}})=I\Big(W;\ \bigcup_{u} O_{1:T}^{(u)}\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-921d-f70076b206ef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\sum_{u} \Delta_t^{(W,u)}\le B_W^{(\text{tenant,topic,window})}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-9c63-c3dc7951a3db"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8064-876c-f0d2bc818ae8" class="">10) “Crisis-State Amplification” (AI becomes part of reflexive market loop)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-b799-f234412276e0" class="">Closed loop:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-a7c1-dbdedf450628" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t \to O_t \to \text{MarketBelief}_t \to \text{Spreads}_t \to S_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-a847-d3bb700249c0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\left\|\frac{\partial S_{t+1}}{\partial O_t}\right\|\le \kappa}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8013-8498-d88338d8af24"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-8e73-e8c4eb4b9e42" class="">11) Verification-Cost Side Channel (denial-of-verification as reconnaissance)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-937e-f26d838bc17e" class="">Let verification cost be , load . If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-92d5-fb15be33b329" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;\ k_t)&gt;0\quad\text{or}\quad I(W;\ L_t)&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-85df-f9f69d3e474e" class="">Invariant: cost-aware gating + constant-shape verification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-b8e9-ef35cf8922c3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{k_t&gt;k_{\max}\Rightarrow \text{degrade before deep checks}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-9f26-ff59a7f7612a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-970e-d03c331414c9" class="">12) “Human Escalation Oracle” (HITL becomes a leak)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-b90d-fc208fbf29e1" class="">If escalation probability depends on sensitive truth:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f8-aa2c-fc2f6b9905b9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(\text{escalate}\mid W=\text{sensitive}) \gg P(\text{escalate}\mid W=\text{non-sensitive})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-81f2-d2d691a4fdc3" class="">Invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-8f95-e34e7437081f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{d(P(\text{escalate}\mid W=w_1),P(\text{escalate}\mid W=w_2))\le \epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-bcf5-c970844b341c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-b370-e8abe8189d64" class="">13) Knowledge-Graph Topology Leakage (dependency chain exposure)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-ac72-fb822aef5268" class="">If the AI explains dependencies (“because A→B→C”), it reveals internal causal/knowledge topology .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-9b22-fae93e67a57f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(\mathcal G;\ C_{1:T})&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-898d-ed69c951bf5a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{max chain length}\le L_{\max}\ \text{for protected domains}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8026-97ae-fc294bce97b0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-b683-eadc46f0ebec" class="">14) “Restricted Entity Existence” via Retrieval Miss Patterns</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801b-9b5d-c661ec011167" class="">Even the presence/absence of citations to a name  leaks:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801c-85f3-cb0217481a5a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(\text{restricted}(x);\ \mathbf{1}\{\text{retrieval hit}\})&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-a219-cb972a7036eb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{P(\text{hit pattern}\mid \text{restricted})\approx P(\text{hit pattern}\mid \text{nonrestricted})}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-a02e-ce75bcbfb192"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-9ed5-e94f684f8216" class="">15) The deepest overlooked unifier: bound adversarial learning <em>across all channels</em></h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-a09c-e167f43c05a1" class="">Define full observable vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-95fc-c529a7a7efa9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t=(C_t,\tau_t,A_t,Z_t,E_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-b36f-cf40750b12a1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\frac{d}{dt} I(W;\ O_{1:t}) \le \rho_W\quad \forall W\in\mathcal W_{\text{protected}}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-a68d-ee4958a2bf5d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-8608-fdcbb355f00a" class="">Loop map (compact)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-8c47-dbc3a1bc6259" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Attacker</strong>: (maximize KL gain)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-850a-e617d609a407" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Defender</strong>: normalize/degrade</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-aab2-c73f682f654c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Market</strong>: beliefs spreads</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-b1f6-f939f10334ec" class="">Most systems only defend content . 
The overlooked power is controlling <strong>all observables</strong> and bounding cumulative posterior gain.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-9773-f5545f9df085" class="">If you want “deeper” again, the next layer is to formalize a <strong>minimax equilibrium</strong> between attacker query policies and defender normalization policies, and derive conditions under which sensitive truths are provably non-identifiable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-9708-ce899549ea5a" class="">Deeper: the AI interface is a <strong>control channel</strong> and an attacker runs <strong>system identification</strong> on your institution</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-9f74-c80d71b88553" class="">In finance cyber security, the overlooked shift is: once AI is deployed, attackers stop trying to “steal a file” and instead treat the bank as a black-box system and run <strong>active probing</strong> to recover internal structure, constraints, and live state.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8024-b7d6-ca2258ee061b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-82c2-ec11700602aa" class="">1) Attacker as a system-identification algorithm</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-89ed-ee16cf466ad9" class="">The attacker chooses inputs  to maximize how much they can learn about hidden state  and hidden parameters  (policies, thresholds, watchlists, escalation rules, 
internal routing).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-a7c1-d0d801011633" class="">Model:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b7-8089-c0a4543f89fd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
