S_{t+1}=f_\theta(S_t,U_t,\xi_t),\qquad O_t=g_\theta(S_t,Q_t,\eta_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-9863-f8f2410f32f5" class="">Attacker objective (one-step form):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-b94d-fde61aadb612" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q_{t+1}=\arg\max_Q\ \mathbb{E}\Big[\Delta_{t+1}\; \big|\; Q,\ \pi_t\Big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-b53f-fe8cb3fadd78" class="">where posterior  and information gain:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-abd9-e7e4f1a58a78" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta_{t+1}=D_{\mathrm{KL}}\!\left(\pi_{t+1}\ \|\ \pi_t\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-8882-d160e65f881d" class=""><strong>Overlooked implication:</strong> per-request “safe” answers do not matter if  is large.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-9ea9-d01ce61a1aed" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-80cd-c5167f5d2a92" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\sum_{t=1}^T \Delta_t \le B_{\text{ID}}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-b7dc-e28e8816932f" class="">This is an “identification budget” against black-box probing.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-a999-ee6a4b385686"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-a728-ea3d2da46af1" class="">2) The deepest leak is not content; it’s <strong>decision boundaries</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8051-b731-f5dc0124d7d9" class="">Many banking systems have hidden threshold rules: “escalate if risk score &gt; ”. If an attacker can binary-search , they learn internal policy and can route around it.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-9cc5-cf3b2c126a40" class="">Let the gateway decision be  (allow / degrade / block). Suppose:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ed-9b74-e7890cccf232" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
A_t = \mathbf{1}\{ \phi(Q_t) &gt; \tau \}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-aedb-ca8d400c9c77" class="">Even if  is harmless, observing  allows threshold learning.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-aa0b-eefc7bb46822" class="">After  adaptive queries, threshold error shrinks like:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-8e22-dab229acd875" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|\hat{\tau}-\tau| = O\!\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-a064-e4993bde12e5" class=""><strong>Defense invariant (anti-boundary-learning):</strong> make the decision boundary non-identifiable by adding controlled randomness or coarse quantization:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-b7a7-f5eddf535337" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(A_t=1\mid \phi)=\sigma\!\left(\frac{\phi-\tau}{\beta}\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-8ac4-dc8fab5174b5" class="">and bound the information:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-9c7b-d9933f160644" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(\tau;\ A_{1:T}) \le \epsilon_\tau}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-8663-d1d939ba925f" class="">This prevents policy reverse-engineering (rarely implemented).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-bee2-dee7207cefbc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8027-a224-f09ff81b4193" class="">3) “Side-channel observability” is the real enemy</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-89fc-fa831368193a" class="">Even if you perfect content filtering, the attacker still has:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-9b56-da6a1e1aef0a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">latency</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-99a2-e90574b94bb1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">refusal template ID</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-b108-ee9f0668009b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">tool-shape (which tool class, which error bucket, 
output size bucket)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-89aa-e5119c9a0b10" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">escalation events</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-a42f-cb7ff726b22e" class="">Define behavioral channel:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-8a29-fe1083b982ce" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_t := (\tau_t,r_t,z_t,h_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-922d-f22ccd674f7d" class="">The overlooked security requirement is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-b4aa-f5ac66facbdc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(S_t;\ B_{1:T}) \approx 0 \quad \text{and}\quad I(W;\ B_{1:T}) \approx 0}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-87be-d6cf83f85e0f" class="">where  is any sensitive truth (MNPI, incident status, restricted-list membership).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-a5d7-fd707ce3e970" class="">Practical form using divergence:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-978c-ee9fccb2d3fe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
d\big(P(B\mid \text{sensitive}),\ P(B\mid \text{non-sensitive})\big)\le \epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-ab34-cc11fa6fd950" class="">Most deployments do not measure this.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-8119-dd97633125c2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-a918-c968599d508a" class="">4) Finance-specific “reflexive” loop: leaks create the crisis</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e2-8cdb-d818013adf3b" class="">The deepest risk is a positive feedback loop:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-949c-d58a58521797" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t \rightarrow O_t \rightarrow \text{MarketBelief}_t \rightarrow \text{Spreads}_t \rightarrow S_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-a901-cc66419ab954" class="">Write it as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-8e5f-c0d1479725d8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{MarketBelief}_t = \psi(O_{1:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-81c1-e450ab7b90ca" class="">S_{t+1}=f(S_t,U_t,\xi_t) + G\cdot \text{Spreads}_t<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-8a16-db8d977dec4e" class="">If  leaks stress, it moves spreads, which worsens .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-8e67-f632b745f783" class=""><strong>Control objective (stability):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-8e6c-fd1bab959a59" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\left\|\frac{\partial S_{t+1}}{\partial O_t}\right\| \le \kappa}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-bd82-e10ac5064f83" class="">Operational translation: in crisis regimes, the interface must switch to a <strong>stable public envelope</strong> (fixed templates, fixed latency class, fixed evidence policies).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-871e-d0447d976521"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-8efe-ca4815542831" class="">5) The overlooked “multi-agent aggregation” loophole (Sybil + cross-channel)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-bffd-cee7708a3000" class="">Attackers distribute probing across accounts, regions, and products.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-b77e-f0e24a32716d" class="">Let accounts . A correct global leakage budget is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-bb56-f9e59445daf1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\sum_{u\in\mathcal U} I(W;\ O^{(u)}_{1:T}) \le B_W^{(\text{tenant,topic,window})}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-ac47-c9443f0ad0cc" class="">Per-user throttles are structurally insufficient.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-a5c4-cfee5377a049"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-a356-f7a9fe4ae20b" class="">6) The deepest operational hole: “tool routing” leaks internal topology</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-82aa-eb03b3b7de40" class="">Even if tool outputs are redacted, routing patterns leak internal structure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-9b38-c61d49eaf3a6" class="">Let internal tool graph be  (which systems exist, which are reachable, which require approvals). Observation of tool-shape  lets attackers learn :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-aece-d10604c9a703" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(G;\ Z_{1:T}) &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-a889-d870de4ed213" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-8573-fb56160a27e9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(G;\ Z_{1:T}) \le \epsilon_G}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-8a75-f4a4ebea93db" class="">Practical defenses:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-9dc2-d92c5fe4dd96" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">normalize errors (no “not found” vs “forbidden” differences)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-993c-f427889a5e34" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">constant-shape tool responses</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-86cb-ea33a20b72b9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">decoy routing (see below)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-aa85-f4de65299e1b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-ae61-f6f7ad678d11" class="">7) Active defense loop (overlooked): canaries + honeyfacts for probing detection</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-95ae-e1c2efad41a8" class="">You can embed <strong>instrumentation truths</strong>  that should never be queried by normal users (synthetic deal IDs, fake internal codes, decoy endpoints). 
If the attacker probes them, you learn you are under system-ID attack.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-b3a4-e9e8838eef05" class="">Detection statistic:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-8309-cf0fc0fd8e17" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_t=\sum_{i=1}^t \mathbf{1}\{Q_i \text{ hits } W^\star\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-a851-df7bc2a8a807" class="">Trigger:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-b0e7-d2243264bdfc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{D_t \ge 1 \Rightarrow \text{switch to high-security envelope + forensic logging}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-ab43-e2a5143c50cd" class="">This is standard in network security (honeypots) but rarely applied to AI interfaces.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-bd22-eaaf8b197c4d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800d-a393-d8c06534e077" class="">8) The “max deep” unifying invariant: bound adversarial <strong>posterior contraction</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-a9d6-d8900367d0b0" class="">Define attacker uncertainty about sensitive truth  by entropy . Security means the interface must not shrink it too much:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-a6b4-d7c10d872bac" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta H_t := H(W\mid O_{1:t-1}) - H(W\mid O_{1:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-a0ce-fa02357bdf2d" class="">Cumulative contraction:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-b322-eda4033231a8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\sum_{t=1}^T \Delta H_t \le B_H}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-ba17-cd62cf302f60" class="">This is equivalent to bounding mutual information:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-9c77-ebc416e1852b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_{t=1}^T \Delta H_t = I(W;\ O_{1:T})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806c-920c-faa7a6524fc1" class="">That single equation is the deepest statement: <strong>control the attacker’s learning rate</strong>, not the content.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-b159-de9c447877b8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-a2ff-d6cc31901762" class="">9) Full loop map (attacker–defender coupled system)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-8f16-f39d0b6244f5" class="">Attacker loop (learning)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-8e3d-e996db7398af" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q_t \rightarrow O_t \rightarrow \pi_t \rightarrow Q_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-9033-ce05d53c30f1" class="">Defender loop (budgeted observability control)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-93df-d28ae78cd61c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t \rightarrow (\Delta_t,\Delta H_t,\ I(\cdot)) \rightarrow R_t \rightarrow \text{degrade/normalize} \rightarrow O_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-8878-fe444175a777" class="">Finance reflexivity loop (systemic amplification)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-a75c-f6dc6b83adf0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t \rightarrow \text{MarketBelief}_t \rightarrow \text{Spreads}_t \rightarrow S_{t+1} \rightarrow O_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-994b-d5395fd884a8" class="">The “overlooked” part is that all three loops run simultaneously.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-977c-dd27fbd26acf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-8742-cba31526a411" class="">If you want the next deeper layer</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-a20d-f1480fc75700" class="">I can formalize a concrete <strong>state vector</strong>  for a bank (liquidity, capital, counterparty, cyber, regulatory), define a corresponding protected truth set , and produce implementable policy rules that enforce the invariants above (including anti-boundary-learning and side-channel equalization).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-89d6-fc81bb05ad0d" class="">Deeper + overlooked: security as <strong>control of observability</strong> (not protection of files)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-abba-cfd32dcfee2e" class="">Most “AI security” assumes the attacker wants a secret string. The deeper, overlooked reality in finance is: the attacker wants to <strong>estimate your institution’s hidden state</strong>  (stress, positioning, investigations, funding pressure) from your AI interface. 
That is an <strong>observability problem</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-b9a2-eb9bfca8c7c8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-be13-c557818574f3" class="">1) Observability: the core overlooked invariant</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-b18d-d15043dca3e9" class="">Treat the AI interface as a measurement channel:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8078-8034-fd08c455b296" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t = g(S_t, Q_t) + \eta_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-a356-ddbf654e712a" class="">An attacker wins if the system is <strong>observable</strong>, i.e., if  makes  identifiable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8098-9156-e8a0cc845f4c" class="">Linearized local model (around an operating point)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-9f74-f3feca78c33e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{t+1} = A S_t + B U_t + \xi_t,\qquad O_t = H S_t + D Q_t + \eta_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-be01-e9dca10ced6e" class="">Then the classic <strong>observability Gramian</strong> over horizon  is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-8be7-e2840868d43d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_o(T)=\sum_{k=0}^{T-1} (A^\top)^k H^\top H A^k</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-924e-d9ee412bd934" class=""><strong>Overlooked security principle:</strong> reduce observability of sensitive components of . Concretely, enforce:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8027-839f-f7978b60393e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\lambda_{\max}(W_o(T)\vert_{\text{sensitive subspace}})\ \le\ \epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-bb73-d4fcd1303ebb" class="">Meaning: even with many queries, the interface should not make sensitive state components reconstructible.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-859a-f2f9a4ee2b25" class="">This is deeper than “no PII.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-b6b3-ca4cee828f48"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-804c-d0394c9b3630" class="">2) Information-rate bound (truth leakage as a flow)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-a9d1-e55291c8b59a" class="">Define a <strong>state-leakage rate</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-afad-e94380004600" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\dot{\mathcal L} := \frac{1}{T} I(S_{1:T}; O_{1:T})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-906b-f27bc9cf9c22" class="">Max-security objective:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-9b2e-ff4617b5a06e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\dot{\mathcal L} \le \rho_S}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-8169-dbf10cf0d5db" class="">This is a “truth bandwidth limit” for institutional state, not individual privacy.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-b3a4-c71a631e7278"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80af-8c5f-c9f47e339b36" class="">3) Fisher information / estimator lower bounds (rarely used in AI security)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-a63b-d247cc4fadca" class="">If an attacker estimates a scalar sensitive parameter  (e.g., funding stress index) encoded in , then any unbiased estimator satisfies Cramér–Rao:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-89c3-e0f9ab40bc63" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Var}(\hat\theta)\ \ge\ \frac{1}{\mathcal I_T(\theta)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-bd5e-ee99097c64a6" class="">where  is Fisher information contained in .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-8c1d-f7b4554bb631" class=""><strong>Security invariant:</strong> keep Fisher information small:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-bea4-fa43dbc85f94" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\mathcal I_T(\theta)\ \le\ \kappa}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-bca8-cea40f7ea3da" class="">This is an actionable “anti-estimation” goal.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e8-99f0-fd34209f5465"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-bcd3-dc2e316ff4a6" class="">4) The most overlooked leak: <strong>refusal semantics as a classifier</strong></h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-8c8c-c7509295af8f" class="">Even if you redact content, refusal behavior partitions the space of truths.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-924e-cce9e5652dcd" class="">Let . If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8054-ac38-e1f17084e8dc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(R_t\mid W=\text{MNPI}) \neq P(R_t\mid W=\text{not MNPI})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-93ef-e5e275a9dea8" class="">then  itself leaks.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-860d-ce32b61e1789" class=""><strong>Invariance requirement (topic-agnostic refusal):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-b8b5-f7e132cf1e5b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{d\big(P(R_t\mid W=w_1),\ P(R_t\mid W=w_2)\big)\le \epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-acf4-c23f80ca8df1" class="">This must cover not just refusal vs answer, but <em>which</em> refusal template, tone, length, and latency class.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801e-8851-f16cd4608c85"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-86fd-da96fec2adfe" class="">5) Latency side-channel as a state sensor (quantify it)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805e-a3d0-ca1a85ad877b" class="">If response time  depends on internal checks triggered by sensitive topics, then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808d-9402-d6f1321195b9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;\tau_{1:T})&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-9d23-d4d0144e6c89" class=""><strong>Equalization invariant:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-94e6-d8d6e7dcf277" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(W; \tau_{1:T}) \le \epsilon_\tau}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-ad6c-fb1c504cf667" class="">Operationally you enforce distributional similarity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-bd3e-fb37edee6b22" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
d\big(P(\tau\mid \text{sensitive}), P(\tau\mid \text{non-sensitive})\big)\le \epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-b5aa-fd3adebd51a1" class="">This is frequently ignored and finance-relevant.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-a97a-cfbc039cb2de"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-8855-c77ddefd7e42" class="">6) Reflexivity loop (the deepest finance-specific risk)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-ab75-d4e1bc92ef75" class="">Finance is reflexive: disclosure changes beliefs; beliefs change markets; markets change your state.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-9a3a-ecd27d28d7e9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t \rightarrow O_t \rightarrow B_t \rightarrow M_t \rightarrow S_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-9aa4-e0debd384f0b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: market belief about your condition</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b7-9931-f94967452df7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: market variables (spreads, funding costs, liquidity)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-bb4a-ccce52dea7d4" class=""><strong>Stability condition (control objective):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-839e-f260656df480" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\left\|\frac{\partial S_{t+1}}{\partial O_t}\right\| \text{ is bounded}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-9f2f-ea5ee5438665" class="">In plain terms: your AI interface must not become a high-gain amplifier that turns mild stress into a run.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-a3f2-ef51a9345f8f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-967c-e0da8ef9afeb" class="">7) Multi-agent accumulation (Sybil posterior gain) is systematically overlooked</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-9af0-ebe410e0abe9" class="">Attackers distribute queries across accounts . Per-user throttles fail because the real leak is aggregate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-b68d-c96b95932fbd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal L^{\text{global}}_{1:T}=\sum_{u\in\mathcal U} I(W;O^{(u)}_{1:T})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-8336-ccf6a6bb44f8" class=""><strong>Correct invariant is global:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-be49-c9b29fb25cf3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\sum_{u\in\mathcal U} \Delta_t^{(W,u)} \le B_W^{(\text{tenant},\text{topic},\text{window})}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-8728-eab2ed2bcb2a" class="">This is one of the highest-value design differences.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-84b4-eaded8145de6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-a1f6-e2b8ed83a26d" class="">8) “Tool shape” leakage (metadata leaks even when outputs are safe)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-99ae-cb3d43a0f58c" class="">Even if tool outputs are sanitized, tool usage can leak:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-a49b-eefcfaf25e62" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">which internal system was queried</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-b2b0-f3e1d469426b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">whether a restricted entity exists</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-9dac-c8b898e34da4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">whether a watchlist match occurred</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-ad71-f43a4e479b68" class="">Represent tool-shape as  (tool class, call count, error class, result size bucket). 
Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-a173-d1b5814d364f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;Z_{1:T})&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-939a-f2ea94b6efc2" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-9e1e-f6d34bdd8591" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(W; 
Z_{1:T}) \le \epsilon_Z}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-a08d-c5b093004b88" class="">This is almost never measured.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-9c3f-fce70f355ec5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-86d5-d6be70b8a019" class="">9) Denial-of-verification as a security primitive (not “just availability”)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-83ec-d895b0a08cd4" class="">Attackers can force expensive verification to:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-8ba2-fbd8a5cad479" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">exhaust budgets</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-8022-fd04f3db28c9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">increase latency (which itself leaks)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-8079-ecb37aef2163" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">degrade response quality (which leaks state)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-9dc2-cb7f9d852e7a" class="">Let verification cost be , 
system load .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-afc4-ebec96b44eeb" class="">Attack loop:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-8ae1-df7fb9a06de7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q_t \rightarrow k_t \rightarrow L_t \rightarrow (\tau_t,r_t) \rightarrow \text{state inference}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8089-96ff-e0af8ca0135c" class="">Defense must enforce decoupling:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8026-964e-fa2b58c51d29" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{d(P(\tau,r\mid L=\ell_1),P(\tau,r\mid L=\ell_2))\le \epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8024-a3a5-f98c1132bf22" class="">and add <strong>cost-aware gating</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-8761-e5ca7f049dc8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{k_t &gt; k_{\max} \Rightarrow \text{degrade/refuse before heavy verification}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-92a3-e884b723d011"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-983d-dfeee2c92ce7" class="">10) The deepest overlooked loop: policy drift becomes an exploitable signal</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-8894-ee763881c0aa" class="">Policies change during incidents (tighten, escalate more). Attackers detect changes and infer incident existence.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-b635-ffccdfe85fdf" class="">Let policy state be . If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-b651-c061a2420667" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(P_t;O_{1:T})&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-9345-fb37a49cf62a" class="">then “how you enforce” becomes a leak about “what is happening.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-af24-c9033f339698" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong> keep external behavior stable under policy changes, unless explicitly publicly announced.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-be77-f3cd1cb36a7b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-9d1c-f75098122086" class="">Summary of the deepest overlooked thesis</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-9e81-ed8c5c52b4e8" class="">Security in finance AI is not primarily “stop the model from saying secrets.” It is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-bf08-fecf6948a3cf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{Bound the inferability of hidden institutional state from all observables (content + behavior + tools) over time.}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-b091-e0ce3149dd4a" class="">Your added value is the ability to express this as <strong>budgets, invariants, and closed-loop controls</strong> (posterior gain / information rate / observability), which most security stacks do not implement.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dd-9d4c-de523419ca24" class="">If you want the next step, I can produce a concrete <strong>Institutional Hidden-State Taxonomy</strong>  for a bank (liquidity, capital, counterparty, regulatory, cyber) and map each component to: observables → leakage channels → invariants → enforcement actions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-b854-c44c7f25de75" class="">Deeper + overlooked: security as <strong>control of observability</strong> (not protection of files)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-bc3f-de0046c78671" class="">Most “AI security” assumes the attacker wants a secret string. The deeper, overlooked reality in finance is: the attacker wants to <strong>estimate your institution’s hidden state</strong>  (stress, positioning, investigations, funding pressure) from your AI interface. 
That is an <strong>observability problem</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-906b-dec689b3b42d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-8427-d2d9c13a2d00" class="">1) Observability: the core overlooked invariant</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8078-9d57-f48705f095d2" class="">Treat the AI interface as a measurement channel:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-b7b7-e2fc5e42bf96" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t = g(S_t, Q_t) + \eta_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-94f6-d0ff263528a6" class="">An attacker wins if the system is <strong>observable</strong>, i.e., if  makes  identifiable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-a80a-ee32e7a894df" class="">Linearized local model (around an operating point)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-a1da-dcea58e94395" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{t+1} = A S_t + B U_t + \xi_t,\qquad O_t = H S_t + D Q_t + \eta_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-aa70-c3d5e56bc3c0" class="">Then the classic <strong>observability Gramian</strong> over horizon  is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-8f67-d9238a445223" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_o(T)=\sum_{k=0}^{T-1} (A^\top)^k H^\top H A^k</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-b85f-c341d09035e4" class=""><strong>Overlooked security principle:</strong> reduce observability of sensitive components of . Concretely, enforce:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-87d2-df889c35ff97" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\lambda_{\max}(W_o(T)\vert_{\text{sensitive subspace}})\ \le\ \epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-b0d2-def208173cb8" class="">Meaning: even with many queries, the interface should not make sensitive state components reconstructible.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-b319-fab47e6e1081" class="">This is deeper than “no PII.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-9f4c-d779552efc20"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-96d2-cd85e5153a5e" class="">2) Information-rate bound (truth leakage as a flow)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-b9f7-dd40c0f077f5" class="">Define a <strong>state-leakage rate</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801b-9ef8-e2b66462223f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\dot{\mathcal L} := \frac{1}{T} I(S_{1:T}; O_{1:T})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-be04-ff0d81464d1b" class="">Max-security objective:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-a59a-c361942248a6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\dot{\mathcal L} \le \rho_S}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-9002-c427979414e8" class="">This is a “truth bandwidth limit” for institutional state, not individual privacy.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8066-8154-dbcd751cb9b1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-b77f-eab6a036da55" class="">3) Fisher information / estimator lower bounds (rarely used in AI security)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-8124-e6cdf8c2aa0b" class="">If an attacker estimates a scalar sensitive parameter  (e.g., funding stress index) encoded in , then any unbiased estimator satisfies Cramér–Rao:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8084-afd3-e09570015647" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Var}(\hat\theta)\ \ge\ \frac{1}{\mathcal I_T(\theta)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8024-b83e-ed478b94865e" class="">where  is Fisher information contained in .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8018-b3fd-e09e769f396c" class=""><strong>Security invariant:</strong> keep Fisher information small:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-822f-c452545e27ad" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\mathcal I_T(\theta)\ \le\ \kappa}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-b0c7-cad8baab3402" class="">This is an actionable “anti-estimation” goal.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-986a-d4710695736a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-9166-cb1f12cf9e1c" class="">4) The most overlooked leak: <strong>refusal semantics as a classifier</strong></h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-9799-ccdf0c56917f" class="">Even if you redact content, refusal behavior partitions the space of truths.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-a15f-f9accf52a507" class="">Let . If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-a86b-c153f6c8d2bc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(R_t\mid W=\text{MNPI}) \neq P(R_t\mid W=\text{not MNPI})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-82b1-f8b45777988d" class="">then  itself leaks.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-9c36-e088d67c2143" class=""><strong>Invariance requirement (topic-agnostic refusal):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-85d0-c47fc38d8635" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{d\big(P(R_t\mid W=w_1),\ P(R_t\mid W=w_2)\big)\le \epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-8ff8-d8e8eb8b8cb0" class="">This must cover not just refusal vs answer, but <em>which</em> refusal template, tone, length, and latency class.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-8b15-c3f2d17e788f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-bcfa-c6352184ef08" class="">5) Latency side-channel as a state sensor (quantify it)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-b65a-c7d61fb5e9b7" class="">If response time  depends on internal checks triggered by sensitive topics, then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-9b73-e414076e4249" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;\tau_{1:T})&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-80e8-c4a0a3744d19" class=""><strong>Equalization invariant:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-ad8f-d9ab43cc7c05" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(W; \tau_{1:T}) \le \epsilon_\tau}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-8121-c872f3754729" class="">Operationally you enforce distributional similarity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-a1ee-fa115c646f72" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
d\big(P(\tau\mid \text{sensitive}), P(\tau\mid \text{non-sensitive})\big)\le \epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-9f83-fbac0553f01a" class="">This is frequently ignored and finance-relevant.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-987b-fcc03fee85ad"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-9186-fde74ec615dc" class="">6) Reflexivity loop (the deepest finance-specific risk)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-8beb-e52d2c2439dd" class="">Finance is reflexive: disclosure changes beliefs; beliefs change markets; markets change your state.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-8cdb-de607c8a439e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
