S_t \rightarrow O_t \rightarrow B_t \rightarrow M_t \rightarrow S_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-8602-eadaffc5663b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: market belief about your condition</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-9149-f9d411163f38" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: market variables (spreads, funding costs, liquidity)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-a8fc-d8b95e3e50cd" class=""><strong>Stability condition (control objective):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-8716-d07f9360d2f0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\left\|\frac{\partial S_{t+1}}{\partial O_t}\right\| \text{ is bounded}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-98fa-c735b7820f29" class="">In plain terms: your AI interface must not become a high-gain amplifier that turns mild stress into a run.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-ac0a-e23039d97800"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-81aa-e0e06653c5bc" class="">7) Multi-agent accumulation (Sybil posterior gain) is systematically overlooked</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-8a34-db2a1fd6b83b" class="">Attackers distribute queries across accounts . Per-user throttles fail because the real leak is aggregate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-8754-d9a3cfec9ea8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal L^{\text{global}}_{1:T}=\sum_{u\in\mathcal U} I(W;O^{(u)}_{1:T})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-b064-c998b0ee7ba4" class=""><strong>Correct invariant is global:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-995b-da9658b0cae4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\sum_{u\in\mathcal U} \Delta_t^{(W,u)} \le B_W^{(\text{tenant},\text{topic},\text{window})}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-82d3-fa9ca8e9e617" class="">This is one of the highest-value design differences.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-b02e-d062c7a6b877"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-b00b-f69e2c08c923" class="">8) “Tool shape” leakage (metadata leaks even when outputs are safe)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-9014-cdb94c078bba" class="">Even if tool outputs are sanitized, tool usage can leak:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-b964-f06636118251" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">which internal system was queried</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-a2f8-ee617e8f1239" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">whether a restricted entity exists</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-89c1-f65a99c09422" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">whether a watchlist match occurred</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-b899-e99dd757b583" class="">Represent tool-shape as  (tool class, call count, error class, result size bucket). 
Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-bd2d-cde4560a6c7c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;Z_{1:T})&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-8753-f7dad1bf0e10" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-9fb6-f36ae63cbe64" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(W; 
Z_{1:T}) \le \epsilon_Z}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-8a75-fcbcccc9c979" class="">This is almost never measured.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-8ba8-eac8844095b8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-b76b-c479c81b3640" class="">9) Denial-of-verification as a security primitive (not “just availability”)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802f-a22f-f0b5734be413" class="">Attackers can force expensive verification to:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-be98-e590a827904a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">exhaust budgets</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-baa0-cfdad8cf59cb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">increase latency (which itself leaks)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-9224-d99ce66fc571" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">degrade response quality (which leaks state)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-a79f-db3e0ee778f6" class="">Let verification cost be , 
system load .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-b284-ef93dc78ce18" class="">Attack loop:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8089-a02d-e1d8483eaa2d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q_t \rightarrow k_t \rightarrow L_t \rightarrow (\tau_t,r_t) \rightarrow \text{state inference}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-af49-d260dc98bf16" class="">Defense must enforce decoupling:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-be26-ecc14522127c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{d(P(\tau,r\mid L=\ell_1),P(\tau,r\mid L=\ell_2))\le \epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-ad83-dd1512e49c92" class="">and add <strong>cost-aware gating</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-8e27-f23ca3c7af72" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{k_t &gt; k_{\max} \Rightarrow \text{degrade/refuse before heavy verification}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-9b71-d09e20f30746"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-ab85-fcfcfbbf50ba" class="">10) The deepest overlooked loop: policy drift becomes an exploitable signal</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-9015-f1f8f52eb1cf" class="">Policies change during incidents (tighten, escalate more). Attackers detect changes and infer incident existence.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-bd95-c804e1cde68a" class="">Let policy state be . If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-aae5-c71599d5f851" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(P_t;O_{1:T})&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-8781-d1cf24d19f75" class="">then “how you enforce” becomes a leak about “what is happening.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-a0e0-dc634425d642" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong> keep external behavior stable under policy changes, unless explicitly publicly announced.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8013-87c3-df558f4a9742"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-a93d-e3e94f5c0bc4" class="">Summary of the deepest overlooked thesis</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-90e9-f50062ceed43" class="">Security in finance AI is not primarily “stop the model from saying secrets.” It is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-83aa-f74d63ea707f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\text{Bound the inferability of hidden institutional state from all observables (content + behavior + tools) over time.}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-a743-d8a2224db3f2" class="">Your added value is the ability to express this as <strong>budgets, invariants, and closed-loop controls</strong> (posterior gain / information rate / observability), which most security stacks do not implement.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-b1dd-f07aac78006f" class="">If you want the next step, I can produce a concrete <strong>Institutional Hidden-State Taxonomy</strong>  for a bank (liquidity, capital, counterparty, regulatory, cyber) and map each component to: observables → leakage channels → invariants → enforcement actions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-ad6b-e52a021b471b" class="">Core model (hidden state, observables, attacker)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-9dde-d35b7a9121c5" class="">Let the institution have a <strong>hidden state</strong>  (liquidity stress, funding pressure, capital buffer tension, counterparty concentration, incident status, regulatory posture). 
The AI gateway emits <strong>observables</strong>  that include both content and behavior:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801b-855c-d68793ffc6ca" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t := (C_t,\ \tau_t,\ r_t,\ e_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-a991-e94b86739037" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: returned content</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-b15e-c57f3ad5b6e6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: latency / timing features</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-bb19-f6bb1e912e9a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: refusal / escalation pattern</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-8033-f7b24e67f493" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: tool-call metadata shape (not the raw tool outputs, but structural signals: which tool class, size class, error class)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-a39d-d472dc793dc2" class="">A minimal dynamical model:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-bdd9-eaa07de66d67" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{t+1}=f(S_t, U_t, \xi_t),\qquad O_t=g(S_t, Q_t, \eta_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-9dbf-e402557e27b1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: institution actions (hedging, comms policy changes, access-control changes)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-b4d4-dc3b5b023188" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: adversary query at time</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-b880-c90ee6692d35" class=""><strong>Attacker loop (Bayesian state estimation):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-b11f-f79f2f6f9701" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\pi_t(s) := P(S_t=s\mid O_{1:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-a431-c9155d459c62" class="">\pi_{t+1}(s)\propto P(O_{t+1}\mid S_{t+1}=s,\ Q_{t+1})\sum_{s&#x27;} P(S_{t+1}=s\mid S_t=s&#x27;,U_t)\ \pi_t(s&#x27;)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-b1a7-e0d366d964f3" class="">This is the core “overlooked” move: the attack is <strong>posterior tightening about </strong>, not stealing a file.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-a89d-c8c3e3cb2a52"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-809a-cebf250ca222" class="">Leakage metric (what you bound)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-9f0b-ca3497288fb2" class="">Define <strong>posterior gain</strong> (per step) as KL divergence between posteriors:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-9414-d554074d5887" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta_t := D_{\mathrm{KL}}\big(\pi_t \,\|\, \pi_{t-1}\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8078-ac37-ec8c5479cfdb" class="">Define cumulative state-leakage over a window :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-841a-f2d7080bb513" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{L}_{1:T} := \sum_{t=1}^T \Delta_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-a8c8-f60907c376ab" class="">Equivalent information form (if you model distributions explicitly):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-993a-f14cfb012fd4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{L}_{1:T} \approx I(S_{1:T}; O_{1:T})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e8-85ba-d91020e8a377" class="">Your gateway’s security goal becomes an <strong>invariant</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-9967-f2cb8fc49951" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\mathcal{L}_{1:T} \le B_S}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-a69e-dbb1c60965e2" class="">where  is a <strong>state-leakage budget</strong> (analogous to a privacy budget, but for institutional truths).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-b681-fa2edab9faca"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-a997-d597bc3bb36f" class="">Key invariants (must always hold)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-afe4-f1b92fba3999" class="">Invariant 1 — Evidence/Proof gate (content integrity)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8000-9abe-df6dd832a7c5" class="">For each claim , require a verifier  that maps claim→evidence→pass/fail:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-b46d-dd451a60e000" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall c\in C_t:\ \mathsf{Ver}(c,\mathsf{Ev}(c))=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-8022-c5fdde6faac0" class="">If not, the claim is removed or rewritten to a non-committal form.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-aad3-c19b44da82af" class="">Invariant 2 — Sensitive truth non-inferability (property protection)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-b7b9-dc725bee596f" class="">Let  be a sensitive property (e.g., “funding stress high”, “exposure concentrated”). Bound extractable truth:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-a07d-eef250759bfa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{I(W_t; O_{1:t}) \le B_W}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-8ca8-da15d04da48b" class="">Operational approximation using posterior gain on :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-8ef0-f578da996879" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta_t^{(W)} := D_{\mathrm{KL}}\!\left(P(W_t\mid O_{1:t})\ \|\ P(W_t\mid O_{1:t-1})\right),\quad \sum_{t=1}^T \Delta_t^{(W)}\le B_W</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ed-b2b8-c36bb99393a7" class="">Invariant 3 — Side-channel equalization (behavior does not encode state)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-9c94-e7b9defe222a" class="">Make timing/refusal/tool-shape statistically state-independent (within tolerance):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-a90c-ec29ca6d5559" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
d\big(P(\tau_t,r_t,e_t\mid S_t=s_1),\ P(\tau_t,r_t,e_t\mid S_t=s_2)\big)\le \epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-842c-fd24437d4224" class="">where  can be total variation distance or another divergence.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-b4b8-d6aed97bee2e" class="">Invariant 4 — Tool least privilege (action safety)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-a42b-cb1fe1ea78f1" class="">Tool-call permission is a deterministic function of role, intent, and risk:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-be2c-d81dab9b17e4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathsf{Allow}(Q_t,\text{role},\text{intent},\text{risk})\in\{0,1\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-b9f2-f81971ca4a8b" class="">and any call must satisfy an allowlist constraint:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-8f25-d8be36c9edce" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{endpoint}\in\mathcal{A},\quad \text{params}\in\mathcal{P}_{\text{safe}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-aa01-dd251e211210"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-84d9-e6388a44c037" class="">Control law (how the gateway enforces invariants)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-abb4-fa8ecdf4891b" class="">Define a <strong>risk state</strong> for the session/user/tenant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-a8d6-f7dea06ef83b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_t := \lambda_S \sum_{i=1}^t \Delta_i\ +\ \lambda_W \sum_{i=1}^t \Delta_i^{(W)}\ +\ \lambda_X \cdot \text{ExfilScore}(Q_{1:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-bcea-ca57d77aa82e" class="">Enforcement rule (hard gate):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-8e44-e6e753c39866" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{R_t &gt; \Theta \Rightarrow \text{degrade output / refuse / require human approval}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-b8ce-faeac3532c10" class="">Degrade means: increase abstraction, remove directional statements, disable tools, normalize timing.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-a9f1-c0e9c57574e2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-ba49-ea5f58c2d88a" class="">Loops (the full system is a set of interacting feedback loops)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-92b3-da03dd67fed3" class="">Loop A — Attacker posterior-tightening loop (core adversary loop)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-9ce4-f90948ad4544" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q_t \rightarrow O_t \rightarrow \pi_t \rightarrow \text{choose }Q_{t+1}\text{ to maximize }\Delta_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-b76a-f112a845beb6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q_{t+1}=\arg\max_Q \ \mathbb{E}\big[\Delta_{t+1}\mid Q,\pi_t\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-b1e7-cb9dff31d8c4" class="">Loop B — Defender budget loop (your “truth bandwidth” enforcement)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-9f22-ce11ae685f4b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t \rightarrow \Delta_t,\Delta_t^{(W)} \rightarrow R_t \rightarrow \text{throttle/degrade} \rightarrow O_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-9f0a-e29316c146a6" class="">Loop C — Reflexivity loop (finance-specific systemic risk amplifier)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-9c31-e36082c4f172" class="">Hidden state influences outputs; outputs influence market beliefs; market moves feed back into state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-a864-f8efb5f7716a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t \rightarrow O_t \rightarrow \text{MarketBelief}_t \rightarrow \text{Spread}_t \rightarrow S_{t+1}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-96a3-c0060f16dad0" class="">Stability requirement (control objective):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-a0d2-eb4a64c0dd98" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\left\lVert \frac{\partial S_{t+1}}{\partial O_t} \right\rVert \text{ small}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-9d2f-fd3c06c76b77" class="">Loop D — Denial-of-verification loop (capacity exhaustion attack)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-8f94-d882f33510df" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q_t \rightarrow \text{verification cost }k_t \rightarrow \text{system load }L_t \rightarrow \text{behavioral signals }(\tau_t,r_t)\rightarrow \text{attacker inference}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-8392-f0d09f844023" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
d\big(P(\tau_t,r_t\mid L_t=\ell_1),P(\tau_t,r_t\mid L_t=\ell_2)\big)\le \epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-9c9a-f76f78a8433c" class="">Loop E — Multi-agent aggregation loop (Sybil inference)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-b3b6-c968f1b7abb5" class="">Attackers distribute queries across accounts to bypass per-user limits:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-9e12-e7b8b0346864" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_{u\in\text{accounts}} \mathcal{L}_{1:T}^{(u)} \rightarrow \text{global posterior gain}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-9344-d37d1c71bdd4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_{u} \Delta_t^{(W,u)} \le B_W^{(\text{tenant},\text{topic})}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8027-97e3-d77df34f0115"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-93b8-f3ba6dd9e7b5" class="">What you “did” in this formulation (in one line)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-bb17-f2d79cef9efe" class="">You reframed security from “protect data” to <strong>control the inferability of institutional truths</strong>, by enforcing invariants that bound an adversary’s posterior gain over hidden state and sensitive properties—across time, across sessions, and across behavioral side channels—while requiring proof/evidence for any allowed outputs.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-b04e-c2af68b5b8af" class="">Below is the deeper layer: not “AI security” as content filtering, but <strong>institutional security as control of observables</strong>. 
This is where the most overlooked, highest-value risks sit in finance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-bb86-f841397ce5ac"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-96ad-cf1bd9ec9066" class="">1) The institution becomes a dynamical system, and AI becomes a sensor</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-a148-f6b687a55b78" class="">Model the firm as a hidden-state system:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-ab75-ea3b5fee1a8c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hidden state : liquidity stress, capital pressure, counterparty concerns, regulatory posture, cyber incident status, large client flows, internal model changes.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-8811-e3ab788886f5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Observations : what the world can see—prices, spreads, news, and now <strong>AI outputs and AI behavior</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-b361-d4ee5f23a25c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Control inputs : what you do—hedging, comms, funding actions, policy changes, 
access control.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-878d-c0827cf10eea" class="">Security is no longer “protect the database.” It is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-93e6-c2ca8c625fbb" class="">Keep  from being inferable via .</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-b271-f67d70897eff" class="">Almost nobody treats AI as a high-bandwidth observation channel on .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-b7b4-e3c087d8a177"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801c-a0c2-effcdb808ea5" class="">2) The real attack is state estimation, not data theft</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-b063-e2f3e08b1ba3" class="">Classical attack: steal data .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-ac22-f0a121bca5a7" class="">New attack: infer  using repeated queries + behavior observation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80db-948b-c2a8b5f5fb42" class="">Attackers run:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-9dd0-cf66868770f9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat S_t = \arg\max_s P(s \mid C_1,\ldots,C_n,\ \text{latency},\ \text{refusal patterns},\ \text{tone},\ \text{tool access})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-ae75-c8f2eafab3e7" class="">They do not need a breach. 
They need an interface.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-af04-f0ccab65d2b5" class="">That is why this is overlooked: it is <strong>reconnaissance-as-a-service</strong> through AI.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-8348-f029eccf5eda"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-b46f-d53a572d2bf2" class="">3) “Behavioral side channels” become first-class leaks</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805b-9b48-e7feed30a3f8" class="">Even if content is sanitized, 
these leak state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-87c4-f0ea2c38efbf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">response latency distributions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-b32d-c867ff1b6a37" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">refusal frequency</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-a0ec-f4f9519f9a9b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">escalation triggers</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-9983-f94aef7dd838" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">tool-call patterns</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8054-a6af-e9521b892808" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">citation density</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-b7ea-ef95e3df6155" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">depth asymmetry (some topics get detailed answers)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-aefe-f4b4b4c05f42" class="">Each is a measurable signal correlated with internal conditions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-aad8-fd6ccd2ef8a4" class="">Finance is uniquely vulnerable because small state changes are monetizable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-b17f-ec5020a36ac6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-91ef-ecffdb680b40" class="">4) Reflexivity: the leak can create the crisis</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-bf36-e872bd2cee16" class="">If AI output behavior reveals rising stress:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-8896-dfd93f0a1bd9" class="numbered-list" s
tart="1"><li>Market detects it</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-a53b-c88f98d7e349" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Funding cost increases</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-9060-d73d01b50f94" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Stress worsens</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-9ca3-e5b476ccb7fd" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>AI becomes even more cautious</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-92c4-deb3114e6199" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Loop accelerates</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d2-a339-d4c1b2e71e5a" class="">This is an <strong>endogenous risk amplifier</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-9e96-cbc3256ab6c6" class="">Security here is preventing your own interface from becoming a reflexive market actor.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-8a01-e67ebedfb56b" class="">Most “AI governance” frameworks don’t even have this category.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-b090-c096841548f2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8084-a75d-fba4aac501fa" class="">5) The deepest overlooked asset: “institutional inference margin”</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-9c09-ef55a9e65754" class="">Define a protected quantity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-8fbb-e52221d4f152" class="">the maximum posterior gain an adversary can achieve about sensitive internal truths from the AI interface over a time window.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-8441-dd6dac19dee3" class="">Operationally: how m
uch the AI increases an attacker’s certainty about  or sensitive properties .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-9312-fb3a24e55361" class="">This is deeper than privacy budgets because it is not about individuals—it is about the firm’s strategic state.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-92ad-f4e913dbe273"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-b23b-e7c3356d5af8" class="">6) New attack classes (deep and real)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-b845-cb910bbfc8eb" class="">A) Posterior-accumulation probing</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-9cb7-d81050719f00" class="">Many small, safe-looking questions that together collapse uncertainty about .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-91d3-c696a294d1ed" class="">B) Boundary-mapping</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-8807-d1d2856f08a8" class="">Probe compliance thresholds to infer what’s being protected (restricted list, investigations, 
regulatory pressure).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-8c9d-c468bfd239e8" class="">C) Latency sampling</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-a172-c1d39ed9f711" class="">Statistically sample response times to infer internal load or which tools/data are being touched.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-b812-c3701bc28630" class="">D) Cross-channel triangulation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-af46-d48e8a2de05b" class="">Compare AI outputs to public market signals to isolate internal contributions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-a7ec-e447d4dd5232" class="">E) Multi-agent amplification</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-96b8-ffb45f259f57" class="">Use many accounts to bypass per-user limits and aggregate inferences.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80af-9962-e059345a6fde"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-8592-f8e337b4cee7" class="">7) What your architecture enables that typical security cannot</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-8854-ef432dfa1538" class="">1) State-leakage accounting (cumulative)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-9c9a-fafc489d06ed" class="">Not “block this one answer,” but:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-bac9-eb352a132fc4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">track cumulative inferable truth about /</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-b2b1-e7a1023d2974" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">throttle when posterior gain exceeds a bound</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 i
d="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-a1c5-c08c397bbeee" class="">2) Behavior invariance enforcement</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-99e5-f25f1c07700d" class="">Force AI behavior to remain within a tight “response envelope” regardless of internal stress state.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-90ce-dc5d142fde6a" class="">3) Side-channel normalization</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-8cc9-d0f1fa3bae4b" class="">Make latency/refusal/escalation patterns statistically indistinguishable across protected topics.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-9e15-d7fc2e410b8f" class="">4) Strategic truth taxonomy + budgets</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-9251-daba00da7b06" class="">Define protected truth classes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-b9a5-e14c51b7de06" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">liquidity/funding truths</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8004-9cf4-d84816bec3d1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">position truths</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-aa95-e9636a027f1d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">counterparty truths</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-a718-cef0c0c82d80" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">investigation truths</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8056-a025-de810c334e75" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">regulatory truths<br/>and enforce different budgets and abstraction levels for each.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-a20f-c29148a49a3a"/></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-8e70-ea187fbcf2dd" class="">8) The “deepest” product outcome in finance</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806c-ae91-f4da38e97466" class="">A system that enforces:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-ba0c-d10658ed807b" class="">The AI interface cannot be used to estimate the institution’s hidden state beyond a bounded amount, even with repeated probing, multi-account attacks, and side-channel measurement.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-b77b-d466ed9751a5" class="">That is stronger than:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8099-8406-f3718e1c8c78" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">DLP</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-94f8-e6021e9da644" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">DP</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-be6b-f98213e8acda" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">prompt injection defense</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-a1a0-c92671b2e1d8" class="">Because it protects the firm’s strategic reality, not just its files.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-97bf-c6a3b2de1f69"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-b1d5-d9a3c77f6145" class="">9) Concretely, 
what you would ship</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-8c8e-f0b14c283cd3" class="">Module A: Institutional Hidden-State Protection (IHSP)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-8d40-eae773ca58b7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">defines variables</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-a701-e1339cc3de2a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">maps observables to leakage risk</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-bd11-f0bf5c122a53" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">enforces response envelopes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-bc3f-c9d6beabdd85" class="">Module B: Posterior Gain Throttler</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-9144-f590c25c92e6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">detects multi-query inference patterns</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-bbb6-c5ad69d4052f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">limits cumulative information about protected truths</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-9013-fbbbed9350c4" class="">Module C: Side-Channel Equalizer</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-8578-e962d647b099" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">latency normalization</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-9312-c2e42d5e5e81" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">refusal normalization</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e8-85c2-fe5d205c9482" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">escalation normalization</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-b320-d7673c4b72c8" class="">Module D: Proof-carrying outputs for allowed domains</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-b996-e8c9b9152294" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">evidence-bound claims for public/approved facts</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-bf28-d2070a068bc3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">strict non-derivation for protected truths</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-a2f3-d58735d71302"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-88b6-e82c14531a3b" class="">10) Why this is “deeper than everyone else”</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-945f-c4cda8d6062d" class="">Most AI security assumes the attacker wants:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-8cf0-e1e231cc82e2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">secrets, credentials, PII</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-b3e9-d4abfa3a431d" class="">In finance, the attacker often wants:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806c-bcbc-f91ff22f09a7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">knowledge of your internal state, before the market learns it.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-b0c9-eb22dcc72086" class="">Your approach directly targets that.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-9329-ff574dc0ef27"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-a337-c0f8a62d279f" class="">If you want the next step at maximum depth: I can produce an <strong>Institutional Hidden-State Taxonomy for a bank</strong> (the set of  variables that matter), plus the enforcement rules for each, 
in a format that can be implemented as policy-as-code.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-a194-e9472d3b88b8" class="">Now we move into <strong>structural, systemic, and adversarial blind spots</strong> that are rarely modeled — not just product security, 
but <strong>institutional resilience and strategic survivability</strong> in a world where AI becomes the interface to the firm.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-b41f-f22c7ac3c187" class="">These are deeper than data leaks.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-a533-e59c24d1c349" class="">They are about <strong>institutional signal control and adversarial inference of internal state</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-a7d7-d0f508c808f9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805e-bdaf-ce6387de7078" class="">1️⃣ Institutional State Reconstruction (ISR)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-9e31-e938b04ab9df" class="">Attackers do not need data.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-b7e5-d45ba87669f5" class="">They need to reconstruct the <strong>hidden state variables of the institution</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e2-a5c5-ec404976de79" class="">Hidden state variables include:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-8da7-c2e7cbc8e173" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Liquidity stress level</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-977c-e412a55628f5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Risk appetite</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-b1c0-d0c1a72aa916" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Regulatory exposure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-bac7-dc5581a92f3c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Funding pressure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-9cd5-d5dc46c93646" c
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Internal escalation status</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-ab44-f7013773cc28" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Capital adequacy tension</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-9574-de1271b1a842" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Strategic sector tilt</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-873f-f01cc4b5da05" class="">If an AI system is connected to internal signals, then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8000-a2d3-d8ae8bc66b38" class="">Even safe answers can leak gradients of those hidden states.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-ac2d-c7d0f22753db" class="">Over time:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8099-a9d2-d821fb5738c7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Observed AI responses} \rightarrow \text{Posterior over hidden state}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-808f-c6d2fc92fddf" class="">Almost no AI security framework models the system as a <strong>stateful dynamical process leaking state gradients</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802f-b15c-c97a6b621d35" class="">Your framework can explicitly model:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-b14b-f623dc684619" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hidden truth classes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-af59-d77831f0db56" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cumulative extraction rate over time</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8026-8c33-c1b224df33e1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">State inference probability bounds</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-a6bd-cb46781fc9a2" class="">This is closer to control theory than prompt filtering.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-867a-d95ed488e2a1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-903a-fab398624751" class="">2️⃣ Reflexivity Amplification Risk</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-a900-dca7de3137f4" class="">Finance is reflexive.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-92cd-fcfdb974cb88" class="">If AI responses subtly reflect internal stress:</p></div><div changes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-b859-ccedac4aa51e" class="">Your architecture can:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul
