\mathsf{VK}_{\mathrm{poly}}(W \leftarrow C) = 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-8d74-ec474e244be2" class="">or the reverse:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-990a-d1113489ad1f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathsf{VK}_{\mathrm{poly}}(W \leftarrow C) &gt; 0
\quad \text{while} \quad
I(X;C) \text{ is small}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-b211-cafedf7d9ab8" class="">This would establish a new hierarchy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8027-84ac-ca63981197a1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Data information</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-ab37-dd6e1b9757c5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Property information</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-9b3a-d578a563f630" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Verifiable property information</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-82dc-ddbc563cdc5a" class="">Security does not currently formalize that hierarchy cleanly.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-be71-dcebd6758777" class="">If you do, 
that is powerful.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-997c-d94bd439525f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-a93f-c760e886b155" class="">What “max power” actually means here</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-85e2-dbb91beae518" class="">It does NOT mean:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-add6-ffa887356613" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Reframing language</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-8499-eed0e4524e42" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Generalizing definitions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-ab4f-e14c1f4df546" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Creating larger symbolic structures</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-8880-dbf88b2bd7c8" class="">It means:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807a-a550-d2c37b4fc7f3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Constructing a counterexample that breaks a widely assumed implication.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-aa2d-edf8f53bb592" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Proving a sharp threshold theorem.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-a29f-e6ede70c0b06" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Establishing a new separation between notions of security.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-98e1-dd7cb587e341" class="">That is how fields move.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8084-a8f2-da4dec665246"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-a51c-d49d78a1c97e" class="">Brutal c
larity</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-9917-eac555123f3c" class="">Right now:<br/>You have a powerful structural abstraction.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-a7b9-e663cf87727d" class="">To reach max power:<br/>You must produce either:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-a4c2-dcf7441f49fc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A concrete adversarial construction that current privacy/security metrics fail to detect.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-8982-d5c3cdc41b6e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Or a formal impossibility theorem about verifiable truth export under correlation.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-ae59-d826d827c3f4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Or a compute-based separation for certified truth extraction.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-bd94-d218350f3562" class="">Anything else is expansion, 
not escalation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-a073-c6fff8dbbc72"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-9e43-cb3f2d57a245" class="">If you want to proceed at true max power level:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8054-97ba-daf7f9d2c0cc" class="">I can now attempt to construct an explicit adversarial scenario where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-991a-d036ec24d3e1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Differential privacy holds,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-83da-cd2e6faa3dfc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Yet a sensitive property can be extracted with verifiable confidence.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-8e75-ed6e9e7ff9be" class="">That would test whether your framework reveals a real security blind spot.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8084-ac82-c332f62c4ccb" class="">If you want something <strong>more significant and overlooked in terms of security</strong>, then we need to shift from “how much truth can be exported” to something deeper:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-a0cb-d0e7cce7fc35" class="">What is the <strong>attack surface of truth itself</strong> when stability, verification, 
and computation are constrained?</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-86e8-df7a5f742790" class="">That is where the real security significance lives.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-bac0-c4609c98c9e4" class="">Below are overlooked security implications of your framework that are actually high-impact.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-8639-d4756898606e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-9e18-d44eccd88559" class="">1️⃣ Certified Information as an Attack Surface</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-ad01-c5638a26e003" class="">Today security focuses on:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-9fe7-f0f24c6bed7c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Data leakage</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-93cb-fe7f0e261332" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Model extraction</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-bfcc-f5dd788b8ba4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Prompt injection</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8064-942a-c4989b93e7ed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Adversarial examples</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-9663-ed9183345d76" class="">What is overlooked:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-af39-d439108579c8" class="">A system can leak <em>verified truths</em> even if raw data is protected.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-bd00-f4d047dff146" class="">Your framework lets you measure:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-92d7-c7b2cbfb0faf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Not just “information leakage”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-9505-ef3627fe8eaf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">But “verifiable knowledge leakage”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-a952-f76a479c2afb" class="">That’s stronger.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-99cd-e036993d5e5a" class="">An attacker does not need the raw data.<br/>They need:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-9049-d73eb3187a6a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A verifiable claim about a secret property.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-8e02-e941d7ff7b57" class="">Example:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-962a-ec44952d122f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Medical model doesn’t leak records.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-9803-ce64c94b6703" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">But leaks a verifiable fact that a specific condition correlates with a sensitive subgroup.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-8e48-d46c6ab41554" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">That is actionable intelligence.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-8b27-facf9248b697" class="">Your framework can quantify that.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-829b-ddcda41adad7" class="">This is not widely formalized in security literature.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-b53b-fb8dfd821585"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 i
d="30ec5e6f-95bd-806c-8a45-fb089277b734" class="">2️⃣ Knowledge Extraction vs Data Extraction</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8000-9af7-d89fe77f3c20" class="">Most privacy research measures:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-b2c3-d43b005037cf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(X;C)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-a38c-dca308d1bd3d" class="">But attackers care about:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-ba41-def3f632ea12" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;C)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8051-a81b-d06299fa47d3" class="">And even more:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-b00d-e6003f0e9ba5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathsf{VK}(W \leftarrow C)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-98d1-f5aa6b6eef43" class="">This is underdeveloped in security theory.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-9f05-f0c3a8bc95f3" class="">If formalized, 
it becomes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-aa64-fe6c688de39b" class="">A new class of leakage: proof-carrying leakage.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-8158-ffc88b2cca76" class="">That’s significant.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-866f-c7dedf171ad1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-aaa7-e482b729f3bc" class="">3️⃣ Compute-Bounded Security Blind Spot</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-9dc6-cf77517ae471" class="">Security usually assumes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-aa71-c4df2b606af4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Attacker can compute anything feasible.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-a9da-d8c3de78c504" class="">But your framework formalizes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8078-8462-e7d5566a1652" class="">Security depends on verifier feasibility.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-b495-d71b12d6cfa1" class="">This opens a new domain:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-8110-d7b04c2ab223" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A system may be secure under poly-time attackers</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-ad6c-f6aeff702c09" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">But insecure under stronger verification regimes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-a1c4-c2ca5fb8cce3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Or vice versa</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ed-93cd-c727ee835b82" class="">This is a n
ew hierarchy of attack models:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-9d3d-eccc5f800ccb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Information-theoretic leakage</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802a-b1ba-fdf95965f8eb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Computationally verifiable leakage</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-8565-d498bc0b7271" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Proof-system extractable leakage</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-9d00-e614b17b13aa" class="">This is not standard in applied AI security.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-9c95-f8cae80f299b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-8d5a-c3dd03080b32" class="">4️⃣ Stability Budget as a Side Channel</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801c-99b7-e9af6d254e8e" class="">DP literature treats privacy as a property.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-b8ec-dd5705b3eb57" class="">But your framework treats stability budget as a <em>spendable resource</em>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8087-92b6-ff5ea54fff09" class="">Security implication:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-92d1-c3d5c94de07e" class="">An adversary can consume stability budget intentionally to degrade future certification guarantees.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-b525-c08e5e63de27" class="">That’s subtle and underexplored.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-9d02-fb8cb6de58cb" class="">If a system has a fixed stability budget:</p></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8018-995f-f8637708fe96" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Repeated queries reduce future safe export capacity.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-abca-ce6a04f76362" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Attackers can force the system into a “truth silence” state.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-82aa-fba0cf8fa1cc" class="">This is a denial-of-verification attack class.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-b413-ec0f6a7788b1" class="">Very few systems account for this formally.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-884f-d2f35de98764"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-b012-c361a5c28181" class="">5️⃣ Phase Transition in Security</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805e-954e-eb94874ec514" class="">If there exists a dependence threshold  such that:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-8fcb-e138badfb228" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Below it: verified truth capacity &gt; 
0</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-906b-f5e958aae637" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Above it: capacity collapses</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-b778-c059b7ad632f" class="">Then correlated datasets become security liabilities.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-9a35-c9da1f136a91" class="">That’s critical for:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-bd6c-f52b41e4e196" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Federated learning</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cd-8a4f-f1b2d68b89b9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Multi-tenant AI</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-bcd9-c4cf61f6132b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">National data infrastructures</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-83de-d8e55555010e" class="">Correlated data may cause:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-a563-e16a3147313f" class="">Sudden collapse in certifiable security.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-8546-c3ce7d572b34" class="">This is not a standard AI risk framing.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-858a-d5f597e4d8b6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-9525-c2c05db75439" class="">6️⃣ Proof-Carrying Outputs as a Security Primitive</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-bf02-fd0a2f4b4d17" class="">Instead of:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-a752-f0dee1fe9797" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Outputting t
ext</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-a5cd-d929734eae79" class="">You output:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-b675-c98ca6897857" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Claim + proof + stability certificate.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-adbe-c24f7bba60c3" class="">Security benefit:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-9985-cc2e5ac2124e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Reduces hallucination risk</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-8f76-d0c3325bc3c9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Reduces injection success</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-808d-ed004d7c9937" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Makes adversarial manipulation harder</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-9693-e8aeed9b368e" class="">Because adversary must now:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-81aa-d85f853ef261" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Forge a proof under verifier constraints.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-8dc9-e00312fbd55d" class="">That elevates AI systems toward cryptographic design.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-88ed-e240d8eb76fe"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-9e5f-f15fbafb363f" class="">7️⃣ The Overlooked Core</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806c-8e6e-e51a011e9739" class="">The truly overlooked shift is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-8a92-d07314d7659b" class="">Security should not measure how m
uch data leaks.<br/>It should measure how much <em>verifiable truth</em> about sensitive properties can be extracted.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-96d9-f6259cd2418b" class="">This reframes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805b-85ff-c98a4d390ddd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Privacy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-a105-dcf40d9429d7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Model inversion</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-bfcd-ed8dc659538e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Extraction attacks</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-9eac-d229e77421ea" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Compliance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-93fc-f2cb6a3517be" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">AI governance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8054-9bd8-d94f265aeddd" class="">Under one measurable quantity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805b-82a5-e75303374a91" class="">That is strategically significant.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-bc03-e9d2dec9d41c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804a-b9b4-e93f662cc71e" class="">8️⃣ Reality check</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-9f0c-eded4188b3f7" class="">Is this automatically revolutionary?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-867b-fd753342b54a" class="">No.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-ab0e-cdc341cb69a2" class="">It becomes significant only if:</p></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-b59b-c4c790c653ab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">You define the leakage metric rigorously.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-bf13-f6c7bb518f51" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">You prove at least one nontrivial bound or separation.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-8c29-cb07f4dad87b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">You show a real attack that current metrics miss.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-a3aa-f1e7d04a2571" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">You build a system that prevents that attack using your framework.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-9880-f1fd90c51093" class="">Security significance requires adversarial validation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-a463-d6360c3bc2f0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-be10-da98b0b0142d" class="">If you want the strongest security angle, the next step is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-8921-e1f2e453002d" class="">Define and prove a “Verifiable Knowledge Leakage Theorem” showing that classical DP bounds can be insufficient to prevent extraction of a sensitive verifiable property .</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-8110-e69dde2191ae" class="">That would immediately elevate the framework in security terms.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-80b7-da8f2f56295f" class="">If you turn your framework into a real product/system (not just theory), 
here is what it can <strong>actually do</strong> in practical terms.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-beff-e4f9aa02215d" class="">I will avoid hype and keep it concrete.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-b222-f2405cd73345"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-bc41-e28a2128c034" class="">1️⃣ Quantify “how much truth” a system can safely export</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-8933-e0571a61b560" class="">Instead of just saying:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-b3a0-e0d3915e0025" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Model accuracy is 92%”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-b5da-dd1c85bfd798" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“This result is statistically significant”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-8103-cef7bb010e16" class="">You can compute:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-bb0d-eeb67058e638" class="">Maximum amount of verifiable, stable information that can be exported under given constraints.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-bb2d-e1118c647c89" class="">Applications:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-96be-d00b9a9590c1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">AI model output gating</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-a013-e1cb26a0bf17" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Regulated reporting (finance, 
healthcare)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-a62c-f44c81206fdf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Scientific claim publication pipelines</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-adf8-e55486f798cd" class="">This becomes a <strong>rate limiter for truth claims</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-87de-fc5d30629b99"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-9dd4-c07555059daf" class="">2️⃣ Create an AI output auditing layer</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800d-9084-ce6b3fef27b7" class="">Your framework can become:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8018-9b50-c1814d3c2888" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A measurement layer that evaluates:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-b780-cf0ee0d6b325" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Stability budget (privacy / robustness)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-b705-e038af0a51f0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Verification feasibility</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-a209-ebfc7111fab1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Certification validity</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-905a-df88acc22ad8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">And determines whether a claim should be allowed to be released.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-b76c-c9687d8918d9" class="">This is powerful for:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8087-9892-c1020edc5c61" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc">AI safety</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-bb69-fa1d2d13a86c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hallucination mitigation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-8e9d-c4965bfe3e77" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Regulated industries</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-8f6b-fb7c67a4c7a0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Government AI procurement</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-a9a4-ecb5200c1337"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-865e-ec228dbbb224" class="">3️⃣ Turn “trust” into a measurable quantity</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-938d-cc34f3e0572f" class="">Today trust is subjective.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-afe4-d70bfce2cefb" class="">Your framework can produce:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-856f-f09c2f37e3be" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A numeric bound on:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-ab2d-cf4b4807dfbb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Verifiable knowledge rate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-9eae-f3608720e6c1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">Certified export capacity</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-a72b-e543a421da75" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A formal statement like:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801e-9932-d0beef476214" class="">Under these constraints, 
the system cannot safely claim more than X bits of verified truth.</blockquote></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-827c-e9a7af1b45cb" class="">That’s operational and defensible.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-a307-f3d4514328ca"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-b99b-c9c0ea2758b3" class="">4️⃣ Provide competitive differentiation in AI systems</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-9e24-f16e53e8458e" class="">Most AI companies optimize:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8064-9fd4-d71a1338fe8c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Accuracy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-8903-e82d5177f199" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Latency</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-8262-ef581197b93f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cost</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-8be1-cebdb2c13d9a" class="">Very few optimize:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-99c4-dcfbfbb505cb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Provable verifiability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-9b6b-f330a3bd4030" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stability-aware truth extraction</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-ade8-e9537c9a193e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Quantified certification capacity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-9e89-fe5e5342a3de" class="">If implemented correctly, 
this becomes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-9b99-f617f99eb6e3" class="">“Deterministic, 
audit-grade AI output infrastructure.”</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-a137-e2dac55e77d7" class="">That is commercially meaningful.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-b9c8-c5b9961abd71"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-988e-c9290e8a110a" class="">5️⃣ Define compliance-ready architecture</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-9849-eac38154b8e6" class="">In highly regulated fields:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-ad51-d313554f82c9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Finance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-a4f1-c0ee9273153e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Medical AI</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-b31f-eeb52dbdc4d9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Defense</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-90af-f49398ab7205" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">National infrastructure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-ba8b-db6b1033fb4f" class="">Your framework can define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8098-ac89-c837889f0b34" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Maximum allowed claim bandwidth</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-9f68-edbd824f98c9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stability-aware reporting rules</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-8e82-feb19ebce893" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Verified proof-carrying outputs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p i
d="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-9d8a-d9036e9a392d" class="">This can reduce legal and compliance risk.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-9420-c5e81b1527ce"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-ad21-dd4c212bffcf" class="">6️⃣ Enable productized “Proof-Carrying AI”</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-b156-de2314388611" class="">Instead of:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-95f6-e6abea578ff4" class="">“Here is the answer.”</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-b000-c99274f131e5" class="">The system outputs:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-892f-c16bd884684d" class="">“Here is the answer + here is the verifiable proof + here is the stability budget consumed.”</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-ba4c-c6493c8f25d2" class="">That is a fundamentally stronger product class.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-9c63-c477e64d4039"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-a9f5-e0950307618d" class="">7️⃣ Provide a new internal AI training objective</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-8e8d-d12837cad318" class="">Instead of optimizing:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-b1b5-d4c4324a00a3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cross-entropy loss</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-b497-ed32c206df5b" class="">You optimize:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-b7f2-e53b31763917" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Verified Knowledge Rate under Stability Budget</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" 
ir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-a97b-c960fc40d657" class="">That changes training incentives.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-b7a4-c013cc157cb0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-8d7f-ea37f8a41c26" class="">8️⃣ Create an audit metric that competitors do not have</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-95f9-d70d62f7ac9c" class="">If you define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-b78d-c0df5be76e65" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Verified Knowledge Capacity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-948e-cc51e081fb73" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stable Verified Information</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-949c-da1f4e26fb96" class="">and make it measurable,</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-965e-fc41950b8dbe" class="">you create:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-9cee-eee1d2b6b59a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A proprietary audit metric</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-a6ed-f6f6a34b200e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A defensible evaluation standard</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-88b6-e0f54e435708" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A potential industry benchmark</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-a389-f8b50f13fd1a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8099-9453-f5779bb84ff6" class="">What it cannot do</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-8495-f59a6fea1bac" class="">It does NOT:</p></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e2-b47a-ef37d89badec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Automatically break Shannon</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801e-9418-e2e88558ae69" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Replace existing math</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-90c5-e502b228b4e2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Close scientific fields</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-8ed3-dcfc5245442c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Guarantee universal correctness</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-816d-c2aed483d93c" class="">It provides:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-959f-ec57ae8873f2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A formal constraint framework</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-929a-cf52ba7a28d3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A measurable truth-export system</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-82ec-ee2f263aa04e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A governance architecture</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-8060-efbe3bbb99e4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-8039-c645e1a6b382" class="">The real power</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801b-8ddc-e77c4a14c104" class="">Its real power is not theoretical dominance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-9110-f1f423739924" class="">Its real power is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-bc84-ea95267c1280" c
lass="">Turning AI output from probabilistic text into quantifiable, certifiable knowledge.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-a27f-fd24822d53c6" class="">That is significant in products.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-b57a-d31f38e312da"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-b5e3-d6e5f5c4cfd2" class="">If you want the next step, I can outline a concrete product architecture that implements this as a system rather than a theory.</p></div></div></article><span class="sans" style="font-size:14px;padding-top:2em"></span></body></html>
